    Library.ApplyGradient = function(self, Parent, Color1, Color2, Rotation)
        local existing = Parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIGradient")
        if existing then
            existing.Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, Color1), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, Color2)}
            existing.Rotation = Rotation or 0
            return existing
        end
        local gradient = InstanceNew("UIGradient")
        gradient.Name = "\0"
        gradient.Parent = Parent
        gradient.Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, Color1), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, Color2)}
        gradient.Rotation = Rotation or 0
        return gradient
    end

    local Tween = { } do
        Tween.__index = Tween

        Tween.Create = function(self, Item, Info, Goal, IsRawItem)
            Item = IsRawItem and Item or Item.Instance
            Info = Info or CachedTweenInfo

            local NewTween = {
                Tween = TweenService:Create(Item, Info, Goal),
                Info = Info,
                Goal = Goal,
                Item = Item
            }

            NewTween.Tween:Play()

            setmetatable(NewTween, Tween)

            return NewTween
        end

        Tween.Get = function(self)
            if not self.Tween then
                return
            end

            return self.Tween, self.Info, self.Goal
        end

        Tween.Pause = function(self)
            if not self.Tween then
                return
            end

            self.Tween:Pause()
        end

        Tween.Play = function(self)
            if not self.Tween then
                return
            end

            self.Tween:Play()
        end

        Tween.Clean = function(self)
            if not self.Tween then
                return
            end

            Tween:Pause()
            self = nil
        end

        Tween.GetProperty = function(self, Item)
            Item = Item or self.Item

            if Item:IsA("Frame") then
                return "BackgroundTransparency"
            elseif Item:IsA("TextLabel") or Item:IsA("TextButton") or Item:IsA("TextBox") then
                return {"BackgroundTransparency", "TextTransparency"}
            elseif Item:IsA("ImageLabel") or Item:IsA("ImageButton") then
                return {"BackgroundTransparency", "ImageTransparency"}
            elseif Item:IsA("UIStroke") then
                return "Transparency"
            end
        end

        Tween.FadeItem = function(self, Item, Property, Visibility, Speed)
            local Item = Item or self.Item

            local OldTransparency = Item[Property]
            Item[Property] = Visibility and 1 or OldTransparency

            local NewTween = Tween:Create(Item, Speed and TweenInfo.new(Speed, Library.Tween.Style, Library.Tween.Direction) or CachedTweenInfo, {
                [Property] = Visibility and OldTransparency or 1
            }, true)

            return NewTween
        end
    end

    local Instances = { } do
        Instances.__index = Instances

        Instances.Create = function(self, Class, Properties)
            local NewItem = {
                Instance = InstanceNew(Class),
                Properties = Properties,
                Class = Class
            }

            setmetatable(NewItem, Instances)

            for Property, Value in NewItem.Properties do
                NewItem.Instance[Property] = Value
            end

            return NewItem
        end

        Instances.Border = function(self)

        Instances.MakeDraggable = function(self)
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            if not self.Instance then
                return
            end

            local Gui = self.Instance

            local Dragging = false
            local DragStart
            local StartPosition
            local MoveConnection
            local EndConnection

            local Set = function(Input)
                LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
                local DragDelta = Input.Position - DragStart

                local NewX = StartPosition.X.Offset + DragDelta.X
                local NewY = StartPosition.Y.Offset + DragDelta.Y

                local ViewportX = Camera.ViewportSize.X
                local ViewportY = Camera.ViewportSize.Y
                local GuiWidth = Gui.AbsoluteSize.X
                local GuiHeight = Gui.AbsoluteSize.Y

                NewX = MathClamp(NewX, 0, ViewportX - GuiWidth)
                NewY = MathClamp(NewY, 0, ViewportY - GuiHeight)

                Gui.Position = UDim2New(0, NewX, 0, NewY)
            end

            self:Connect("InputBegan", function(Input)
                LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
                if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    Dragging = true

                    Library:BringToFront(Gui)

                    DragStart = Input.Position
                    StartPosition = Gui.Position

                    if StartPosition.X.Scale ~= 0 or StartPosition.Y.Scale ~= 0 then
                        StartPosition = UDim2New(0, Gui.AbsolutePosition.X, 0, Gui.AbsolutePosition.Y)
                        Gui.Position = StartPosition
                    end

                    if MoveConnection then MoveConnection:Disconnect() MoveConnection = nil end
                    if EndConnection then EndConnection:Disconnect() EndConnection = nil end

                    MoveConnection = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(moveInput)
                        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
                        if (moveInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or moveInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) and Dragging then
                            Set(moveInput)
                        end
                    end)

                    EndConnection = Input.Changed:Connect(function()
                        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
                        if Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                            Dragging = false
                            if MoveConnection then MoveConnection:Disconnect() MoveConnection = nil end
                            if EndConnection then EndConnection:Disconnect() EndConnection = nil end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)

            self:Connect("InputEnded", function(Input)
                LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
                if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    Dragging = false
                    if MoveConnection then MoveConnection:Disconnect() MoveConnection = nil end
                    if EndConnection then EndConnection:Disconnect() EndConnection = nil end
                end
            end)

            return Dragging
        end

        Instances.MakeResizeable = function(self, Minimum, Maximum)
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            if not self.Instance then
                return
            end

            local Gui = self.Instance

            local Resizing = false
            local Start = UDim2New()
            local Delta = UDim2New()
            local ResizeMax = Gui.Parent.AbsoluteSize - Gui.AbsoluteSize
            local MoveConnection
            local EndConnection

            local ResizeButton = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                                Parent = Gui,
                                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 1),
                                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                                Size = UDim2New(0, 8, 0, 8),
                                Position = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                Name = "\0",
                                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Visible = true,
                Text = ""
                        })

            ResizeButton:Connect("InputBegan", function(Input)
                LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
                if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    Resizing = true

                    Start = Gui.Size - UDim2New(0, Input.Position.X, 0, Input.Position.Y)

                    if MoveConnection then MoveConnection:Disconnect() MoveConnection = nil end
                    if EndConnection then EndConnection:Disconnect() EndConnection = nil end

                    MoveConnection = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(moveInput)
                        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
                        if moveInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and Resizing then
                            local maxSz = Maximum or Gui.Parent.AbsoluteSize - Gui.AbsoluteSize
                            local d = Start + UDim2New(0, moveInput.Position.X, 0, moveInput.Position.Y)
                            d = UDim2New(0, math.clamp(d.X.Offset, Minimum.X, maxSz.X), 0, math.clamp(d.Y.Offset, Minimum.Y, maxSz.Y))
                            Gui.Size = d
                        end
                    end)

                    EndConnection = Input.Changed:Connect(function()
                        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
                        if Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                            Resizing = false
                            if MoveConnection then MoveConnection:Disconnect() MoveConnection = nil end
                            if EndConnection then EndConnection:Disconnect() EndConnection = nil end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)

            ResizeButton:Connect("InputEnded", function(Input)
                LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
                if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    Resizing = false
                    if MoveConnection then MoveConnection:Disconnect() MoveConnection = nil end
                    if EndConnection then EndConnection:Disconnect() EndConnection = nil end
                end
            end)

            return Resizing
        end

    local CustomFont = { } do
        local function downloadWithRetries(url, maxRetries)
            maxRetries = maxRetries or 6
            for attempt = 1, maxRetries do
                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    return game:HttpGet(url)
                end)
                if ok and type(result) == "string" and #result > 1000 then
                    return result
                end
                task.wait(0.35 * attempt)
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function safeCustomAsset(path)
            local ok, asset = pcall(function()
                return getcustomasset(path)
            end)
            if ok and asset then
                return asset
            end
            return nil
        end

        function CustomFont:New(Name, Weight, Style, Data)
            local jsonPath = Library.Folders.Assets .. "/" .. Name .. ".json"
            local ttfPath = Library.Folders.Assets .. "/" .. Name .. ".ttf"

            -- 1. Check if valid existing JSON and TTF are already cached
            if isfile(jsonPath) and isfile(ttfPath) then
                local isValid = false
                pcall(function()
                    local ttfContent = readfile(ttfPath)
                    local jsonContent = readfile(jsonPath)
                    if #ttfContent > 5000 and #jsonContent > 10 then
                        isValid = true
                    end
                end)
                if isValid then
                    local asset = safeCustomAsset(jsonPath)
                    if asset then
                        local ok, font = pcall(function() return Font.new(asset) end)
                        if ok and font then return font end
                    end
                else
                    -- Corrupted cache: remove bad files and re-download
                    pcall(delfile, jsonPath)
                    pcall(delfile, ttfPath)
                end
            end

            -- 2. Download TTF with retries if missing or corrupted
            local ttfData = nil
            if isfile(ttfPath) then
                pcall(function()
                    local content = readfile(ttfPath)
                    if #content > 5000 then ttfData = content end
                end)
            end

            if not ttfData and Data and Data.Url then
                ttfData = downloadWithRetries(Data.Url, 6)
                if not ttfData and Data.FallbackUrl then
                    ttfData = downloadWithRetries(Data.FallbackUrl, 4)
                end
                if ttfData then
                    pcall(function() writefile(ttfPath, ttfData) end)
                end
            end

            -- 3. Generate JSON and load custom Font Face
            if isfile(ttfPath) then
                local ttfAsset = safeCustomAsset(ttfPath)
                if ttfAsset then
                    local FontData = {
                        name = Name,
                        faces = { {
                            name = "Regular",
                            weight = Weight or 200,
                            style = Style or "Regular",
                            assetId = ttfAsset
                        } }
                    }

                    pcall(function()
                        writefile(jsonPath, HttpService:JSONEncode(FontData))
                    end)

                    if isfile(jsonPath) then
                        local jsonAsset = safeCustomAsset(jsonPath)
                        if jsonAsset then
                            local ok, font = pcall(function() return Font.new(jsonAsset) end)
                            if ok and font then
                                return font
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            -- 4. Clean Fallback font if network/filesystem fails
            return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
        end

        function CustomFont:Get(Name)
            local jsonPath = Library.Folders.Assets .. "/" .. Name .. ".json"
            if isfile(jsonPath) then
                local asset = safeCustomAsset(jsonPath)
                if asset then
                    local ok, font = pcall(function() return Font.new(asset) end)
                    if ok and font then
                        return font
                    end
                end
            end
            return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
        end

        CustomFont:New("Windows-XP-Tahoma", 200, "Regular", {
            Url = "https://github.com/sametexe001/luas/raw/refs/heads/main/fonts/windows-xp-tahoma.ttf",
            FallbackUrl = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sametexe001/luas/main/fonts/windows-xp-tahoma.ttf"
        })

        Library.Font = CustomFont:Get("Windows-XP-Tahoma") or Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
    end

        Position = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 0, 10),
        Name = "\0",
        Size = UDim2New(0.34, 0, 1, -14),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    })

    Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
        Parent = Library.NotifHolder.Instance,
        VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
        Padding = UDimNew(0, 10)
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
        while true do
            if not Library then break end
            local pickers = Library.RainbowColorpickers
            if pickers and #pickers > 0 then
                Library.RainbowHue = (Library.RainbowHue + (Library.RainbowSpeed or 0.001)) % 1
                local HasActive = false
                local Color = FromHSV(Library.RainbowHue, 1, 1)
                for _, Colorpicker in pickers do
                    if Colorpicker.Rainbow and Colorpicker.Set then
                        HasActive = true
                        Colorpicker:Set(Color, Colorpicker.Alpha or 0)
                    end
                end

                if HasActive then
                    task.wait(0.016)
                else
                    task.wait(1)
                end
            else
                task.wait(1)
            end
        end
    end)

    Library.GetImage = function(self, Image)
        local ImageData = self.Images[Image]

        if not ImageData then
            return ""
        end

        local assetPath = self.Folders.Assets .. "/" .. ImageData[1]
        if not isfile(assetPath) then
            pcall(function()
                if not isfolder(self.Folders.Assets) then
                    makefolder(self.Folders.Assets)
                end
                if isfile("opiods/Assets/" .. ImageData[1]) then
                    writefile(assetPath, readfile("opiods/Assets/" .. ImageData[1]))
                else
                    writefile(assetPath, game:HttpGet(ImageData[2]))
                end
            end)
        end

        if isfile(assetPath) then
            local ok, asset = pcall(getcustomasset, assetPath)
            if ok and asset then
                return asset
            end
        end

        if isfile("opiods/Assets/" .. ImageData[1]) then
            local ok, asset = pcall(getcustomasset, "opiods/Assets/" .. ImageData[1])
            if ok and asset then
                return asset
            end
        end

        return ImageData[2] or ""
    end

    Library.Round = function(self, Number, Float)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
        local Multiplier = 1 / (Float or 1)
        return MathFloor(Number * Multiplier) / Multiplier
    end

    Library.ToRich = function(self, Text, Color)
        return `<font color="rgb({MathFloor(Color.R * 255)}, {MathFloor(Color.G * 255)}, {MathFloor(Color.B * 255)})">{Text}</font>`
    end

        local function closeDialog()
            pcall(function()
                snowRunning = false
                local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                TweenService:Create(overlay, tweenInfo, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(Items["Dialog"].Instance, tweenInfo, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                for _, desc in Items["Dialog"].Instance:GetDescendants() do
                    if desc:IsA("UIStroke") then
                        TweenService:Create(desc, tweenInfo, {Transparency = 1}):Play()
                    elseif desc:IsA("TextLabel") then
                        TweenService:Create(desc, tweenInfo, {TextTransparency = 1}):Play()
                    elseif desc:IsA("Frame") or desc:IsA("TextButton") then
                        TweenService:Create(desc, tweenInfo, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                    end
                end
                for _, p in snowParticles do
                    pcall(function()
                        TweenService:Create(p, tweenInfo, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                    end)
                end
                task.wait(0.3)
                dialogGui:Destroy()
            end)
        end

        Items["YesButton"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {

        Items["YesButton"]:OnHover(function()
            Items["YesButton"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(50, 80, 50)})
        end)
        Items["YesButton"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
            Items["YesButton"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(40, 65, 40)})
        end)
        Items["NoButton"]:OnHover(function()
            Items["NoButton"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(80, 45, 45)})
        end)
        Items["NoButton"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
            Items["NoButton"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(65, 35, 35)})
        end)

        local confirmed = false
        local function handleYes()
            if confirmed then return end
            confirmed = true
            closeDialog()
            if OnConfirm then
                Library:SafeCall(OnConfirm)
            end
        end

        local cancelled = false
        local function handleNo()
            if cancelled then return end
            cancelled = true
            closeDialog()
            if OnCancel then
                Library:SafeCall(OnCancel)
            end
        end

        Items["YesButton"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", handleYes)
        Items["YesButton"]:Connect("MouseButton1Click", handleYes)
        Items["NoButton"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", handleNo)
        Items["NoButton"]:Connect("MouseButton1Click", handleNo)

        for _, desc in Items["Dialog"].Instance:GetDescendants() do
            if desc:IsA("UIStroke") then
                desc.Transparency = 1
            elseif desc:IsA("TextLabel") then
                desc.TextTransparency = 1
            elseif desc:IsA("Frame") or desc:IsA("TextButton") then
                desc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            end
        end

        local function spawnSnowflake()
            local size = math.random(2, 5)
            local startX = math.random(0, 100) / 100
            local drift = (math.random(-15, 15)) / 100
            local duration = math.random(20, 40) / 10
            local alpha = math.random(3, 7) / 10

            local flake = InstanceNew("Frame")
            flake.Name = "\0"
            flake.Size = UDim2New(0, size, 0, size)
            flake.Position = UDim2New(startX, 0, -0.02, 0)
            flake.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(
                math.random(160, 200),
                math.random(200, 225),
                math.random(235, 255)
            )
            flake.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - alpha
            flake.BorderSizePixel = 0
            flake.ZIndex = 50001
            flake.Parent = overlay

            local corner = InstanceNew("UICorner")
            corner.CornerRadius = UDimNew(1, 0)
            corner.Parent = flake

            table.insert(snowParticles, flake)

            local endX = startX + drift
            TweenService:Create(flake, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                Position = UDim2New(endX, 0, 1.05, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.85
            }):Play()

            task.delay(duration, function()
                pcall(function() flake:Destroy() end)
            end)
        end

        Library:Thread(function()
            local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            TweenService:Create(overlay, tweenInfo, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.4}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(Items["Dialog"].Instance, tweenInfo, {BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play()
            for _, desc in Items["Dialog"].Instance:GetDescendants() do
                if desc == Items["ButtonContainer"].Instance then continue end
                if desc:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    TweenService:Create(desc, tweenInfo, {Transparency = 0}):Play()
                elseif desc:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    TweenService:Create(desc, tweenInfo, {TextTransparency = 0}):Play()
                elseif desc:IsA("Frame") or desc:IsA("TextButton") then
                    TweenService:Create(desc, tweenInfo, {BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play()
                end
            end

            for i = 1, 8 do
                spawnSnowflake()
            end

            while snowRunning do
                task.wait(math.random(8, 20) / 100)
                if not snowRunning then break end
                spawnSnowflake()
            end
        end)
    end

    Library.Unload = function(self)
        for Index, Value in self.Connections do
            Value.Connection:Disconnect()
        end

        for Index, Value in self.Threads do
            coroutine.close(Value)
        end

        if self.Holder then
            self.Holder:Clean()
        end

        Library = nil
        getgenv().Library = nil
    end

    Library.Thread = function(self, Function)
        local NewThread = coroutine.create(Function)

        coroutine.wrap(function()
            coroutine.resume(NewThread)
        end)()

        TableInsert(self.Threads, NewThread)

        return NewThread
    end

    Library.SafeCall = function(self, Function, ...)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
        local fn = type(self) == "function" and self or Function
        local Arguements = type(self) == "function" and { Function, ... } or { ... }
        if type(fn) ~= "function" then
            return false
        end

        local Success, Result = pcall(fn, TableUnpack(Arguements))

        if not Success then
            pcall(function()
                Library:Notification("Error caught in function:\n" .. tostring(Result), 5, FromRGB(255, 0, 0))
            end)
            warn(Result)
            return false
        end

        return Success, Result
    end

    Library.Connect = function(self, Event, Callback, Name)
        Name = Name or StringFormat("Connection_%s_%s", self.UnnamedConnections + 1, HttpService:GenerateGUID(false))

        local Connection = Event:Connect(Callback)
        local ConnectionTable = {
            Name = Name,
            Connection = Connection
        }

        TableInsert(self.Connections, ConnectionTable)

        return Connection
    end

    Library.Disconnect = function(self, Name)
        for Index, Value in self.Connections do
            if Value.Name == Name then
                Value.Connection:Disconnect()
                TableRemove(self.Connections, Index)
                break
            end
        end
    end

    Library.GetTransparencyPropertyFromItem = function(self, Item)
        local TransparencyProps = {
            ["TextLabel"] = "TextTransparency",
            ["TextButton"] = "TextTransparency",
            ["TextBox"] = "TextTransparency",
            ["ImageLabel"] = "ImageTransparency",
            ["ImageButton"] = "ImageTransparency",
            ["Frame"] = "BackgroundTransparency",
            ["ScrollingFrame"] = {"BackgroundTransparency", "ScrollBarImageTransparency"},
            ["UIStroke"] = "Transparency",
            ["CanvasGroup"] = "GroupTransparency"
        }

        for Class, Prop in TransparencyProps do
            if Item:IsA(Class) then
                return Prop
            end
        end

        return
    end

    Library.NextFlag = function(self)
        local FlagNumber = self.UnnamedFlags + 1
        return StringFormat("Flag Number %s %s", FlagNumber, HttpService:GenerateGUID(false))
    end

    Library.AddToTheme = function(self, Item, Properties)
        if type(Item) == "table" and Item.Instance then
            Item = Item.Instance
        end

        local ThemeData = {
            Item = Item,
            Properties = Properties,
        }

        for Property, Value in ThemeData.Properties do
            if type(Value) == "string" then
                Item[Property] = self.Theme[Value]
            end
        end

        TableInsert(self.ThemeItems, ThemeData)
        self.ThemeMap[Item] = ThemeData
    end

    Library.GetConfig = function(self)
        local Config = { }

        local Success, Result = Library:SafeCall(function()
            for Index, Value in Library.Flags do
                if type(Value) == "table" and (Value.Key ~= nil or Value.Mode ~= nil) then
                    Config[Index] = {Key = Value.Key and tostring(Value.Key) or nil, Mode = Value.Mode or "Toggle"}
                elseif type(Value) == "table" and Value.Color then
                    local ColorVal = Value.Color
                    local Hex
                    if type(ColorVal) == "string" then
                        Hex = ColorVal
                    elseif typeof(ColorVal) == "Color3" then
                        Hex = ColorVal:ToHex()
                    end
                    Config[Index] = {Color = "#" .. (Hex or "ffffff"), Alpha = Value.Alpha}
                else
                    Config[Index] = Value
                end
            end
            Config["Settings_NotifPosition"] = nil
        end)

        return HttpService:JSONEncode(Config)
    end

    Library.LoadConfig = function(self, Config)
        local SuccessDec, Decoded = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(Config)
        end)
        if not SuccessDec or type(Decoded) ~= "table" then
            Library:Notification("Invalid config file format", 5, FromRGB(255, 0, 0))
            return
        end

        local Unwrapped = { }
        for Index, Entry in Decoded do
            if type(Entry) == "table" and Entry.v ~= nil then
                Unwrapped[Index] = Entry.v
            else
                Unwrapped[Index] = Entry
            end
        end
        Unwrapped["Settings_NotifPosition"] = nil

        local Success, Result = Library:SafeCall(function()
            for Flag, SetFunction in Library.SetFlags do
                local Value = Unwrapped[Flag]

                if Value == nil then
                    local DefaultVal = Library.Defaults and Library.Defaults[Flag]
                    if DefaultVal ~= nil then
                        pcall(function()
                            if type(DefaultVal) == "table" and DefaultVal.Color ~= nil then
                                SetFunction(DefaultVal.Color, DefaultVal.Alpha)
                            else
                                SetFunction(DefaultVal)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                else
                    pcall(function()
                        if type(Value) == "table" and (Value.Key ~= nil or Value.Mode ~= nil) then
                            SetFunction(Value)
                        elseif type(Value) == "table" and Value.Color ~= nil then
                            SetFunction(Value.Color, Value.Alpha)
                        else
                            SetFunction(Value)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

        if Success then
            Library:Notification("Successfully loaded config", 5, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0))
        end
    end

    Library.DeleteConfig = function(self, Config)
        local baseName = tostring(Config):gsub("%.json$", "")
        local p1 = Library.Folders.Configs .. "/" .. baseName .. ".json"
        local p2 = Library.Folders.Configs .. "/" .. tostring(Config)
        if isfile(p1) then
            pcall(function() delfile(p1) end)
            Library:Notification("Deleted config " .. baseName, 5, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0))
        elseif isfile(p2) then
            pcall(function() delfile(p2) end)
            Library:Notification("Deleted config " .. baseName, 5, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0))
        end
    end

    Library.SaveConfig = function(self, Config)
        local baseName = tostring(Config):gsub("%.json$", "")
        local p1 = Library.Folders.Configs .. "/" .. baseName .. ".json"
        pcall(function()
            writefile(p1, Library:GetConfig())
        end)
        Library:Notification("Saved config " .. baseName, 5, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0))
    end

    Library.RefreshConfigsList = function(self, Element)
        local List = { }

        for Index, Value in listfiles(Library.Folders.Configs) do
            local FileName = StringGSub(Value, Library.Folders.Configs .. "\\", "")
            FileName = StringGSub(FileName, Library.Folders.Configs .. "/", "")
            FileName = StringGSub(FileName, ".json", "")
            List[Index] = FileName
        end

        Element:Refresh(List)
    end

    Library.ChangeItemTheme = function(self, Item, Properties)
        Item = Item.Instance or Item

        if not self.ThemeMap[Item] then
            return
        end

        self.ThemeMap[Item].Properties = Properties
        self.ThemeMap[Item] = self.ThemeMap[Item]
    end

    Library.ChangeTheme = function(self, Theme, Color)
        self.Theme[Theme] = Color

        for _, Item in self.ThemeItems do
            for Property, Value in Item.Properties do
                if type(Value) == "string" and Value == Theme then
                    Item.Item[Property] = Color
                end
            end
        end
    end

    Library.SavePath = function(self, Name, Path)
        if not Name or not Path or Path == "" then
            return
        end
        Library.Folders[Name] = Path
        if not isfolder(Path) then
            pcall(makefolder, Path)
        end
        if Name == "Assets" then
            for _, ImageData in pairs(Library.Images) do
                local ImageName = ImageData[1]
                local ImageLink = ImageData[2]
                local assetPath = Path .. "/" .. ImageName
                if not isfile(assetPath) then
                    pcall(function()
                        if isfile("opiods/Assets/" .. ImageName) then
                            writefile(assetPath, readfile("opiods/Assets/" .. ImageName))
                        else
                            writefile(assetPath, game:HttpGet(ImageLink))
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    Library.AnimatedGradients = { }

    Library.ApplyAnimatedGradient = function(self, StrokeInstance)
        if not StrokeInstance then
            return
        end

        local StrokeGradient = InstanceNew("UIGradient")
        StrokeGradient.Name = "\0"
        StrokeGradient.Rotation = 0
        StrokeGradient.Color = RGBSequence{
            RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, Library.Theme.Accent),
            RGBSequenceKeypoint(0.5, Library.Theme.Accent),
            RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, Library.Theme.Accent)
        }
        StrokeGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.1, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.9, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        })
        StrokeGradient.Parent = StrokeInstance

        local GradientData = {
            Gradient = StrokeGradient,
            Angle = 0
        }
        TableInsert(Library.AnimatedGradients, GradientData)

        return StrokeGradient
    end

    Library.ApplyClickGradient = function(self, ButtonInstance)
        if not ButtonInstance then return end

        for _, Child in ButtonInstance:GetChildren() do
            if Child.Name == "ClickGradient" and Child:IsA("UIStroke") then
                Child:Destroy()
            end
        end

        local Stroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
        Stroke.Name = "ClickGradient"
        Stroke.Thickness = 3
        Stroke.Transparency = 0
        Stroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
        Stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        Stroke.Color = Library.Theme.Accent
        Stroke.Parent = ButtonInstance

        local StrokeGradient = InstanceNew("UIGradient")
        StrokeGradient.Name = "\0"
        StrokeGradient.Rotation = 0
        StrokeGradient.Color = RGBSequence{
            RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, Library.Theme.Accent),
            RGBSequenceKeypoint(0.5, Library.Theme.Accent),
            RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, Library.Theme.Accent)
        }
        StrokeGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        })
        StrokeGradient.Parent = Stroke

        local GradientData = {
            Gradient = StrokeGradient,
            Angle = 0
        }
        TableInsert(Library.AnimatedGradients, GradientData)

        task.delay(1, function()
            local FadeTween = TweenService:Create(Stroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Transparency = 1})
            FadeTween:Play()
            FadeTween.Completed:Wait()
            local Index = TableFind(Library.AnimatedGradients, GradientData)
            if Index then
                TableRemove(Library.AnimatedGradients, Index)
            end
            Stroke:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
        while true do
            if not Library then break end
            local gradList = Library.AnimatedGradients
            if gradList and #gradList > 0 and Library.Holder and Library.Holder.Instance and Library.Holder.Instance.Enabled then
                local Dt = 0.033
                local FullRotationTime = (Library.Tween and Library.Tween.Time or 0.3) * 10
                local Increment = (Dt / FullRotationTime) * 360
                for i = #gradList, 1, -1 do
                    local GradData = gradList[i]
                    if GradData and GradData.Gradient and GradData.Gradient.Parent then
                        GradData.Angle = ((GradData.Angle or 0) + Increment) % 360
                        GradData.Gradient.Rotation = GradData.Angle
                    elseif not GradData or not GradData.Gradient or not GradData.Gradient.Parent then
                        TableRemove(gradList, i)
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.033)
            else
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end
    end)

    local OldChangeThemeForGradients = Library.ChangeTheme
    Library.ChangeTheme = function(self, Theme, Color)
        OldChangeThemeForGradients(self, Theme, Color)
        if Theme == "Accent" then
            for _, GradData in Library.AnimatedGradients do
                GradData.Gradient.Color = RGBSequence{
                    RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, Color),
                    RGBSequenceKeypoint(0.5, Color),
                    RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, Color)
                }
            end
        end
    end

    local ZCounter = 10

    Library.BringToFront = function(self, Frame)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
        if not Frame then
            return
        end
        ZCounter = ZCounter + 10
        local Delta = ZCounter - Frame.ZIndex
        Frame.ZIndex = ZCounter
        for _, Descendant in Frame:GetDescendants() do
            if Descendant:IsA("GuiObject") then
                Descendant.ZIndex = Descendant.ZIndex + Delta
            end
        end
    end

    Library.IsMouseOverFrame = function(self, Frame)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
        Frame = Frame.Instance

        local MousePosition = Vector2New(Mouse.X, Mouse.Y)

        return MousePosition.X >= Frame.AbsolutePosition.X and MousePosition.X <= Frame.AbsolutePosition.X + Frame.AbsoluteSize.X
        and MousePosition.Y >= Frame.AbsolutePosition.Y and MousePosition.Y <= Frame.AbsolutePosition.Y + Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
    end

    Library.Watermark = function(self, Name)
        local Watermark = { }

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Watermark"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Library.Holder.Instance,
                Size = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 20),
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 15, 0, 15),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20)
            })  Items["Watermark"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Items["Watermark"]:MakeDraggable()

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Watermark"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["Watermark"].Instance,
                PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 2),
                PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 5),
                PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 5)
            })

            Items["Title"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Watermark"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = Name,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 15),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 1),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Title"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Title"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["AccentLine"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Watermark"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, -5, 0, -2),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 10, 0, 2),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(235, 157, 255)
            })  Items["AccentLine"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Accent"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["AccentLine"].Instance,
                Rotation = 0,
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
                })
            })
        end

        function Watermark:SetVisibility(Bool)
            if Items["Watermark"] and Items["Watermark"].Instance then
                Items["Watermark"].Instance.Visible = Bool
            end
        end

        Watermark.Format = Name
        Watermark.Formatter = nil

        function Watermark:Update(Text)
            if Text then
                Watermark.Format = Text
            end
            local DisplayText = Watermark.Format
            if Watermark.Formatter and type(Watermark.Formatter) == "function" then
                local Success, Result = pcall(Watermark.Formatter)
                if Success and type(Result) == "string" then
                    DisplayText = Result
                end
            end
            if Items["Title"] and Items["Title"].Instance then
                Items["Title"].Instance.Text = DisplayText
            end
        end

        function Watermark:SetFormatter(Func)
            if type(Func) == "function" then
                Watermark.Formatter = Func
            else
                Watermark.Formatter = nil
            end
        end

        Library.WatermarkInstance = Watermark
        Library.WatermarkFrame = Items["Watermark"].Instance

        Library.UpdateWatermark = function(self, Text)
            if Library.WatermarkInstance and Library.WatermarkInstance.Update then
                Library.WatermarkInstance:Update(Text)
            end
        end

        Library.SetWatermarkFormatter = function(self, Func)
            if Library.WatermarkInstance and Library.WatermarkInstance.SetFormatter then
                Library.WatermarkInstance:SetFormatter(Func)
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                if Library.WatermarkInstance and Library.WatermarkInstance.Formatter then
                    Library.WatermarkInstance:Update()
                end
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)

        return Watermark
    end

    Library.Notification = function(self, Text, Duration, Color, Icon, AvatarUserId)
        Text = tostring(Text or "")
        Duration = tonumber(Duration) or 3
        Color = typeof(Color) == "Color3" and Color or (Library.Theme and Library.Theme.Accent or FromRGB(235, 157, 255))
        
        local isAvatar = false
        local avatarId = AvatarUserId
        if not avatarId and typeof(Icon) == "table" and (Icon.Avatar or Icon.UserId) then
            avatarId = Icon.Avatar or Icon.UserId
        elseif not avatarId and typeof(Icon) == "number" then
            avatarId = Icon
        end
        if avatarId then
            isAvatar = true
        end

        local lines = string.split(Text, "\n")
        local lineCount = #lines
        local extraH = (lineCount > 1) and ((lineCount - 1) * 15) or 0
        local notifHeight = (isAvatar and 34 or 22) + extraH

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Notification"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Library.NotifHolder.Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, notifHeight),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20)
            })  Items["Notification"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Notification"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["Notification"].Instance,
                PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, isAvatar and 2 or 1),
                PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, isAvatar and 10 or 8),
                PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, isAvatar and 6 or 5)
            })

            Items["Title"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Notification"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = Text,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = isAvatar and UDim2New(1, -32, 0, 16 + extraH) or UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 15 + extraH),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = (isAvatar and lineCount <= 1) and UDim2New(0, 32, 0.5, 0) or (isAvatar and UDim2New(0, 32, 0, 4) or UDim2New(0, 13, 0, 2)),
                AnchorPoint = (isAvatar and lineCount <= 1) and Vector2New(0, 0.5) or Vector2New(0, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
                TextSize = isAvatar and 13 or 12,
                TextYAlignment = (lineCount > 1) and Enum.TextYAlignment.Top or Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Title"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Title"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["AccentLine"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Notification"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 1),
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 2),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color,
                ZIndex = 2
            })

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["AccentLine"].Instance,
                Rotation = 0,
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
                })
            })

            if isAvatar then
                Items["Avatar"] = Instances:Create("ImageLabel", {
                    Parent = Items["Notification"].Instance,
                    ImageColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                    ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Name = "\0",
                    Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. tostring(avatarId) .. "&w=150&h=150",
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0.5),
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 24, 0, 24),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(25, 25, 30)
                })

                Instances:Create("UICorner", {
                    Parent = Items["Avatar"].Instance,
                    CornerRadius = UDimNew(0, 4)
                })

                Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = Items["Avatar"].Instance,
                    Color = FromRGB(45, 45, 55),
                    Thickness = 1
                })
            else
                Items["Icon"] = Instances:Create("ImageLabel", {
                    Parent = Items["Notification"].Instance,
                    ImageColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                    ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Name = "\0",
                    Image = "rbxassetid://94324346713012",
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2New(0, -2, 0, 3),
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 13, 0, 13),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                })

                if not Icon then
                    Items["Icon"]:Clean()
                    Items["Title"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 2)
                else
                    Items["Icon"].Instance.Image = typeof(Icon) == "table" and Icon[1] or tostring(Icon)
                    Items["Icon"].Instance.ImageColor3 = (typeof(Icon) == "table" and Icon[2]) or FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                end
            end
        end

        Items["Notification"].Instance.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        Items["Notification"].Instance.Size = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0)
        for Index, Value in Items["Notification"].Instance:GetDescendants() do
            if Value:IsA("UIStroke") then
                Value.Transparency = 1
            elseif Value:IsA("TextLabel") then
                Value.TextTransparency = 1
            elseif Value:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                Value.ImageTransparency = 1
            elseif Value:IsA("Frame") then
                Value.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            end
        end

        Library:Thread(function()
            Items["Notification"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundTransparency = 0, Size = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, notifHeight)})

            task.wait(0.06)

            for Index, Value in Items["Notification"].Instance:GetDescendants() do
                if Value:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    Tween:Create(Value, nil, {Transparency = 0}, true)
                elseif Value:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    Tween:Create(Value, nil, {TextTransparency = 0}, true)
                elseif Value:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                    Tween:Create(Value, nil, {ImageTransparency = 0}, true)
                elseif Value:IsA("Frame") then
                    Tween:Create(Value, nil, {BackgroundTransparency = 0}, true)
                end
            end

            TweenService:Create(Items["AccentLine"].Instance, TweenInfo.new(Duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
                Size = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 2)
            }):Play()

            task.delay(Duration + 0.1, function()
                for Index, Value in Items["Notification"].Instance:GetDescendants() do
                    if Value:IsA("UIStroke") then
                        Tween:Create(Value, nil, {Transparency = 1}, true)
                    elseif Value:IsA("TextLabel") then
                        Tween:Create(Value, nil, {TextTransparency = 1}, true)
                    elseif Value:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                        Tween:Create(Value, nil, {ImageTransparency = 1}, true)
                    elseif Value:IsA("Frame") then
                        Tween:Create(Value, nil, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, true)
                    end
                end

                task.wait(0.06)

                Items["Notification"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0)})

                task.wait(0.5)
                Items["Notification"]:Clean()
            end)
        end)
    end
    Library.Notify = Library.Notification
