                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Title"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Title"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["Content"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 20),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["Content"].Instance,
                Padding = UDimNew(0, 4),
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            })
        end

        function KeybindList:Add(Mode, Name, Key)
            local KeyStr = Key and tostring(Key) or ""
            local IsBound = KeyStr ~= "" and KeyStr ~= "None" and KeyStr ~= "nil" and KeyStr ~= "Unknown" and KeyStr ~= "Empty"

            local NewKey = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Content"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = "(" .. tostring(Mode or "Toggle") .. ") " .. tostring(Name or "Keybind") .. " - " .. (IsBound and KeyStr or "None"),
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 15),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                TextSize = 12,
                Visible = false,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  NewKey:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = NewKey.Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            function NewKey:Set(Mode, Name, Key)
                local S = Key and tostring(Key) or ""
                local Bound = S ~= "" and S ~= "None" and S ~= "nil" and S ~= "Unknown" and S ~= "Empty"
                if Bound then
                    NewKey.Instance.Text = "(" .. tostring(Mode or "Toggle") .. ") " .. tostring(Name or "Keybind") .. " - " .. S
                else
                    NewKey.Instance.Visible = false
                end
            end

            function NewKey:SetStatus(Status)
                if Status == "Active" then
                    NewKey:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent})
                    NewKey:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Accent"})
                else
                    NewKey:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text})
                    NewKey:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})
                end
            end

            return NewKey
        end

        function KeybindList:SetVisibility(Bool)
            if Items["KeybindList"] and Items["KeybindList"].Instance then
                Items["KeybindList"].Instance.Visible = Bool
            end
        end

        Library.KeyListFrame = Items["KeybindList"].Instance

        return KeybindList
    end

    Library.RiskyPopup = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local Popup = {
            Window = Data.Window,
            Parent = Data.Parent,
            Text = Data.Text or "Are you sure?",
            ConfirmText = Data.ConfirmText or "Confirm",
            CancelText = Data.CancelText or "Cancel",
            WaitTime = Data.WaitTime or 1.2,
            OnConfirm = Data.OnConfirm or function() end,
            OnCancel = Data.OnCancel or function() end
        }

        local CanConfirm = false
        local Closed = false
        local CountdownThread = nil

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Backdrop"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Library.Holder.Instance,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Text = "",
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                ZIndex = 1000,
                Active = true,
                Name = "\0"
            })
            Library:BringToFront(Items["Backdrop"].Instance)

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Backdrop"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{
                    RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
                    RGBSequenceKeypoint(0.5, FromRGB(10, 10, 15)),
                    RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(0, 0, 0))
                },
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.55),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.3)
                })
            })

            Items["Popup"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Backdrop"].Instance,
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0.5, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(0, 300, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(38, 38, 46),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 1001,
                Name = "\0"
            })

            Items["PopupScale"] = Instances:Create("UIScale", {
                Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
                Scale = 0.85,
                Name = "\0"
            })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(180, 160, 255),
                Thickness = 1
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Popup"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{
                    RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(48, 48, 56)),
                    RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(32, 32, 40))
                }
            })

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
                PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 14),
                PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 14),
                PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 14),
                PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 14)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
                Padding = UDimNew(0, 10)
            })

            Items["Divider"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(50, 50, 58),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 1),
                ZIndex = 1002,
                Name = "\0"
            })

            Items["Title"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                Text = "RISKY FEATURE",
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(220, 90, 90),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 16),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 13,
                ZIndex = 1002,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Items["Body"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                Text = Popup.Text,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(195, 195, 200),
                TextWrapped = true,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                ZIndex = 1002,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Items["ButtonHolder"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 24),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 1002,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["ButtonHolder"].Instance,
                FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
                Padding = UDimNew(0, 8),
                HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
            })

            Items["Confirm"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Items["ButtonHolder"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                Text = Popup.ConfirmText .. " (2.0s)",
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(140, 140, 150),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2New(0.5, -4, 0, 24),
                TextSize = 12,
                ZIndex = 1002,
                Active = false,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(33, 33, 36),
                Name = "\0"
            })
            Items["Confirm"]:AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Confirm"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{ RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100)) }
            })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Confirm"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Outline" })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Confirm"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Color = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Name = "\0"
            })

            Items["Cancel"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Items["ButtonHolder"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                Text = Popup.CancelText,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2New(0.5, -4, 0, 24),
                TextSize = 12,
                ZIndex = 1002,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(33, 33, 36),
                Name = "\0"
            })
            Items["Cancel"]:AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border", TextColor3 = "Text" })

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Cancel"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{ RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100)) }
            })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Cancel"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Outline" })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Cancel"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Color = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Name = "\0"
            })
        end

        local SafeTween = function(Instance, Info, Goal)
            if Closed or not Instance or not Instance.Parent then
                return
            end
            local Tween = TweenService:Create(Instance, Info, Goal)
            Tween:Play()
            return Tween
        end

        local OpenTime = Library.Tween.Time
        Items["Popup"].Instance.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        task.defer(function()
            TweenService:Create(Items["Backdrop"].Instance, CachedQuartTweenInfo, {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(Items["Popup"].Instance, CachedQuartTweenInfo, {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0
            }):Play()
        end)

        CountdownThread = Library:Thread(function()
            local StartTime = os.clock()
            local Duration = Popup.WaitTime

            while not Closed do
                local Elapsed = os.clock() - StartTime
                local Remaining = Duration - Elapsed

                if Remaining <= 0 then
                    CanConfirm = true
                    Items["Confirm"].Instance.Text = Popup.ConfirmText
                    Items["Confirm"].Instance.TextColor3 = FromRGB(220, 220, 225)
                    Items["Confirm"].Instance.Active = true
                    break
                end

                Items["Confirm"].Instance.Text = StringFormat("%s (%.1fs)", Popup.ConfirmText, Remaining)
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end)

        local HoverTweenInfo = CachedHoverTweenInfo

        Items["Confirm"]:OnHover(function()
            if CanConfirm and not Closed then
                TweenService:Create(Items["Confirm"].Instance, HoverTweenInfo, {
                    BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]
                }):Play()
                Items["Confirm"]:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })
            end
        end)

        Items["Confirm"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
            if CanConfirm and not Closed then
                TweenService:Create(Items["Confirm"].Instance, HoverTweenInfo, {
                    BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Element"]
                }):Play()
                Items["Confirm"]:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })
            end
        end)

        Items["Cancel"]:OnHover(function()
            if not Closed then
                TweenService:Create(Items["Cancel"].Instance, HoverTweenInfo, {
                    BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]
                }):Play()
                Items["Cancel"]:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })
            end
        end)

        Items["Cancel"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
            if not Closed then
                TweenService:Create(Items["Cancel"].Instance, HoverTweenInfo, {
                    BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Element"]
                }):Play()
                Items["Cancel"]:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })
            end
        end)

        local Close = function()
            if Closed then
                return
            end
            Closed = true
            CanConfirm = false

            local CloseTime = Library.Tween.Time
            if Items["Backdrop"] and Items["Backdrop"].Instance and Items["Backdrop"].Instance.Parent then
                TweenService:Create(Items["Backdrop"].Instance, CachedQuartTweenInfo, {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1
                }):Play()
            end
            if Items["Popup"] and Items["Popup"].Instance then
                TweenService:Create(Items["Popup"].Instance, CachedQuartTweenInfo, {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1
                }):Play()
                for _, child in Items["Popup"].Instance:GetDescendants() do
                    local props = Library:GetTransparencyPropertyFromItem(child)
                    if props then
                        local propList = type(props) == "table" and props or { props }
                        for _, prop in propList do
                            TweenService:Create(child, CachedQuartTweenInfo, {
                                [prop] = 1
                            }):Play()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            task.delay(CloseTime + 0.05, function()
                if Items["Backdrop"] and Items["Backdrop"].Instance and Items["Backdrop"].Instance.Parent then
                    Items["Backdrop"]:Clean()
                end
            end)
        end

        Items["Confirm"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            if not CanConfirm or Closed then
                return
            end

            CanConfirm = false

            TweenService:Create(Items["Confirm"].Instance, CachedHoverTweenInfo, {
                BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Accent"]
            }):Play()
            Items["Confirm"]:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Accent" })

            task.wait(0.1)

            Library:SafeCall(Popup.OnConfirm)
            Close()
        end)

        Items["Cancel"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            Close()
            Library:SafeCall(Popup.OnCancel)
        end)

        Items["Backdrop"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            Close()
            Library:SafeCall(Popup.OnCancel)
        end)

        Popup.Items = Items

        function Popup:Close()
            Close()
        end

        return Popup
    end

    Library.Popup = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or {}
        local Popup = {
            Title = Data.Title or "POPUP",
            Subtitle = Data.Subtitle,
            Text = Data.Text or Data.Content or "",
            ConfirmText = Data.ConfirmText or Data.ButtonText or "Dismiss",
            OnConfirm = Data.OnConfirm or Data.OnClose or function() end,
        }

        local Closed = false
        local Items = {}

        local dialogGui = InstanceNew("ScreenGui")
        dialogGui.Name = "\0"
        dialogGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        dialogGui.DisplayOrder = 99999
        dialogGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        dialogGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
        dialogGui.Parent = gethui()

        Items["Backdrop"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
            Parent = dialogGui,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Text = "",
            BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
            ZIndex = 1000,
            Active = true,
            Name = "\0"
        })

        Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
            Parent = Items["Backdrop"].Instance,
            Rotation = 90,
            Color = RGBSequence{
                RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
                RGBSequenceKeypoint(0.5, FromRGB(10, 10, 15)),
                RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(0, 0, 0))
            },
            Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.55),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.3)
            })
        })

        local popupWidth = Data.Width or 360
        Items["Popup"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
            Parent = Items["Backdrop"].Instance,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2New(0, popupWidth, 0, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20),
            BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
            BorderSizePixel = 2,
            ZIndex = 1001,
            Name = "\0"
        }):AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border" })

        Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
            Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
            LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
            Name = "\0",
            Color = FromRGB(185, 208, 250),
            Thickness = 1.5
        }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Accent" })

        Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
            Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
            PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 0),
            PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 7),
            PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 7),
            PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 7)
        })

        Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
            Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            Padding = UDimNew(0, 0)
        })

        Items["Header"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
            Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
            Name = "\0",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 19),
            ZIndex = 1002,
            LayoutOrder = 1
        })

        Items["Title"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
            Parent = Items["Header"].Instance,
            FontFace = Library.Font,
            Text = Popup.Title or "UPDATE LOG",
            TextColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2New(0.5, -6, 0, 15),
            Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 2),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            TextSize = 12,
            ZIndex = 1003,
            Name = "\0"
        })

        Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
            Parent = Items["Title"].Instance,
            LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
            Name = "\0"
        }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

        local rawSub = Popup.Subtitle or ""
        Items["Subtitle"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
            Parent = Items["Header"].Instance,
            FontFace = Library.Font,
            Text = rawSub:gsub("[\r\n]+%s*", " "),
            TextColor3 = FromRGB(255, 65, 65),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 0, 15),
            Position = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 0, 2),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            TextSize = 12,
            ZIndex = 1003,
            Name = "\0"
        }):AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "WindowSubtitle" })

        Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
            Parent = Items["Subtitle"].Instance,
            LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
            Name = "\0"
        }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

        Items["Inline"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
            Parent = Items["Popup"].Instance,
            Name = "\0",
            Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(20, 20, 25),
            BorderColor3 = FromRGB(27, 27, 32),
            BorderSizePixel = 2,
            ZIndex = 1002,
            LayoutOrder = 2
        }):AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Outline" })

        Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
            Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
            LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
            Color = Library.Theme.Border or FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
            Name = "\0"
        }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Border" })

        Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
            Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
            PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 8),
            PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 8),
            PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 8),
            PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 8)
        })

        Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
            Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            Padding = UDimNew(0, 8)
        })

        local rawText = tostring(Popup.Text)
        local bullets = {}
        if rawText:find("%%NEWBULLET%%") then
            for part in rawText:gmatch("([^%%]+)") do
                local cleaned = part:gsub("^NEWBULLET", ""):gsub("NEWBULLET$", "")
                if #cleaned > 0 then
                    table.insert(bullets, cleaned)
                end
            end
        else
            for line in rawText:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
                table.insert(bullets, line)
            end
        end

        local ContentScroll = InstanceNew("ScrollingFrame")
        ContentScroll.Name = "ContentScroll"
        ContentScroll.Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance
        ContentScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ContentScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ContentScroll.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, MathClamp(#bullets * 20, 60, 360))
        ContentScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 2
        ContentScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent or FromRGB(185, 208, 250)
        ContentScroll.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.2
        ContentScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0)
        ContentScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        ContentScroll.ZIndex = 1003
        ContentScroll.LayoutOrder = 4

        local ScrollLayout = InstanceNew("UIListLayout")
        ScrollLayout.Parent = ContentScroll
        ScrollLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        ScrollLayout.Padding = UDimNew(0, 4)

        local currentLayoutOrder = 1
        local hasSeenAddition = false
        local hasDivided = false

        for _, bulletText in ipairs(bullets) do
            local cleanBullet = bulletText:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
            if #cleanBullet > 0 then
                local isDivider = cleanBullet == "%DIVIDER%" or cleanBullet == "[DIVIDER]" or cleanBullet == "---"
                local isAddition = cleanBullet:sub(1, 3) == "[+]"
                local isFix = cleanBullet:sub(1, 3) == "[~]"

                if isAddition then
                    hasSeenAddition = true
                elseif isFix and hasSeenAddition and not hasDivided then
                    hasDivided = true
                    local DivContainer = InstanceNew("Frame")
                    DivContainer.Name = "GroupDivider"
                    DivContainer.Parent = ContentScroll
                    DivContainer.Size = UDim2New(1, -6, 0, 9)
                    DivContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    DivContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    DivContainer.ZIndex = 1003
                    DivContainer.LayoutOrder = currentLayoutOrder
                    currentLayoutOrder = currentLayoutOrder + 1

                    local DivLine = InstanceNew("Frame")
                    DivLine.Name = "Line"
                    DivLine.Parent = DivContainer
                    DivLine.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 1)
                    DivLine.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
                    DivLine.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(50, 50, 58)
                    DivLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    DivLine.ZIndex = 1004
                end

                if isDivider then
                    local DivContainer = InstanceNew("Frame")
                    DivContainer.Name = "ExplicitDivider"
                    DivContainer.Parent = ContentScroll
                    DivContainer.Size = UDim2New(1, -6, 0, 9)
                    DivContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    DivContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    DivContainer.ZIndex = 1003
                    DivContainer.LayoutOrder = currentLayoutOrder
                    currentLayoutOrder = currentLayoutOrder + 1

                    local DivLine = InstanceNew("Frame")
                    DivLine.Name = "Line"
                    DivLine.Parent = DivContainer
                    DivLine.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 1)
                    DivLine.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
                    DivLine.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(50, 50, 58)
                    DivLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    DivLine.ZIndex = 1004
                else
                    local Row = InstanceNew("Frame")
                    Row.Name = "Bullet_" .. currentLayoutOrder
                    Row.Parent = ContentScroll
                    Row.Size = UDim2New(1, -6, 0, 18)
                    Row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    Row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    Row.ZIndex = 1003
                    Row.LayoutOrder = currentLayoutOrder
                    currentLayoutOrder = currentLayoutOrder + 1

                    local displayText = cleanBullet

                    if isAddition or isFix then
                        displayText = cleanBullet:sub(4):gsub("^%s+", "")

                        local Tag = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
                        Tag.Name = "Tag"
                        Tag.Parent = Row
                        Tag.FontFace = Library.Font
                        Tag.Text = isAddition and "[+]" or "[~]"
                        Tag.TextColor3 = FromRGB(245, 95, 95)
                        Tag.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        Tag.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        Tag.Size = UDim2New(0, 18, 0, 16)
                        Tag.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 1)
                        Tag.TextSize = 12
                        Tag.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                        Tag.ZIndex = 1004

                        local TextLabel = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
                        TextLabel.Name = "Text"
                        TextLabel.Parent = Row
                        TextLabel.FontFace = Library.Font
                        TextLabel.Text = displayText
                        TextLabel.TextColor3 = isAddition and FromRGB(215, 215, 215) or FromRGB(185, 185, 192)
                        TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        TextLabel.Size = UDim2New(1, -22, 1, 0)
                        TextLabel.Position = UDim2New(0, 20, 0, 0)
                        TextLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                        TextLabel.TextWrapped = true
                        TextLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        TextLabel.TextSize = 12
                        TextLabel.ZIndex = 1004
                    else
                        local TextLabel = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
                        TextLabel.Name = "Text"
                        TextLabel.Parent = Row
                        TextLabel.FontFace = Library.Font
                        TextLabel.Text = displayText
                        TextLabel.TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208)
                        TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        TextLabel.Size = UDim2New(1, -4, 1, 0)
                        TextLabel.Position = UDim2New(0, 4, 0, 0)
                        TextLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                        TextLabel.TextWrapped = true
                        TextLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        TextLabel.TextSize = 12
                        TextLabel.ZIndex = 1004
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        Items["ButtonHolder"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
            Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 18),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ZIndex = 1003,
            LayoutOrder = 5,
            Name = "\0"
        })

        Items["Confirm"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
            Parent = Items["ButtonHolder"].Instance,
            BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
            BorderSizePixel = 2,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Name = "\0",
            Text = "",
            Selectable = false,
            Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
            ZIndex = 1003,
            BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(33, 33, 36)
        }):AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })

        Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
            Parent = Items["Confirm"].Instance,
            Rotation = 90,
            Color = RGBSequence{ RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100)) }
        })

        Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
            Parent = Items["Confirm"].Instance,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
            LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
            Name = "\0",
            Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
        }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Outline" })

        Items["ConfirmText"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
            Parent = Items["Confirm"].Instance,
            FontFace = Library.Font,
            TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
            Text = Popup.ConfirmText or "Dismiss",
            Name = "\0",
            Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
            Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, -1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            TextSize = 12,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            ZIndex = 1004
        }):AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })

        Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
            Parent = Items["ConfirmText"].Instance,
            LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
            Name = "\0"
        }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

        task.defer(function()
            TweenService:Create(Items["Backdrop"].Instance, CachedQuartTweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.55 }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(Items["Popup"].Instance, CachedQuartTweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
        end)

        local Close = function()
            if Closed then return end
            Closed = true
            TweenService:Create(Items["Backdrop"].Instance, CachedQuartTweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(Items["Popup"].Instance, CachedQuartTweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
            for _, child in ipairs(Items["Popup"].Instance:GetDescendants()) do
                if child:IsA("TextLabel") or child:IsA("TextButton") then
                    TweenService:Create(child, CachedQuartTweenInfo, { TextTransparency = 1, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                elseif child:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    TweenService:Create(child, CachedQuartTweenInfo, { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
                elseif child:IsA("Frame") or child:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                    TweenService:Create(child, CachedQuartTweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                end
            end
            task.delay(0.35, function()
                if Items["Backdrop"] and Items["Backdrop"].Instance and Items["Backdrop"].Instance.Parent then
                    Items["Backdrop"]:Clean()
                end
                if dialogGui and dialogGui.Parent then
                    dialogGui:Destroy()
                end
            end)
            if Popup.OnConfirm then
                Library:SafeCall(Popup.OnConfirm)
            end
        end

        Items["Confirm"]:OnHover(function()
            if not Closed then
                TweenService:Create(Items["Confirm"].Instance, CachedHoverTweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"] }):Play()
                Items["Confirm"]:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })
            end
        end)

        Items["Confirm"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
            if not Closed then
                TweenService:Create(Items["Confirm"].Instance, CachedHoverTweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Element"] }):Play()
                Items["Confirm"]:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })
            end
        end)

        Items["Confirm"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            if not Closed then
                Items["ConfirmText"]:ChangeItemTheme({ TextColor3 = "Accent" })
                Items["Confirm"]:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Accent" })
                Items["ConfirmText"]:Tween(nil, { TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent })
                Items["Confirm"]:Tween(nil, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent })
                task.delay(0.1, function()
                    Close()
                end)
            end
        end)

        Popup.Items = Items
        function Popup:Close()
            Close()
        end

        return Popup
    end

    Library.Playerlist = function(self, Window)
        if not Window then
            return
        end

        local Playerlist = {
            Window = Window,
            Players = { },
            CurrentPlayer = nil,
            Elements = { }
        }

        local StatusColors = {
            Local = FromRGB(100, 160, 255),
            Neutral = FromRGB(180, 180, 185),
            Friendly = FromRGB(95, 217, 122),
            Enemy = FromRGB(220, 90, 90)
        }

        local PlayersPage = Window:Page({
            Name = "Players",
            Columns = 1
        })

        Playerlist.Page = PlayersPage

        local ListSection = PlayersPage:Section({
            Name = "Players",
            Side = 1
        })

        local Items = { }
        Items["Holder"] = InstanceNew("ScrollingFrame")

        Items["Holder"].BorderColor3 = Library.Theme.Border

        HeaderStatus.TextColor3 = FromRGB(140, 140, 148)
        HeaderStatus.Text = "Status"
        HeaderStatus.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        HeaderStatus.Size = UDim2New(0.2, 0, 1, 0)

        local HeaderAlive = InstanceNew("TextLabel")

        HeaderAlive.Text = "Alive"

        HeaderAlive.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        HeaderAlive.BorderSizePixel = 0
        HeaderAlive.TextSize = 11
        HeaderAlive.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        local HeaderDist = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
        HeaderDist.Parent = HeaderRow

        HeaderDist.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        HeaderDist.Size = UDim2New(0.25, 0, 1, 0)
        HeaderDist.Position = UDim2New(0.75, 0, 0, 0)
        HeaderDist.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        HeaderDist.BorderSizePixel = 0
        HeaderDist.TextSize = 11
        HeaderDist.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        local DetailSection = PlayersPage:Section({
            Name = "Selected Player",
            Side = 1
        })

        local AvatarFrame = InstanceNew("ImageLabel")
        AvatarFrame.Parent = DetailSection.Elements["Content"].Instance
        AvatarFrame.Name = "\0"
        AvatarFrame.Image = "rbxassetid://0"
        AvatarFrame.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        AvatarFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Background
        AvatarFrame.BorderColor3 = Library.Theme.Border
        AvatarFrame.BorderSizePixel = 1
        AvatarFrame.Size = UDim2New(0, 64, 0, 64)
        AvatarFrame.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0)

        Library:AddToTheme(AvatarFrame, { BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border" })

        local AvatarStroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
        AvatarStroke.Parent = AvatarFrame
        AvatarStroke.Color = Library.Theme.Outline
        AvatarStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        AvatarStroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter

        local DetailName = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
        DetailName.Parent = DetailSection.Elements["Content"].Instance
        DetailName.Name = "\0"
        DetailName.FontFace = Library.Font
        DetailName.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text
        DetailName.Text = "No player selected"
        DetailName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        DetailName.Size = UDim2New(1, -200, 0, 16)
        DetailName.Position = UDim2New(0, 74, 0, 0)
        DetailName.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        DetailName.BorderSizePixel = 0
        DetailName.TextSize = 13
        DetailName.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        Library:AddToTheme(DetailName, { TextColor3 = "Text" })

        local DetailId = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
        DetailId.Parent = DetailSection.Elements["Content"].Instance
        DetailId.Name = "\0"
        DetailId.FontFace = Library.Font

        DetailId.Text = "UserID: -"
        DetailId.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        DetailId.Size = UDim2New(1, -200, 0, 14)
        DetailId.Position = UDim2New(0, 74, 0, 18)
        DetailId.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        DetailId.BorderSizePixel = 0
        DetailId.TextSize = 11
        DetailId.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        local DetailStatus = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
        DetailStatus.Parent = DetailSection.Elements["Content"].Instance
        DetailStatus.Name = "\0"
        DetailStatus.FontFace = Library.Font
        DetailStatus.TextColor3 = FromRGB(140, 140, 148)
        DetailStatus.Text = "Status: -"
        DetailStatus.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        DetailStatus.Size = UDim2New(1, -200, 0, 14)
        DetailStatus.Position = UDim2New(0, 74, 0, 34)
        DetailStatus.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        DetailStatus.BorderSizePixel = 0
        DetailStatus.TextSize = 11
        DetailStatus.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        local CopyIconColor = FromRGB(140, 140, 148)

        local ClipboardImage = Library:GetImage("Clipboard")

        CopyNameIcon.Name = "\0"
        CopyNameIcon.Image = ClipboardImage
        CopyNameIcon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        CopyNameIcon.ImageColor3 = CopyIconColor
        CopyNameIcon.ImageTransparency = 0.2
        CopyNameIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        CopyNameIcon.Size = UDim2New(0, 12, 0, 12)

        CopyNameIcon.ZIndex = 20
        CopyNameIcon.Visible = false
        CopyNameIcon.AutoButtonColor = false

        CopyNameIcon.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            CopyNameIcon.ImageColor3 = CopyIconHover
            CopyNameIcon.ImageTransparency = 0
        end)
        CopyNameIcon.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            CopyNameIcon.ImageColor3 = CopyIconColor
            CopyNameIcon.ImageTransparency = 0.2
        end)
        CopyNameIcon.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
            if Playerlist.CurrentPlayer then
                local Text = Playerlist.CurrentPlayer.Name
                pcall(function() setclipboard(Text) end)
                Library:Notification("Username copied: " .. Text, 2, Library.Theme.Accent)
            end
        end)

        local CopyIdIcon = InstanceNew("ImageButton")
        CopyIdIcon.Parent = DetailSection.Elements["Section"].Instance
        CopyIdIcon.Name = "\0"
        CopyIdIcon.Image = ClipboardImage
        CopyIdIcon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        CopyIdIcon.ImageColor3 = CopyIconColor
        CopyIdIcon.ImageTransparency = 0.2
        CopyIdIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        CopyIdIcon.Size = UDim2New(0, 12, 0, 12)
        CopyIdIcon.Position = UDim2New(0, 74, 0, 20)
        CopyIdIcon.ZIndex = 20
        CopyIdIcon.Visible = false
        CopyIdIcon.AutoButtonColor = false

        CopyIdIcon.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            CopyIdIcon.ImageColor3 = CopyIconHover
            CopyIdIcon.ImageTransparency = 0
        end)
        CopyIdIcon.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            CopyIdIcon.ImageColor3 = CopyIconColor
            CopyIdIcon.ImageTransparency = 0.2
        end)
        CopyIdIcon.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
            if Playerlist.CurrentPlayer then
                local Text = tostring(Playerlist.CurrentPlayer.UserId)
                pcall(function() setclipboard(Text) end)
                Library:Notification("UserID copied: " .. Text, 2, Library.Theme.Accent)
            end
        end)

        local UpdateCopyIconPositions = function()
            local NameW = DetailName.TextBounds.X
            local IdW = DetailId.TextBounds.X
            if NameW > 0 then
                CopyNameIcon.Position = UDim2New(0, 7 + 74 + NameW + 4, 0, 21 + 1)
            end
            if IdW > 0 then
                CopyIdIcon.Position = UDim2New(0, 7 + 74 + IdW + 4, 0, 21 + 18)
            end
        end

        DetailName:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            task.defer(UpdateCopyIconPositions)
        end)
        DetailId:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            task.defer(UpdateCopyIconPositions)
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            for _ = 1, 10 do
                task.wait(0.05)
                UpdateCopyIconPositions()
                if DetailName.TextBounds.X > 0 and DetailId.TextBounds.X > 0 then
                    break
                end
            end
        end)

        local SpectatingState = {
            Active = false,
            Player = nil,
            OriginalSubject = nil,
            OriginalCameraType = nil
        }

        local StopSpectating = function()
            if not SpectatingState.Active then return end
            pcall(function()
                if SpectatingState.OriginalCameraType ~= nil then
                    Camera.CameraType = SpectatingState.OriginalCameraType
                    SpectatingState.OriginalCameraType = nil
                end
                if SpectatingState.OriginalSubject ~= nil then
                    Camera.CameraSubject = SpectatingState.OriginalSubject
                    SpectatingState.OriginalSubject = nil
                elseif LocalPlayer.Character then
                    local Hum = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if Hum then
                        Camera.CameraSubject = Hum
                    end
                end
            end)
            SpectatingState.Active = false
            SpectatingState.Player = nil
            Library:Notification("Stopped spectating", 2, Library.Theme.Accent)
        end

        local StartSpectating = function(Player)
            if not Player then return end
            if SpectatingState.Active and SpectatingState.Player == Player then
                StopSpectating()
                return
            end

            if not SpectatingState.Active then
                SpectatingState.OriginalSubject = Camera.CameraSubject
                SpectatingState.OriginalCameraType = Camera.CameraType
            end

            SpectatingState.Active = true
            SpectatingState.Player = Player
            pcall(function()
                Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
                local Char = Player.Character
                if Char then
                    local HRP = Char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if HRP then
                        Camera.CameraSubject = HRP
                        Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                    end
                end
            end)
            Library:Notification("Spectating " .. Player.DisplayName, 2, Library.Theme.Accent)
        end

        local WatchSpectate = InstanceNew("Frame")
        WatchSpectate.Parent = DetailSection.Elements["Content"].Instance
        WatchSpectate.Name = "\0"
        WatchSpectate.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        WatchSpectate.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 102)
        WatchSpectate.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 4)
        WatchSpectate.BorderSizePixel = 0
        WatchSpectate.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        local StatusDropdown = InstanceNew("TextButton")
        StatusDropdown.Parent = DetailSection.Elements["Section"].Instance
        StatusDropdown.Name = "\0"
        StatusDropdown.FontFace = Library.Font
        StatusDropdown.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text
        StatusDropdown.Text = "Neutral"
        StatusDropdown.AutoButtonColor = false
        StatusDropdown.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0)
        StatusDropdown.Position = UDim2New(1, -7, 0, 21)
        StatusDropdown.Size = UDim2New(0, 140, 0, 22)
        StatusDropdown.BorderSizePixel = 2
        StatusDropdown.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element
        StatusDropdown.BorderColor3 = Library.Theme.Border
        StatusDropdown.TextSize = 12
        StatusDropdown.ZIndex = 10

        Library:AddToTheme(StatusDropdown, { BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border", TextColor3 = "Text" })

        local SDStroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
        SDStroke.Parent = StatusDropdown
        SDStroke.Color = Library.Theme.Outline
        SDStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        SDStroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter

        Library:AddToTheme(SDStroke, { Color = "Outline" })

        local SDGradient = InstanceNew("UIGradient")
        SDGradient.Parent = StatusDropdown
        SDGradient.Rotation = 90
        SDGradient.Color = RGBSequence{ RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100)) }

        local SDIcon = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
        SDIcon.Parent = StatusDropdown
        SDIcon.Name = "\0"
        SDIcon.FontFace = Library.Font
        SDIcon.TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215)
        SDIcon.Text = "+"
        SDIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        SDIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0.5)
        SDIcon.Position = UDim2New(1, -4, 0.5, 0)
        SDIcon.Size = UDim2New(0, 12, 1, 0)
        SDIcon.BorderSizePixel = 0
        SDIcon.TextSize = 12
        SDIcon.ZIndex = 11
        SDIcon.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        local SDOptions = InstanceNew("Frame")
        SDOptions.Parent = Library.Holder.Instance
        SDOptions.Name = "\0"
        SDOptions.Visible = false
        SDOptions.ZIndex = 100
        SDOptions.BorderSizePixel = 2
        SDOptions.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        SDOptions.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Inline
        SDOptions.BorderColor3 = Library.Theme.Border

        Library:AddToTheme(SDOptions, { BackgroundColor3 = "Inline", BorderColor3 = "Border" })

        local SDOptionsStroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
        SDOptionsStroke.Parent = SDOptions
        SDOptionsStroke.Color = Library.Theme.Outline
        SDOptionsStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        SDOptionsStroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter

        Library:AddToTheme(SDOptionsStroke, { Color = "Outline" })

        local SDOptionsLayout = InstanceNew("UIListLayout")
        SDOptionsLayout.Parent = SDOptions
        SDOptionsLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        SDOptionsLayout.Padding = UDimNew(0, 2)

        local SDOptionsPad = InstanceNew("UIPadding")
        SDOptionsPad.Parent = SDOptions
        SDOptionsPad.PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 4)
        SDOptionsPad.PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 4)
        SDOptionsPad.PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 4)
        SDOptionsPad.PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 4)

        local CurrentStatus = "Neutral"
        local SDOpen = false

        local SetStatusDropdownValue = function(Value)
            CurrentStatus = Value
            StatusDropdown.Text = Value

            if not Playerlist.CurrentPlayer then
                return
            end

            if Playerlist.CurrentPlayer == LocalPlayer then
                return
            end

            local PlayerData = Playerlist.Players[Playerlist.CurrentPlayer.Name]
            if PlayerData then
                PlayerData.Status = Value
                PlayerData.StatusLabel.TextColor3 = StatusColors[Value] or StatusColors.Neutral
                PlayerData.StatusLabel.Text = Value

                if DetailStatus then
                    DetailStatus.Text = "Status: " .. Value
                    DetailStatus.TextColor3 = StatusColors[Value] or StatusColors.Neutral
                end

                Playerlist:SetStatus(Playerlist.CurrentPlayer, Value)
            end
        end

        local StatusItems = { "Neutral", "Friendly", "Enemy" }

        for _, Item in StatusItems do
            local OptBtn = InstanceNew("TextButton")
            OptBtn.Parent = SDOptions
            OptBtn.Name = "\0"
            OptBtn.FontFace = Library.Font
            OptBtn.TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208)
            OptBtn.Text = Item
            OptBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
            OptBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            OptBtn.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 16)
            OptBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            OptBtn.TextSize = 11
            OptBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            OptBtn.ZIndex = 101
            OptBtn.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

            Library:AddToTheme(OptBtn, { TextColor3 = "Text" })

            OptBtn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
                SetStatusDropdownValue(Item)
                SDOpen = false
                SDOptions.Visible = false
                SDIcon.Text = "+"
            end)

            OptBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                OptBtn.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
            end)

            OptBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                OptBtn.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text
            end)
        end

        local SDJustOpened = false

        StatusDropdown.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
            SDOpen = not SDOpen

            if SDOpen then
                local AbsPos = StatusDropdown.AbsolutePosition
                local AbsSize = StatusDropdown.AbsoluteSize
                SDOptions.Position = UDim2New(0, AbsPos.X, 0, AbsPos.Y + AbsSize.Y + 4)
                SDOptions.Size = UDim2New(0, AbsSize.X, 0, 0)
                SDOptions.Visible = true
                SDIcon.Text = "-"
                SDJustOpened = true
                task.delay(0.15, function()
                    SDJustOpened = false
                end)
            else
                SDOptions.Visible = false
                SDIcon.Text = "+"
            end
        end)

        Library:Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(Input)
            if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and SDOpen and not SDJustOpened then
                local MousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                local GuiInset = game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset().Y
                MousePos = Vector2New(MousePos.X, MousePos.Y - GuiInset)

                local DPos = StatusDropdown.AbsolutePosition
                local DSize = StatusDropdown.AbsoluteSize
                local OPos = SDOptions.AbsolutePosition
                local OSize = SDOptions.AbsoluteSize

                local InDropdown = MousePos.X >= DPos.X and MousePos.X <= DPos.X + DSize.X and MousePos.Y >= DPos.Y and MousePos.Y <= DPos.Y + DSize.Y
                local InOptions = MousePos.X >= OPos.X and MousePos.X <= OPos.X + OSize.X and MousePos.Y >= OPos.Y and MousePos.Y <= OPos.Y + OSize.Y

                if not InDropdown and not InOptions then
                    SDOpen = false
                    SDOptions.Visible = false
                    SDIcon.Text = "+"
                end
            end
        end)

        local SpectateToggleOn = false

        local SpectateToggle = DetailSection:Toggle({
            Name = "Spectate",
            Default = false,
            Flag = "Playerlist_Spectate",
            Callback = function(Value)
                SpectateToggleOn = Value
                if Value then
                    if not Playerlist.CurrentPlayer then
                        Library:Notification("No player selected", 2, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                        SpectateToggle:Set(false)
                        return
                    end
                    StartSpectating(Playerlist.CurrentPlayer)
                else
                    StopSpectating()
                end
            end
        })

        if SpectateToggle.Items and SpectateToggle.Items["Toggle"] then
            local ToggleFrame = SpectateToggle.Items["Toggle"].Instance
            ToggleFrame.Parent = DetailSection.Elements["Section"].Instance
            ToggleFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0)
            ToggleFrame.Position = UDim2New(1, -7, 0, 48)
            ToggleFrame.Size = UDim2New(0, 140, 0, 16)
            ToggleFrame.ZIndex = 10
        end

        local UpdateSpectateToggleVisual = function(Value)
            if Value ~= nil then
                SpectateToggleOn = Value
                SpectateToggle:Set(Value)
            end
        end

        local TeleportBtn = InstanceNew("TextButton")
        TeleportBtn.Parent = DetailSection.Elements["Section"].Instance
        TeleportBtn.Name = "\0"
        TeleportBtn.FontFace = Library.Font
        TeleportBtn.TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208)
        TeleportBtn.Text = "Teleport"
        TeleportBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        TeleportBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0)
        TeleportBtn.Position = UDim2New(1, -7, 0, 75)
        TeleportBtn.Size = UDim2New(0, 140, 0, 22)
        TeleportBtn.BorderSizePixel = 2
        TeleportBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element
        TeleportBtn.BorderColor3 = Library.Theme.Border
        TeleportBtn.TextSize = 12
        TeleportBtn.ZIndex = 10

        Library:AddToTheme(TeleportBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border", TextColor3 = "Text" })

        local TeleportStroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
        TeleportStroke.Parent = TeleportBtn
        TeleportStroke.Color = Library.Theme.Outline
        TeleportStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        TeleportStroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
        Library:AddToTheme(TeleportStroke, { Color = "Outline" })

        local TeleportGradient = InstanceNew("UIGradient")
        TeleportGradient.Parent = TeleportBtn
        TeleportGradient.Rotation = 90
        TeleportGradient.Color = RGBSequence{ RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100)) }

        TeleportBtn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
            if not Playerlist.CurrentPlayer then
                Library:Notification("No player selected", 2, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                return
            end
            if Playerlist.CurrentPlayer == LocalPlayer then
                Library:Notification("Cannot teleport to yourself", 2, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                return
            end

            local TargetChar = Playerlist.CurrentPlayer.Character
            if not TargetChar then
                Library:Notification("Target has no character", 2, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                return
            end
            local TargetHRP = TargetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not TargetHRP then
                Library:Notification("Target has no HumanoidRootPart", 2, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                return
            end

            local LocalChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not LocalChar then
                Library:Notification("You have no character", 2, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                return
            end
            local LocalHRP = LocalChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not LocalHRP then
                Library:Notification("You have no HumanoidRootPart", 2, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                return
            end

            LocalHRP.CFrame = TargetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 4)
            Library:Notification("Teleported to " .. Playerlist.CurrentPlayer.DisplayName, 2, Library.Theme.Accent)
        end)

        TeleportBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TeleportBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]
        end)
        TeleportBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TeleportBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element
        end)

        local GetDistance = function(Player)
            local Char = Player.Character
            if not Char then
                return -1
            end

            local HRP = Char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not HRP then
                return -1
            end

            local LocalChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not LocalChar then
                return -1
            end

            local LocalHRP = LocalChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not LocalHRP then
                return -1
            end

            local Dist = (HRP.Position - LocalHRP.Position).Magnitude
            return MathFloor(Dist)
        end

        local IsAlive = function(Player)
            local Char = Player.Character
            if not Char then
                return false
            end

            local Hum = Char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not Hum then
                return false
            end

            return Hum.Health > 0
        end

        local UpdateDetailPanel = function(Player)
            if not Player then
                AvatarFrame.Image = "rbxassetid://0"
                DetailName.Text = "No player selected"
                DetailId.Text = "UserID: -"
                DetailStatus.Text = "Status: -"
                DetailStatus.TextColor3 = FromRGB(140, 140, 148)
                CopyNameIcon.Visible = false
                CopyIdIcon.Visible = false
                StopSpectating()
                SpectateToggleOn = false
                UpdateSpectateToggleVisual()
                if SDOpen then
                    SDOpen = false
                    SDOptions.Visible = false
                    SDIcon.Text = "+"
                end
                CurrentStatus = "Neutral"
                StatusDropdown.Text = "Neutral"
                return
            end

            local Success, Thumbnail = pcall(function()
                return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(Player.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420)
            end)

            if Success and Thumbnail then
                AvatarFrame.Image = Thumbnail
            end

            DetailName.Text = Player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. Player.Name .. ")"
            DetailId.Text = "UserID: " .. tostring(Player.UserId)

            if Player == LocalPlayer then
                DetailStatus.Text = "Status: Local Player"
                DetailStatus.TextColor3 = StatusColors.Local
            else
                local PlayerData = Playerlist.Players[Player.Name]
                local Status = PlayerData and PlayerData.Status or "Neutral"
                DetailStatus.Text = "Status: " .. Status
                DetailStatus.TextColor3 = StatusColors[Status] or StatusColors.Neutral
            end

            CopyNameIcon.Visible = true
            CopyIdIcon.Visible = true
            UpdateCopyIconPositions()

            if SpectatingState.Active and SpectatingState.Player ~= Player then
                StopSpectating()
                SpectateToggleOn = false
                UpdateSpectateToggleVisual()
            end

            if SDOpen then
                SDOpen = false
                SDOptions.Visible = false
                SDIcon.Text = "+"
            end

            if Player == LocalPlayer then
                CurrentStatus = "Local"
                StatusDropdown.Text = "Local"
            else
                local PlayerData = Playerlist.Players[Player.Name]
                local Status = PlayerData and PlayerData.Status or "Neutral"
                CurrentStatus = Status
                StatusDropdown.Text = Status
            end
        end

        local SelectPlayer = function(Player)
            for _, Data in Playerlist.Players do
                Data.Selected = false
                if Data.Background then
                    TweenService:Create(Data.Background, CachedHoverTweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                end
            end

            if Player then
                local PlayerData = Playerlist.Players[Player.Name]
                if PlayerData then
                    PlayerData.Selected = true
                    if PlayerData.Background then
                        TweenService:Create(PlayerData.Background, CachedHoverTweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.7 }):Play()
                    end
                end
            end

            Playerlist.CurrentPlayer = Player
            UpdateDetailPanel(Player)
        end

        function Playerlist:Add(Player)
            if Playerlist.Players[Player.Name] then
                return
            end

            local Row = InstanceNew("TextButton")
            Row.Parent = Items["Holder"]
            Row.Name = "\0"
            Row.FontFace = Library.Font
            Row.TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            Row.Text = ""
            Row.AutoButtonColor = false
            Row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            Row.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 22)
            Row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            Row.TextSize = 12
            Row.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

            local Bg = InstanceNew("Frame")
            Bg.Parent = Row
            Bg.Name = "\0"
            Bg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            Bg.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0)
            Bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
            Bg.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]

            Library:AddToTheme(Bg, { BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element" })

            local NameLabel = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
            NameLabel.Parent = Row
            NameLabel.Name = "\0"
            NameLabel.FontFace = Library.Font
            NameLabel.TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208)
            NameLabel.Text = Player.DisplayName
            NameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            NameLabel.Size = UDim2New(0.4, 0, 1, 0)
            NameLabel.Position = UDim2New(0, 4, 0, 0)
            NameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            NameLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
            NameLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
            NameLabel.TextSize = 11
            NameLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            NameLabel.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

            Library:AddToTheme(NameLabel, { TextColor3 = "Text" })

            local StatusText = "Neutral"
            local StatusColor = StatusColors.Neutral

            if Player == LocalPlayer then
                StatusText = "Local"
                StatusColor = StatusColors.Local
            end

            local StatusLbl = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
            StatusLbl.Parent = Row
            StatusLbl.Name = "\0"
            StatusLbl.FontFace = Library.Font
            StatusLbl.TextColor3 = StatusColor
            StatusLbl.Text = StatusText
            StatusLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            StatusLbl.Size = UDim2New(0.2, 0, 1, 0)
            StatusLbl.Position = UDim2New(0.4, 0, 0, 0)
            StatusLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            StatusLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
            StatusLbl.BorderSizePixel = 0
            StatusLbl.TextSize = 11
            StatusLbl.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

            local AliveLbl = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
            AliveLbl.Parent = Row
            AliveLbl.Name = "\0"
            AliveLbl.FontFace = Library.Font
            AliveLbl.TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208)
            AliveLbl.Text = IsAlive(Player) and "Yes" or "No"
            AliveLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            AliveLbl.Size = UDim2New(0.15, 0, 1, 0)
            AliveLbl.Position = UDim2New(0.6, 0, 0, 0)
            AliveLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            AliveLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
            AliveLbl.BorderSizePixel = 0
            AliveLbl.TextSize = 11
            AliveLbl.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

            Library:AddToTheme(AliveLbl, { TextColor3 = "Text" })

            local DistLbl = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
            DistLbl.Parent = Row
            DistLbl.Name = "\0"
            DistLbl.FontFace = Library.Font
            DistLbl.TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208)
            DistLbl.Text = tostring(GetDistance(Player)) .. "st"
            DistLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            DistLbl.Size = UDim2New(0.25, 0, 1, 0)
            DistLbl.Position = UDim2New(0.75, 0, 0, 0)
            DistLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            DistLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
            DistLbl.BorderSizePixel = 0
            DistLbl.TextSize = 11
            DistLbl.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

            Library:AddToTheme(DistLbl, { TextColor3 = "Text" })

            local PlayerData = {
                Player = Player,
                Name = Player.Name,
                Status = "Neutral",
                Selected = false,
                Row = Row,
                Background = Bg,
                NameLabel = NameLabel,
                StatusLabel = StatusLbl,
                AliveLabel = AliveLbl,
                DistanceLabel = DistLbl
            }

            if Player == LocalPlayer then
                PlayerData.Status = "Local"
            end

            Row.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
                SelectPlayer(Player)
            end)

            Row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                if not PlayerData.Selected then
                    TweenService:Create(Bg, CachedHoverTweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.85 }):Play()
                end
            end)

            Row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                if not PlayerData.Selected then
                    TweenService:Create(Bg, CachedHoverTweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                end
            end)

            Playerlist.Players[Player.Name] = PlayerData
            return PlayerData
        end

        function Playerlist:Remove(Name)
            local Data = Playerlist.Players[Name]
            if Data then
                Data.Row:Destroy()
            end
            Playerlist.Players[Name] = nil

            if Playerlist.CurrentPlayer and Playerlist.CurrentPlayer.Name == Name then
                Playerlist.CurrentPlayer = nil
                UpdateDetailPanel(nil)
            end
        end

        function Playerlist:Refresh()
            for _, Data in Playerlist.Players do
                if Data.AliveLabel then
                    Data.AliveLabel.Text = IsAlive(Data.Player) and "Yes" or "No"
                end
                if Data.DistanceLabel then
                    Data.DistanceLabel.Text = tostring(GetDistance(Data.Player)) .. "st"
                end
            end

            if Playerlist.CurrentPlayer then
                UpdateDetailPanel(Playerlist.CurrentPlayer)
            end
        end

        function Playerlist:GetSelected()
            return Playerlist.CurrentPlayer
        end

        function Playerlist:GetStatus(Player)
            if not Player then
                return nil
            end

            local Data = Playerlist.Players[Player.Name]
            return Data and Data.Status or "Neutral"
        end

        function Playerlist:GetAllStatuses()
            local Result = { }
            for Name, Data in Playerlist.Players do
                Result[Name] = Data.Status or "Neutral"
            end
            return Result
        end

        function Playerlist:SetStatus(Player, Status)
            if not Player then return end
            local Data = Playerlist.Players[Player.Name]
            if Data then
                Data.Status = Status
                Data.StatusLabel.Text = Status
                Data.StatusLabel.TextColor3 = StatusColors[Status] or StatusColors.Neutral
            end
        end

        for _, Player in Players:GetPlayers() do
            Playerlist:Add(Player)
        end

        Library:Connect(Players.PlayerAdded, function(Player)
            Playerlist:Add(Player)
        end)

        Library:Connect(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(Player)
            Playerlist:Remove(Player.Name)
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                if Playerlist.Window.IsOpen then
                    Playerlist:Refresh()
                end
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                if SpectatingState.Active and SpectatingState.Player then
                    pcall(function()
                        local Char = SpectatingState.Player.Character
                        if Char then
                            local HRP = Char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if HRP then
                                Camera.CameraSubject = HRP
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                task.wait(0.03)
            end
        end)

        Playerlist.Elements = Items
        Library.PlayerlistInstance = Playerlist
        return Playerlist
    end

    Library.MusicPlayer = function(self)
        local MusicPlayer = { }

        local HttpRequest
        if syn and syn.request then
            HttpRequest = syn.request
        elseif http and http.request then
            HttpRequest = http.request
        elseif http_request then
            HttpRequest = http_request
        elseif request then
            HttpRequest = request
        end

        local LastError = ""
        local EqBars = { }
        local ArtistRainbowActive = false

        local FormatTime = function(ms)
            local TotalSec = MathFloor(ms / 1000)
            local Min = MathFloor(TotalSec / 60)
            local Sec = TotalSec % 60
            return StringFormat("%d:%02d", Min, Sec)
        end

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Window"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
