            local function CreateControlButton(Text)
                local Btn = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                    Parent = Items["Controls"].Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                    Text = Text,
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(33, 33, 36),
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                    BorderSizePixel = 2,
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 60, 0, 24),
                    TextSize = 12,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                })
                Btn:AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border", TextColor3 = "Text" })

                Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = Btn.Instance,
                    ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                    LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                    Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
                }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Outline" })

                Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                    Parent = Btn.Instance,
                    Rotation = 90,
                    Color = RGBSequence{ RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100)) }
                })

                Btn:OnHover(function()
                    TweenService:Create(Btn.Instance, CachedHoverTweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"] }):Play()
                end)

                Btn:OnHoverLeave(function()
                    TweenService:Create(Btn.Instance, CachedHoverTweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element }):Play()
                end)

                return Btn
            end

            Items["PrevBtn"] = CreateControlButton("<<")
            Items["PlayBtn"] = CreateControlButton("Play")
            Items["NextBtn"] = CreateControlButton(">>")

            Items["LyricsDivider"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Window"].Instance,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(38, 38, 45),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 1),
                ZIndex = 2
            })
            Items["LyricsDivider"]:AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Outline" })

            Items["LyricsHolder"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Window"].Instance,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["LyricsHolder"].Instance,
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
                Padding = UDimNew(0, 2),
                HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
            })

            Items["LyricsPrev"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["LyricsHolder"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(90, 90, 98),
                Text = "",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextWrapped = true,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Items["LyricsCurrent"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["LyricsHolder"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                Text = "No lyrics",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                TextWrapped = true,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })
            Items["LyricsCurrent"]:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["LyricsCurrent"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
            }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

            Items["LyricsNext"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["LyricsHolder"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(90, 90, 98),
                Text = "",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextWrapped = true,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })
        end

        local State = {
            IsPlaying = false,
            Progress = 0,
            Duration = 0,
            TrackId = nil,
            Seeking = false,
            Tempo = 120,
            Energy = 0.5,
            Danceability = 0.5,
            Segments = nil,
            Beats = nil,
            LastSegIdx = 1,
            LastBeatIdx = 1,
            BeatPulse = 0,
            LastFrameTime = 0,
            BassPhase = 0,
            MidPhase = 0,
            TreblePhase = 0,
            Lyrics = nil,
            LastLyricIdx = 0
        }

        local SpotifyRequest = function(method, url, body)
            if not Library.SpotifyToken or Library.SpotifyToken == "" then
                LastError = "No token set"
                return nil, 0
            end

            if not HttpRequest then
                LastError = "No HTTP function found"
                return nil, 0
            end

            local Token = Library.SpotifyToken
            if Token:lower():sub(1, 7) == "bearer " then
                Token = Token:sub(8)
            end

            local RequestOptions = {
                Url = url,
                Method = method,
                Headers = {
                    ["Authorization"] = "Bearer " .. Token,
                    ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
                    ["Accept"] = "application/json"
                }
            }

            if body and body ~= "" then
                RequestOptions.Body = body
            end

            local Success, Response = pcall(HttpRequest, RequestOptions)

            if not Success then
                LastError = tostring(Response):sub(1, 100)
                return nil, 0
            end

            if not Response then
                LastError = "Empty response"
                return nil, 0
            end

            local ResBody = Response.Body or Response.body or ""
            local ResCode = Response.StatusCode or Response.statusCode or Response.Status or 0

            if type(ResCode) == "string" then
                ResCode = tonumber(ResCode:match("(%d+)")) or 0
            end

            return ResBody, ResCode
        end

        local ShowDebug = false

        local UpdateFromSpotify = function()
            local Body, Code = SpotifyRequest("GET", "https://api.spotify.com/v1/me/player/currently-playing")

            if ShowDebug then
                ShowDebug = false
                if Code == 0 then
                    Library:Notification("Music: " .. LastError, 7, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                elseif Code == 401 then
                    Library:Notification("Music: Token invalid/expired (401)", 5, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                elseif Code == 204 then
                    Library:Notification("Music: Connected! No song playing (204)", 5, Library.Theme.Accent)
                elseif Code == 200 then
                    Library:Notification("Music: Connected & playing! (200)", 5, Library.Theme.Accent)
                else
                    Library:Notification("Music: HTTP " .. tostring(Code), 5, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                end
            end

            if Code == 204 or Code == 0 or not Body or Body == "" then
                State.IsPlaying = false
                Items["PlayBtn"].Instance.Text = "Play"
                Items["EqHolder"].Instance.Visible = false
                return
            end

            if Code ~= 200 then
                if Code == 401 then
                    Items["SongName"].Instance.Text = "Token expired"
                    Items["AlbumName"].Instance.Text = "Re-authenticate"
                    Items["ArtistName"].Instance.Text = "-"
                end
                return
            end

            local Success, Data = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(Body)
            end)

            if not Success or not Data then
                return
            end

            State.IsPlaying = Data.is_playing or false
            State.Progress = Data.progress_ms or 0
            State.Duration = (Data.item and Data.item.duration_ms) or 0

            local Item = Data.item
            if Item then
                if Item.id ~= State.TrackId then
                    State.TrackId = Item.id

                    Items["SongName"].Instance.Text = Item.name or "Unknown"

                    local Album = Item.album
                    if Album then
                        Items["AlbumName"].Instance.Text = Album.name or "-"

                        if Album.images and Album.images[1] then
                            local ImgUrl = Album.images[1].url
                            if ImgUrl and ImgUrl ~= "" then
                                task.spawn(function()
                                    local ImgSuccess, ImgData = pcall(function()
                                        local ImgReq = HttpRequest({
                                            Url = ImgUrl,
                                            Method = "GET"
                                        })
                                        return ImgReq.Body
                                    end)

                                    if ImgSuccess and ImgData and ImgData ~= "" then
                                        local CachePath = "opiods/Assets/cover_" .. tostring(Item.id) .. ".jpg"
                                        if not isfile(CachePath) then
                                            writefile(CachePath, ImgData)
                                        end

                                        local AssetId = getcustomasset(CachePath)
                                        if AssetId then
                                            Items["Cover"].Instance.Image = AssetId
                                        end
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    local Artists = {}
                    if Item.artists then
                        for _, Artist in Item.artists do
                            TableInsert(Artists, Artist.name)
                        end
                    end
                    local ArtistStr = TableConcat(Artists, ", ")
                    Items["ArtistName"].Instance.Text = ArtistStr

                    local IsCarti = false
                    for _, A in Artists do
                        if A:lower():find("playboi carti") or A:lower():find("carti") then
                            IsCarti = true
                            break
                        end
                    end

                    if IsCarti then
                        ArtistRainbowActive = true
                        Items["ArtistName"].Instance.TextColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    else
                        ArtistRainbowActive = false
                        Items["ArtistRainbow"].Instance.Color = RGBSequence{
                            RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(150, 150, 158)),
                            RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(150, 150, 158))
                        }
                        Items["ArtistName"].Instance.TextColor3 = FromRGB(150, 150, 158)
                    end

                    if Item.id then
                        local FeatBody, FeatCode = SpotifyRequest("GET", "https://api.spotify.com/v1/audio-features/" .. Item.id)
                        if FeatCode == 200 and FeatBody then
                            local FSuccess, FeatData = pcall(function()
                                return HttpService:JSONDecode(FeatBody)
                            end)
                            if FSuccess and FeatData then
                                State.Tempo = FeatData.tempo or 120
                                State.Energy = FeatData.energy or 0.5
                                State.Danceability = FeatData.danceability or 0.5
                            end
                        end

                        State.Segments = nil
                        State.Beats = nil
                        State.LastSegIdx = 1
                        State.LastBeatIdx = 1
                        State.Lyrics = nil
                        State.LastLyricIdx = 0
                        Items["LyricsPrev"].Instance.Text = ""
                        Items["LyricsCurrent"].Instance.Text = "Loading lyrics..."
                        Items["LyricsNext"].Instance.Text = ""

                        task.spawn(function()
                            local AnaBody, AnaCode = SpotifyRequest("GET", "https://api.spotify.com/v1/audio-analysis/" .. Item.id)
                            if AnaCode == 200 and AnaBody then
                                local ASuccess, AnaData = pcall(function()
                                    return HttpService:JSONDecode(AnaBody)
                                end)
                                if ASuccess and AnaData then
                                    if AnaData.beats and #AnaData.beats > 0 then
                                        State.Beats = AnaData.beats
                                    end
                                    if AnaData.segments and #AnaData.segments > 0 then
                                        State.Segments = AnaData.segments
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                State.Segments = nil
                                State.Beats = nil
                            end
                        end)

                        task.spawn(function()
                            if not HttpRequest then
                                Items["LyricsCurrent"].Instance.Text = "No HTTP function"
                                return
                            end

                            local TrackName = (Item.name or ""):gsub("%s+", "+")
                            local AlbumName = ""
                            if Item.album and Item.album.name then
                                AlbumName = Item.album.name:gsub("%s+", "+")
                            end
                            local ArtistName = ""
                            if Item.artists and Item.artists[1] then
                                ArtistName = (Item.artists[1].name or ""):gsub("%s+", "+")
                            end

                            local ParseLyrics = function(Body)
                                local Success, Data = pcall(function()
                                    return HttpService:JSONDecode(Body)
                                end)
                                if not Success or not Data then
                                    return nil
                                end

                                local Synced = nil
                                if type(Data) == "table" and Data.syncedLyrics then
                                    Synced = Data.syncedLyrics
                                elseif type(Data) == "table" and #Data > 0 then
                                    for _, Entry in Data do
                                        if Entry.syncedLyrics and Entry.syncedLyrics ~= "" then
                                            Synced = Entry.syncedLyrics
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                if not Synced then
                                    return nil
                                end

                                local ParsedLines = {}
                                for Line in Synced:gmatch("[^\n]+") do
                                    local Min, Sec, Cs, Text = Line:match("%[(%d+):(%d+)%.(%d+)%](.*)")
                                    if Min then
                                        local CsNum = tonumber(Cs) or 0
                                        local CsLen = #Cs
                                        local MsMul = 10
                                        if CsLen == 1 then MsMul = 100
                                        elseif CsLen == 2 then MsMul = 10
                                        elseif CsLen >= 3 then MsMul = 1
                                        end
                                        local TimeMs = (tonumber(Min) * 60 + tonumber(Sec)) * 1000 + CsNum * MsMul
                                        TableInsert(ParsedLines, {
                                            time = TimeMs,
                                            text = Text or ""
                                        })
                                    end
                                end
                                if #ParsedLines > 0 then
                                    return ParsedLines
                                end
                                return nil
                            end

                            local DoRequest = function(Url)
                                local Success, Response = pcall(HttpRequest, {
                                    Url = Url,
                                    Method = "GET",
                                    Headers = {
                                        ["Accept"] = "application/json"
                                    },
                                    Timeout = 10
                                })
                                if not Success or not Response then
                                    return 0, ""
                                end
                                local Body = Response.Body or Response.body or ""
                                local Code = Response.StatusCode or Response.statusCode or Response.Status or 0
                                if type(Code) == "string" then
                                    Code = tonumber(Code:match("(%d+)")) or 0
                                end
                                return Code, Body
                            end

                            local Code, Body = DoRequest(
                                "https://lrclib.net/api/get?artist_name=" .. ArtistName .. "&album_name=" .. AlbumName .. "&track_name=" .. TrackName
                            )

                            local Parsed = nil
                            if Code == 200 and Body and Body ~= "" then
                                Parsed = ParseLyrics(Body)
                            end

                            if not Parsed then
                                Code, Body = DoRequest(
                                    "https://lrclib.net/api/search?track_name=" .. TrackName .. "&artist_name=" .. ArtistName
                                )
                                if Code == 200 and Body and Body ~= "" then
                                    Parsed = ParseLyrics(Body)
                                end
                            end

                            if not Parsed then
                                Code, Body = DoRequest(
                                    "https://lrclib.net/api/search?q=" .. TrackName
                                )
                                if Code == 200 and Body and Body ~= "" then
                                    Parsed = ParseLyrics(Body)
                                end
                            end

                            if Parsed then
                                State.Lyrics = Parsed
                            else
                                Items["LyricsCurrent"].Instance.Text = "No synced lyrics"
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end

            Items["PlayBtn"].Instance.Text = State.IsPlaying and "Pause" or "Play"
            Items["EqHolder"].Instance.Visible = State.IsPlaying

            if State.Duration > 0 then
                local Ratio = State.Progress / State.Duration
                Items["ProgressFill"].Instance.Size = UDim2New(Ratio, 0, 1, 0)
                Items["TimeLabel"].Instance.Text = FormatTime(State.Progress) .. " / " .. FormatTime(State.Duration)
            end

        end

        local UpdateLyrics = function()
            if State.Lyrics and #State.Lyrics > 0 then
                local CurrentLyricIdx = 0
                local AdjustedProgress = State.Progress
                for i, Line in State.Lyrics do
                    if Line.time <= AdjustedProgress then
                        CurrentLyricIdx = i
                    else
                        break
                    end
                end

                if CurrentLyricIdx ~= State.LastLyricIdx then
                    State.LastLyricIdx = CurrentLyricIdx

                    local PrevText = ""
                    local CurrText = ""
                    local NextText = ""

                    if CurrentLyricIdx > 0 then
                        PrevText = State.Lyrics[CurrentLyricIdx - 1] and State.Lyrics[CurrentLyricIdx - 1].text or ""
                        CurrText = State.Lyrics[CurrentLyricIdx].text or ""
                        NextText = State.Lyrics[CurrentLyricIdx + 1] and State.Lyrics[CurrentLyricIdx + 1].text or ""
                    else
                        CurrText = State.Lyrics[1] and State.Lyrics[1].text or ""
                        NextText = State.Lyrics[2] and State.Lyrics[2].text or ""
                    end

                    if CurrText == "" then
                        CurrText = "♪"
                    end

                    TweenService:Create(Items["LyricsCurrent"].Instance, CachedHoverTweenInfo, { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                    task.delay(0.15, function()
                        Items["LyricsPrev"].Instance.Text = PrevText
                        Items["LyricsCurrent"].Instance.Text = CurrText
                        Items["LyricsNext"].Instance.Text = NextText
                        TweenService:Create(Items["LyricsCurrent"].Instance, CachedHoverTweenInfo, { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()
                    end)
                end
            end
        end

        local Seek = function(positionMs)
            SpotifyRequest("PUT", "https://api.spotify.com/v1/me/player/seek?position_ms=" .. tostring(positionMs))
        end

        local SendPlayback = function(action)
            local Method = "POST"
            if action == "play" or action == "pause" then
                Method = "PUT"
            end
            local _, Code = SpotifyRequest(Method, "https://api.spotify.com/v1/me/player/" .. action)
            return Code
        end

        Items["PrevBtn"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            SendPlayback("previous")
            task.wait(0.3)
            UpdateFromSpotify()
        end)

        Items["PlayBtn"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            local Action = State.IsPlaying and "pause" or "play"
            local Code = SendPlayback(Action)

            State.IsPlaying = not State.IsPlaying
            Items["PlayBtn"].Instance.Text = State.IsPlaying and "Pause" or "Play"
            Items["EqHolder"].Instance.Visible = State.IsPlaying

            task.wait(0.5)
            UpdateFromSpotify()
        end)

        Items["NextBtn"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            SendPlayback("next")
            task.wait(0.3)
            UpdateFromSpotify()
        end)

        local ProgressTrack = Items["ProgressTrack"].Instance
        local ProgressFill = Items["ProgressFill"].Instance
        local SeekMoveConn
        local SeekEndConn

        ProgressTrack.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
            State.Seeking = true
            local MousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            local GuiInset = game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset().Y
            MousePos = Vector2New(MousePos.X, MousePos.Y - GuiInset)
            local TrackPos = ProgressTrack.AbsolutePosition
            local TrackSize = ProgressTrack.AbsoluteSize
            local Ratio = MathClamp((MousePos.X - TrackPos.X) / TrackSize.X, 0, 1)

            ProgressFill.Size = UDim2New(Ratio, 0, 1, 0)
            if State.Duration > 0 then
                local NewPos = MathFloor(Ratio * State.Duration)
                Items["TimeLabel"].Instance.Text = FormatTime(NewPos) .. " / " .. FormatTime(State.Duration)
            end

            if SeekMoveConn then SeekMoveConn:Disconnect() SeekMoveConn = nil end
            if SeekEndConn then SeekEndConn:Disconnect() SeekEndConn = nil end

            SeekMoveConn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(Input)
                if State.Seeking and (Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    local mPos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                    local gInset = game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset().Y
                    mPos = Vector2New(mPos.X, mPos.Y - gInset)
                    local tPos = ProgressTrack.AbsolutePosition
                    local tSize = ProgressTrack.AbsoluteSize
                    local r = MathClamp((mPos.X - tPos.X) / tSize.X, 0, 1)

                    ProgressFill.Size = UDim2New(r, 0, 1, 0)
                    if State.Duration > 0 then
                        local nPos = MathFloor(r * State.Duration)
                        Items["TimeLabel"].Instance.Text = FormatTime(nPos) .. " / " .. FormatTime(State.Duration)
                    end
                end
            end)

            SeekEndConn = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(Input)
                if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and State.Seeking then
                    State.Seeking = false
                    if SeekMoveConn then SeekMoveConn:Disconnect() SeekMoveConn = nil end
                    if SeekEndConn then SeekEndConn:Disconnect() SeekEndConn = nil end
                    local r = ProgressFill.Size.X.Scale
                    if State.Duration > 0 then
                        local nPos = MathFloor(r * State.Duration)
                        Seek(nPos)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                pcall(UpdateFromSpotify)

                if State.IsPlaying and State.Duration > 0 then
                    State.Progress = State.Progress + 1000
                    if State.Progress > State.Duration then
                        State.Progress = State.Duration
                    end

                    if not State.Seeking then
                        local Ratio = State.Progress / State.Duration
                        ProgressFill.Size = UDim2New(Ratio, 0, 1, 0)
                        Items["TimeLabel"].Instance.Text = FormatTime(State.Progress) .. " / " .. FormatTime(State.Duration)
                    end
                end

                task.wait(1)
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                if State.IsPlaying and Items["EqHolder"].Instance.Visible then
                    local Now = os.clock()
                    local dt = State.LastFrameTime > 0 and (Now - State.LastFrameTime) or 0.033
                    State.LastFrameTime = Now
                    dt = MathClamp(dt, 0.016, 0.1)

                    State.Progress = State.Progress + (dt * 1000)
                    local ProgressSec = State.Progress / 1000

                    local CurrentSegment = nil
                    if State.Segments and #State.Segments > 0 then
                        while State.LastSegIdx <= #State.Segments do
                            local Seg = State.Segments[State.LastSegIdx]
                            if Seg.start and Seg.start <= ProgressSec then
                                CurrentSegment = Seg
                                State.LastSegIdx = State.LastSegIdx + 1
                            else
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        if not CurrentSegment and State.LastSegIdx > 1 then
                            CurrentSegment = State.Segments[State.LastSegIdx - 1]
                        end
                    end

                    if State.Beats and #State.Beats > 0 then
                        while State.LastBeatIdx <= #State.Beats do
                            local Beat = State.Beats[State.LastBeatIdx]
                            if Beat.start and Beat.start <= ProgressSec then
                                State.BeatPulse = 1
                                State.LastBeatIdx = State.LastBeatIdx + 1
                            else
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        local BeatInterval = 60 / State.Tempo
                        local BeatPhase = ProgressSec % BeatInterval
                        if BeatPhase < dt * 2 then
                            State.BeatPulse = 1
                        end
                    end

                    State.BeatPulse = State.BeatPulse * (1 - dt * 6)
                    if State.BeatPulse < 0.01 then
                        State.BeatPulse = 0
                    end

                    local BeatSpeed = (State.Tempo / 60) * 2 * math.pi * dt
                    State.BassPhase = State.BassPhase + BeatSpeed
                    State.MidPhase = State.MidPhase + BeatSpeed * 2.1
                    State.TreblePhase = State.TreblePhase + BeatSpeed * 3.7

                    local Pitches = nil
                    local Loudness = -15

                    if CurrentSegment then
                        Pitches = CurrentSegment.pitches
                        Loudness = CurrentSegment.loudness_max or CurrentSegment.loudness_start or -15
                    end

                    local LoudnessNorm = MathClamp((Loudness + 30) / 30, 0.3, 1.5)
                    local BeatBoost = State.BeatPulse * 7
                    local EnergyMul = 0.4 + State.Energy * 0.6
                    local DanceMul = 0.4 + State.Danceability * 0.6

                    for i, Bar in EqBars do
                        local PitchVal = 0
                        if Pitches and #Pitches >= 12 then
                            if i == 1 then
                                PitchVal = ((Pitches[1] or 0) + (Pitches[2] or 0)) * 0.5
                            elseif i == 2 then
                                PitchVal = ((Pitches[3] or 0) + (Pitches[4] or 0)) * 0.5
                            elseif i == 3 then
                                PitchVal = ((Pitches[5] or 0) + (Pitches[6] or 0)) * 0.5
                            elseif i == 4 then
                                PitchVal = ((Pitches[7] or 0) + (Pitches[8] or 0)) * 0.5
                            elseif i == 5 then
                                PitchVal = ((Pitches[9] or 0) + (Pitches[10] or 0)) * 0.5
                            elseif i == 6 then
                                PitchVal = ((Pitches[11] or 0) + (Pitches[12] or 0)) * 0.5
                            end
                        end

                        local SynthBase
                        local SynthAmp
                        local Phase

                        if i <= 2 then
                            Phase = State.BassPhase + i * 0.5
                            SynthBase = 2
                            SynthAmp = 8 * EnergyMul
                        elseif i <= 4 then
                            Phase = State.MidPhase + i * 0.3
                            SynthBase = 2
                            SynthAmp = 6 * DanceMul
                        else
                            Phase = State.TreblePhase + i * 0.2
                            SynthBase = 2
                            SynthAmp = 5 * (State.Energy * 0.5 + 0.5)
                        end

                        local SynthNoise = math.abs(math.sin(Phase)) * 0.4 + 0.6
                        local Noise2 = math.abs(math.sin(Phase * 2.3 + i * 1.7)) * 0.2 + 0.8
                        local Combined = SynthNoise * Noise2

                        local PitchHeight = 0
                        if PitchVal > 0 then
                            PitchHeight = PitchVal * 8 * LoudnessNorm
                        end

                        local BeatHeight = 0
                        if i <= 2 then
                            BeatHeight = BeatBoost
                        end

                        local SynthHeight = SynthAmp * Combined

                        local Height = SynthBase + SynthHeight + PitchHeight + BeatHeight
                        Height = MathClamp(Height, 2, 14)
                        Bar.Instance.Size = UDim2New(0, 4, 0, Height)
                    end
                else
                    State.LastFrameTime = 0
                end

                UpdateLyrics()

                task.wait(0.033)
            end
        end)

        Library.MusicPlayerInstance = MusicPlayer
        MusicPlayer.Items = Items

        local TokenFile = Library.Folders.Configs .. "/spotify_token.json"

        Library.LoadSpotifyToken = function()
            if not isfile(TokenFile) then
                return
            end
            local Success, Content = pcall(readfile, TokenFile)
            if not Success or not Content then
                return
            end
            local ParseSuccess, Data = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(Content)
            end)
            if not ParseSuccess or type(Data) ~= "table" or not Data.token then
                pcall(delfile, TokenFile)
                return
            end
            Library.SpotifyToken = Data.token
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(1)
                if Library.MusicPlayerInstance then
                    Library.MusicPlayerInstance:Refresh()
                end
            end)
        end

        Library.SaveSpotifyToken = function(Token)
            if not Token or Token == "" then
                if isfile(TokenFile) then
                    pcall(delfile, TokenFile)
                end
                return
            end
            local Data = { token = Token }
            pcall(function()
                writefile(TokenFile, HttpService:JSONEncode(Data))
            end)
        end

        Library.DeleteSpotifyToken = function()
            if isfile(TokenFile) then
                pcall(delfile, TokenFile)
            end
            Library.SpotifyToken = ""
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(2)
            Library.LoadSpotifyToken()
        end)

        function MusicPlayer:SetVisibility(Bool)
            Items["Window"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end

        function MusicPlayer:Refresh()
            ShowDebug = true
            UpdateFromSpotify()
        end

        return MusicPlayer
    end

    Library.ESPPreview = function(self)
        local ESPPreview = {
            Config = {
                ShowBox = true,
                ShowName = true,
                ShowHealth = true,
                ShowDistance = false,
                BoxThickness = 1,
                NameSize = 12,
                UseStatusColors = true,
                Enabled = false
            },
            ESPObjects = { },
            ViewportRig = nil
        }

        local StatusColors = {
            Local = FromRGB(100, 160, 255),
            Neutral = FromRGB(180, 180, 185),
            Friendly = FromRGB(95, 217, 122),
            Enemy = FromRGB(220, 90, 90)
        }

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Window"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Library.Holder.Instance,
                Size = UDim2New(0, 200, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 500, 0, 100),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,

            })
            Items["Window"]:AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Inline", BorderColor3 = "Outline" })
            Library.ESPPreviewFrame = Items["Window"].Instance

            local ESPStroke = Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Window"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(235, 157, 255)

            Items["AccentLine"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Window"].Instance,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(167, 139, 250),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, -2),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 2),
                ZIndex = 2
            })
            Items["AccentLine"]:AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Accent" })

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["AccentLine"].Instance,
                Rotation = 0,
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
                })
            })

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["Window"].Instance,
                PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 8),
                PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 8),
                PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 8),
                PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 8)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["Window"].Instance,
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
                Padding = UDimNew(0, 6)
            })

            Items["Title"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Items["Window"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                Text = "ESP Preview",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 14),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })
            Items["Title"]:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })

            Items["Title"].Instance.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
                local MainFrame = Items["Window"].Instance
                local StartPos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                local StartFrame = MainFrame.Position

                local MoveConn
                MoveConn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                        local MousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                        local DeltaX = MousePos.X - StartPos.X
                        local DeltaY = MousePos.Y - StartPos.Y
                        MainFrame.Position = UDim2New(
                            StartFrame.X.Scale, StartFrame.X.Offset + DeltaX,
                            StartFrame.Y.Scale, StartFrame.Y.Offset + DeltaY
                        )
                    end
                end)

                local EndConn
                EndConn = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                        MoveConn:Disconnect()
                        EndConn:Disconnect()
                    end
                end)
            end)

            Items["Viewport"] = Instances:Create("ViewportFrame", {
                Parent = Items["Window"].Instance,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(30, 30, 36),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.6,
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                BorderSizePixel = 1,

        local ViewportInstance = Items["Viewport"].Instance

        local VpCamera = InstanceNew("Camera")
        VpCamera.Parent = ViewportInstance
        VpCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0, 5.5, 6), Vector3.new(0, 5.5, 0))
        VpCamera.FieldOfView = 50
        ViewportInstance.CurrentCamera = VpCamera

        local CamAngle = 0
        local CamPitch = 0.2
        local CamDist = 7
        local Dragging = false
        local LastMouseX = 0
        local LastMouseY = 0

        local GetAdjustedMousePos = function()
            local MousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            local GuiInset = game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset()
            return Vector2New(MousePos.X, MousePos.Y - GuiInset.Y)
        end

        Library:Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                if Items["Window"].Instance.Visible then
                    local MousePos = GetAdjustedMousePos()
                    local VPPos = ViewportInstance.AbsolutePosition
                    local VPSize = ViewportInstance.AbsoluteSize
                    local InVP = MousePos.X >= VPPos.X and MousePos.X <= VPPos.X + VPSize.X
                        and MousePos.Y >= VPPos.Y and MousePos.Y <= VPPos.Y + VPSize.Y
                    if InVP then
                        Dragging = true
                        LastMouseX = MousePos.X
                        LastMouseY = MousePos.Y
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        Library:Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                Dragging = false
            end
        end)

        Library:Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
            if Dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                local MousePos = GetAdjustedMousePos()
                local DX = MousePos.X - LastMouseX
                local DY = MousePos.Y - LastMouseY
                CamAngle = CamAngle - DX * 0.01
                CamPitch = math.clamp(CamPitch + DY * 0.01, -0.5, 0.8)
                LastMouseX = MousePos.X
                LastMouseY = MousePos.Y
            end
        end)

        Library:Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
                if Items["Window"].Instance.Visible then
                    local MousePos = GetAdjustedMousePos()
                    local VPPos = ViewportInstance.AbsolutePosition
                    local VPSize = ViewportInstance.AbsoluteSize
                    local InVP = MousePos.X >= VPPos.X and MousePos.X <= VPPos.X + VPSize.X
                        and MousePos.Y >= VPPos.Y and MousePos.Y <= VPPos.Y + VPSize.Y
                    if InVP then
                        CamDist = math.clamp(CamDist - input.Position.Z, 4, 15)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        local function UpdateCamera()
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            local x = math.sin(CamAngle) * CamDist * math.cos(CamPitch)
            local z = math.cos(CamAngle) * CamDist * math.cos(CamPitch)
            local y = 5.5 + math.sin(CamPitch) * CamDist
            VpCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(x, y, z), Vector3.new(0, 5.5, 0))
        end

        local function CreateR6Dummy()
            local Model = InstanceNew("Model")
            Model.Name = "ESPDummy"

            local Humanoid = InstanceNew("Humanoid")
            Humanoid.RigType = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6
            Humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
            Humanoid.NameDisplayDistance = 0
            Humanoid.HealthDisplayDistance = 0
            Humanoid.Parent = Model

            local GrayColor = FromRGB(163, 162, 165)

            local function CreatePart(Name, Size)
                local Part = InstanceNew("Part")
                Part.Name = Name
                Part.Size = Size
                Part.Color = GrayColor
                Part.Anchored = false
                Part.CanCollide = false
                Part.Parent = Model
                return Part
            end

            local Head = CreatePart("Head", Vector3.new(2, 1, 1))
            local Torso = CreatePart("Torso", Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
            local LeftArm = CreatePart("Left Arm", Vector3.new(1, 2, 1))
            local RightArm = CreatePart("Right Arm", Vector3.new(1, 2, 1))
            local LeftLeg = CreatePart("Left Leg", Vector3.new(1, 2, 1))
            local RightLeg = CreatePart("Right Leg", Vector3.new(1, 2, 1))

            Torso.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 6, 0)
            Head.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 7.5, 0)
            LeftArm.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1.5, 6, 0)
            RightArm.CFrame = CFrame.new(1.5, 6, 0)
            LeftLeg.CFrame = CFrame.new(-0.5, 4, 0)
            RightLeg.CFrame = CFrame.new(0.5, 4, 0)

            Head.Anchored = true
            Torso.Anchored = true
            LeftArm.Anchored = true
            RightArm.Anchored = true
            LeftLeg.Anchored = true
            RightLeg.Anchored = true

            Model.PrimaryPart = Torso

            local HeadMesh = InstanceNew("SpecialMesh")
            HeadMesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Head
            HeadMesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1.25, 1.25, 1.25)
            HeadMesh.Parent = Head

            local Face = InstanceNew("Decal")
            Face.Name = "face"
            Face.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/face.png"
            Face.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
            Face.Parent = Head

            local function CreateMotor6D(Name, Part0, Part1, C0, C1)
                local M = InstanceNew("Motor6D")
                M.Name = Name
                M.Part0 = Part0
                M.Part1 = Part1
                M.C0 = C0
                M.C1 = C1
                M.Parent = Part0
                return M
            end

            CreateMotor6D("Neck", Torso, Head,
                CFrame.new(0, 1, 0) * CFrame.Angles(-math.pi/2, 0, 0),
                CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(-math.pi/2, 0, 0))

            CreateMotor6D("Left Shoulder", Torso, LeftArm,
                CFrame.new(-1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, -math.pi/2, 0),
                CFrame.new(0.5, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, -math.pi/2, 0))

            CreateMotor6D("Right Shoulder", Torso, RightArm,
                CFrame.new(1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi/2, 0),
                CFrame.new(-0.5, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi/2, 0))

            CreateMotor6D("Left Hip", Torso, LeftLeg,
                CFrame.new(-1, -1, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, -math.pi/2, 0),
                CFrame.new(-0.5, 1, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, -math.pi/2, 0))

            CreateMotor6D("Right Hip", Torso, RightLeg,
                CFrame.new(1, -1, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi/2, 0),
                CFrame.new(0.5, 1, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi/2, 0))

            local Animator = InstanceNew("Animator")
            Animator.Parent = Humanoid

            Model.Parent = ViewportInstance

            local DanceTrack = nil

            local LoadDance = function()
                pcall(function()
                    local DanceAnim = InstanceNew("Animation")
                    DanceAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://182435998"
                    local Success, Track = pcall(function()
                        return Animator:LoadAnimation(DanceAnim)
                    end)
                    if Success and Track then
                        DanceTrack = Track
                        DanceTrack.Looped = true
                        DanceTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
                        DanceTrack:Play(0.1)
                    end
                end)

                if not DanceTrack then
                    pcall(function()
                        local DanceAnim = InstanceNew("Animation")
                        DanceAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://182435998"
                        local Success, Track = pcall(function()
                            return Humanoid:LoadAnimation(DanceAnim)
                        end)
                        if Success and Track then
                            DanceTrack = Track
                            DanceTrack.Looped = true
                            DanceTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
                            DanceTrack:Play(0.1)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            LoadDance()

            task.delay(0.5, function()
                if not DanceTrack or (DanceTrack and not DanceTrack.IsPlaying) then
                    LoadDance()
                end
            end)

            task.delay(2, function()
                if not DanceTrack or (DanceTrack and not DanceTrack.IsPlaying) then
                    LoadDance()
                end
            end)

            return Model, Humanoid, {
                Head = Head,
                Torso = Torso,
                LeftArm = LeftArm,
                RightArm = RightArm,
                LeftLeg = LeftLeg,
                RightLeg = RightLeg,
                Animator = Animator,
                DanceTrack = DanceTrack,
                ReloadAnimation = LoadDance
            }
        end

        local DummyModel, DummyHumanoid, DummyParts = CreateR6Dummy()
        ESPPreview.ViewportRig = DummyModel

        local GetDrawingAPI = function()
            if Drawing then return Drawing end
            if drawing then return drawing end
            return nil
        end

        local DrawingAPI = GetDrawingAPI()

        local ProjectPointViewport = function(worldPos, cam, vpWidth, vpHeight)
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            local rel = cam.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(worldPos)
            if rel.Z >= -0.1 then
                return nil, false
            end

            local halfFovRad = math.rad(cam.FieldOfView) / 2
            local tanHalfFov = math.tan(halfFovRad)
            local aspect = vpWidth / vpHeight

            local ndcX = (rel.X / -rel.Z) / (tanHalfFov * aspect)
            local ndcY = (rel.Y / -rel.Z) / tanHalfFov

            local screenX = (ndcX + 1) * 0.5 * vpWidth
            local screenY = (1 - ndcY) * 0.5 * vpHeight

            return Vector2New(screenX, screenY), true
        end

        local ProjectPointScreen = function(worldPos, cam)
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            local screenPos, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(worldPos)
            if not onScreen then
                return nil, false
            end
            local GuiInset = game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset()
            return Vector2New(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y - GuiInset.Y), true
        end

        local GetBoundingBox2D = function(model, cam, vpWidth, vpHeight, useViewport)
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            local corners = {}
            for _, part in model:GetDescendants() do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local s = part.Size / 2
                    local cf = part.CFrame
                    table.insert(corners, cf * Vector3.new(s.X, s.Y, s.Z))
                    table.insert(corners, cf * Vector3.new(-s.X, s.Y, s.Z))
                    table.insert(corners, cf * Vector3.new(s.X, -s.Y, s.Z))
                    table.insert(corners, cf * Vector3.new(-s.X, -s.Y, s.Z))
                    table.insert(corners, cf * Vector3.new(s.X, s.Y, -s.Z))
                    table.insert(corners, cf * Vector3.new(-s.X, s.Y, -s.Z))
                    table.insert(corners, cf * Vector3.new(s.X, -s.Y, -s.Z))
                    table.insert(corners, cf * Vector3.new(-s.X, -s.Y, -s.Z))
                end
            end
            if #corners == 0 then return nil end

            local minX, minY = math.huge, math.huge
            local maxX, maxY = -math.huge, -math.huge
            local anyInFront = false

            for _, c in corners do
                local rel = cam.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(c)
                if rel.Z < -0.1 then
                    anyInFront = true
                    local sp
                    if useViewport then
                        sp = ProjectPointViewport(c, cam, vpWidth, vpHeight)
                    else
                        sp = ProjectPointScreen(c, cam)
                    end
                    if sp then
                        minX = math.min(minX, sp.X)
                        minY = math.min(minY, sp.Y)
                        maxX = math.max(maxX, sp.X)
                        maxY = math.max(maxY, sp.Y)
                    end
                end
            end

            if not anyInFront then return nil end
            if minX == math.huge then return nil end

            return { MinX = minX, MinY = minY, MaxX = maxX, MaxY = maxY }
        end



        local PreviewStatus = "Neutral"
        local DummyHolder = InstanceNew("Frame")
        DummyHolder.Name = "\0"
        DummyHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        DummyHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0)

        DummyBoxHandler.BorderSizePixel = 0
        DummyBoxHandler.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        DummyBoxHandler.ZIndex = 10
        DummyBoxHandler.Parent = DummyHolder

        local DummyBoxColor = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
        DummyBoxColor.Color = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        DummyBoxColor.Parent = DummyBoxHandler

        local DummyBoxOutline = InstanceNew("Frame")
        DummyBoxOutline.Name = "\0"
        DummyBoxOutline.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        DummyBoxOutline.Position = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1)
        DummyBoxOutline.Size = UDim2New(1, -2, 1, -2)
        DummyBoxOutline.BorderSizePixel = 0
        DummyBoxOutline.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        DummyBoxOutline.ZIndex = 9

        local DummyBoxOutlineStroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
        DummyBoxOutlineStroke.Color = FromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        DummyBoxOutlineStroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
        DummyBoxOutlineStroke.Parent = DummyBoxOutline

        local DummyCorners = InstanceNew("Frame")
        DummyCorners.Name = "\0"
        DummyCorners.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        DummyCorners.Position = UDim2New(0, -1, 0, 2)
        DummyCorners.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0)
        DummyCorners.BorderSizePixel = 0
        DummyCorners.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        DummyCorners.ZIndex = 11
        DummyCorners.Parent = DummyHolder

        local MakeCornerPiece = function(anchorPoint, position, size, rotation)
            local Bg = InstanceNew("Frame")
            Bg.Name = "line"
            Bg.BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            Bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            Bg.Rotation = rotation or 0
            Bg.Parent = DummyCorners
            Bg.AnchorPoint = anchorPoint
            Bg.Position = position
            Bg.Size = size
            Bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
            Bg.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0)

            local Fill = InstanceNew("Frame")
            Fill.Position = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1)
            Fill.Size = UDim2New(1, -2, 1, -2)
            Fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
            Fill.Parent = Bg
            return Fill
        end

        local TLH = MakeCornerPiece(Vector2New(0, 0), UDim2New(0, 0, 0, -2), UDim2New(0, 8, 0, 3))
        local TLV = MakeCornerPiece(Vector2New(0, 0), UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 1), UDim2New(0, 3, 0, 8))
        local TRH = MakeCornerPiece(Vector2New(1, 0), UDim2New(1, 0, 0, -2), UDim2New(0, 8, 0, 3))
        local TRV = MakeCornerPiece(Vector2New(1, 0), UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 1), UDim2New(0, 3, 0, 8))
        local BLH = MakeCornerPiece(Vector2New(0, 1), UDim2New(0, -1, 1, -2), UDim2New(0, 8, 0, 3))
        local BLV = MakeCornerPiece(Vector2New(0, 1), UDim2New(0, 0, 1, -4), UDim2New(0, 3, 0, 8), 180)
        local BRH = MakeCornerPiece(Vector2New(1, 1), UDim2New(1, -1, 1, -2), UDim2New(0, 8, 0, 3))
        local BRV = MakeCornerPiece(Vector2New(1, 1), UDim2New(1, 0, 1, -4), UDim2New(0, 3, 0, 8), 180)

        local CornerFills = { TLH, TLV, TRH, TRV, BLH, BLV, BRH, BRV }

        local DummyHpBg = InstanceNew("Frame")
        DummyHpBg.Name = "\0"
        DummyHpBg.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0)
        DummyHpBg.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        DummyHpBg.Position = UDim2New(0, -5, 0, 0)
        DummyHpBg.Size = UDim2New(0, 4, 1, 0)
        DummyHpBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
        DummyHpBg.ZIndex = 11
        DummyHpBg.Parent = DummyHolder

        local DummyHpFill = InstanceNew("Frame")
        DummyHpFill.Name = "\0"
        DummyHpFill.Position = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1)
        DummyHpFill.Size = UDim2New(1, -2, 1, -2)
        DummyHpFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        DummyHpFill.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(95, 217, 122)
        DummyHpFill.ZIndex = 12
        DummyHpFill.Parent = DummyHpBg

        local function DrawDummyESP()
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            if not Items["Window"].Instance.Visible then
                DummyHolder.Visible = false
                return
            end

            local VPSize = ViewportInstance.AbsoluteSize
            local box = GetBoundingBox2D(DummyModel, VpCamera, VPSize.X, VPSize.Y, true)
            if not box then
                DummyHolder.Visible = false
                return
            end

            local BoxW = box.MaxX - box.MinX
            local BoxH = box.MaxY - box.MinY
            DummyHolder.Position = UDim2New(0, box.MinX, 0, box.MinY)
            DummyHolder.Size = UDim2New(0, BoxW, 0, BoxH)
            DummyHolder.Visible = true

            local Status = PreviewStatus
            local Color = StatusColors[Status] or StatusColors.Neutral
            if ESPPreview.Config.UseStatusColors == false then
                Color = Library.Theme.Accent
            end

            if ESPPreview.Config.ShowName then
                local text = Status == "Local" and "LocalPlayer" or Status
                DummyNameLabel.Text = text
                DummyNameLabel.TextColor3 = Color
                DummyNameLabel.Visible = true
            else
                DummyNameLabel.Visible = false
            end

            if ESPPreview.Config.ShowBox then
                DummyBoxHandler.Visible = true
                DummyBoxOutline.Visible = true
                DummyBoxColor.Color = Color
                DummyBoxColor.Thickness = 1
                DummyBoxOutlineStroke.Thickness = 3
                DummyCorners.Visible = false
            else
                DummyBoxHandler.Visible = false
                DummyBoxOutline.Visible = false
                DummyCorners.Visible = false
            end

            if ESPPreview.Config.ShowHealth then
                local humanoid = DummyHumanoid
                local ratio = 1
                if humanoid then
                    ratio = MathClamp(humanoid.Health / humanoid.MaxHealth, 0, 1)
                end
                DummyHpFill.Size = UDim2New(1, -2, ratio, -2)
                DummyHpFill.Position = UDim2New(0, 1, 1 - ratio, 1)
                if ratio > 0.5 then
                    DummyHpFill.BackgroundColor3 = StatusColors.Friendly
                elseif ratio > 0.25 then
                    DummyHpFill.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 200, 50)
                else
                    DummyHpFill.BackgroundColor3 = StatusColors.Enemy
                end
                DummyHpBg.Visible = true
            else
                DummyHpBg.Visible = false
            end
        end

        local PlayerOverlays = {}

        local function ClearPlayerOverlay(player)
            local overlay = PlayerOverlays[player]
            if overlay then
                if overlay.Holder then overlay.Holder.Parent = nil end
                pcall(function()
                    if overlay.BoxHandler then overlay.BoxHandler:Destroy() end
                    if overlay.BoxOutline then overlay.BoxOutline:Destroy() end
                    if overlay.NameLabel then overlay.NameLabel:Destroy() end
                    if overlay.HpBg then overlay.HpBg:Destroy() end
                    if overlay.HpFill then overlay.HpFill:Destroy() end
                    if overlay.Holder then overlay.Holder:Destroy() end
                end)
            end
            PlayerOverlays[player] = nil
        end

        local function ClearAllPlayerOverlays()
            for p, _ in PlayerOverlays do ClearPlayerOverlay(p) end
        end

        local function EnsurePlayerOverlay(player)
            if PlayerOverlays[player] then return PlayerOverlays[player] end

            local overlay = {}

            overlay.Holder = InstanceNew("Frame")
            overlay.Holder.Name = "\0"
            overlay.Holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            overlay.Holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
            overlay.Holder.Visible = false
            overlay.Holder.ZIndex = 50
            overlay.Holder.Parent = Library.Holder.Instance

            overlay.BoxOutline = InstanceNew("Frame")
            overlay.BoxOutline.Name = "\0"
            overlay.BoxOutline.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            overlay.BoxOutline.Position = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1)
            overlay.BoxOutline.Size = UDim2New(1, -2, 1, -2)
            overlay.BoxOutline.BorderSizePixel = 0
            overlay.BoxOutline.ZIndex = 49
            overlay.BoxOutline.Parent = overlay.Holder

            local OutlineStroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
            OutlineStroke.Color = FromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            OutlineStroke.Thickness = 3
            OutlineStroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
            OutlineStroke.Parent = overlay.BoxOutline

            overlay.BoxHandler = InstanceNew("Frame")
            overlay.BoxHandler.Name = "\0"
            overlay.BoxHandler.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            overlay.BoxHandler.Position = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1)
            overlay.BoxHandler.Size = UDim2New(1, -2, 1, -2)
            overlay.BoxHandler.BorderSizePixel = 0
            overlay.BoxHandler.ZIndex = 50
            overlay.BoxHandler.Parent = overlay.Holder

            overlay.BoxStroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
            overlay.BoxStroke.Color = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            overlay.BoxStroke.Thickness = 1
            overlay.BoxStroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
            overlay.BoxStroke.Parent = overlay.BoxHandler

            overlay.NameLabel = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
            overlay.NameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            overlay.NameLabel.FontFace = Library.Font
            overlay.NameLabel.TextColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            overlay.NameLabel.Text = ""
            overlay.NameLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0.5, 1)
            overlay.NameLabel.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0)
            overlay.NameLabel.Position = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 0, -5)
            overlay.NameLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
            overlay.NameLabel.TextSize = 12
            overlay.NameLabel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            overlay.NameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            overlay.NameLabel.ZIndex = 51
            overlay.NameLabel.Parent = overlay.Holder

            overlay.HpBg = InstanceNew("Frame")
            overlay.HpBg.Name = "\0"
            overlay.HpBg.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0)
            overlay.HpBg.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            overlay.HpBg.Position = UDim2New(0, -5, 0, 0)
            overlay.HpBg.Size = UDim2New(0, 4, 1, 0)
            overlay.HpBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
            overlay.HpBg.ZIndex = 51
            overlay.HpBg.Parent = overlay.Holder

            overlay.HpFill = InstanceNew("Frame")
            overlay.HpFill.Name = "\0"
            overlay.HpFill.Position = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1)
            overlay.HpFill.Size = UDim2New(1, -2, 1, -2)
            overlay.HpFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
            overlay.HpFill.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(95, 217, 122)
            overlay.HpFill.ZIndex = 52
            overlay.HpFill.Parent = overlay.HpBg

            PlayerOverlays[player] = overlay
            return overlay
        end

        local function DrawPlayerESP(player)
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            if not ESPPreview.Config.Enabled then
                ClearPlayerOverlay(player)
                return
            end

            local Char = player.Character
            if not Char then
                ClearPlayerOverlay(player)
                return
            end

            local Hum = Char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not Hum then
                ClearPlayerOverlay(player)
                return
            end

            local overlay = EnsurePlayerOverlay(player)

            local box = GetBoundingBox2D(Char, Camera, 0, 0, false)
            if not box then
                overlay.Holder.Visible = false
                return
            end

            local BoxW = box.MaxX - box.MinX
            local BoxH = box.MaxY - box.MinY
            overlay.Holder.Position = UDim2New(0, box.MinX, 0, box.MinY)
            overlay.Holder.Size = UDim2New(0, BoxW, 0, BoxH)
            overlay.Holder.Visible = true

            local Status = "Neutral"
            if player == LocalPlayer then
                Status = "Local"
            elseif Library.PlayerlistInstance then
                local S = Library.PlayerlistInstance:GetStatus(player)
                if S and S ~= "" then
                    Status = S
                end
            end

            local Color = StatusColors[Status] or StatusColors.Neutral
            if ESPPreview.Config.UseStatusColors == false then
                Color = Library.Theme.Accent
            end

            if ESPPreview.Config.ShowBox then
                overlay.BoxHandler.Visible = true
                overlay.BoxOutline.Visible = true
                overlay.BoxStroke.Color = Color
                overlay.BoxStroke.Thickness = 1
            else
                overlay.BoxHandler.Visible = false
                overlay.BoxOutline.Visible = false
            end

            if ESPPreview.Config.ShowName then
                overlay.NameLabel.Text = player.DisplayName
                overlay.NameLabel.TextColor3 = Color
                overlay.NameLabel.Visible = true
            else
                overlay.NameLabel.Visible = false
            end

            if ESPPreview.Config.ShowHealth then
                local hRatio = Hum.MaxHealth > 0 and MathClamp(Hum.Health / Hum.MaxHealth, 0, 1) or 0
                overlay.HpFill.Size = UDim2New(1, -2, hRatio, -2)
                overlay.HpFill.Position = UDim2New(0, 1, 1 - hRatio, 1)
                if hRatio > 0.5 then
                    overlay.HpFill.BackgroundColor3 = StatusColors.Friendly
                elseif hRatio > 0.25 then
                    overlay.HpFill.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 200, 50)
                else
                    overlay.HpFill.BackgroundColor3 = StatusColors.Enemy
                end
                overlay.HpBg.Visible = true
            else
                overlay.HpBg.Visible = false
            end
        end

        function ESPPreview:SetPreviewStatus(status)
            PreviewStatus = status
        end

        function ESPPreview:SetConfig(key, value)
            ESPPreview.Config[key] = value
        end

        function ESPPreview:SetVisibility(bool)
            Items["Window"].Instance.Visible = bool
        end

        function ESPPreview:SetEnabled(bool)
            ESPPreview.Config.Enabled = bool
            if not bool then ClearAllPlayerOverlays() end
        end

        Library:Connect(Players.PlayerAdded, function(player)
            if ESPPreview.Config.Enabled then
                task.wait(1)
                DrawPlayerESP(player)
            end
        end)

        Library:Connect(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(player)
            ClearPlayerOverlay(player)
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            local AnimationCheckTimer = 0
            while true do
                pcall(DrawDummyESP)

                AnimationCheckTimer = AnimationCheckTimer + 0.03
                if AnimationCheckTimer >= 3 then
                    AnimationCheckTimer = 0
                    pcall(function()
                        if DummyParts and DummyParts.DanceTrack then
                            if not DummyParts.DanceTrack.IsPlaying then
                                DummyParts.DanceTrack:Play(0.1)
                            end
                        elseif DummyParts and DummyParts.ReloadAnimation then
                            DummyParts.ReloadAnimation()
                        end
                    end)
                end

                if ESPPreview.Config.Enabled then
                    for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
                        if player ~= LocalPlayer then
                            pcall(DrawPlayerESP, player)
                        end
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.03)
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                if not Dragging then
                    CamAngle = CamAngle + 0.005
                end
                if VpCamera and VpCamera.Parent then
                    UpdateCamera()
                end
                task.wait(0.016)
            end
        end)

        ESPPreview.Items = Items
        ESPPreview.StatusColors = StatusColors

        Library.ESPPreviewInstance = ESPPreview

        return ESPPreview
    end
