        local FormatInstanceName = function(Instance)
            if not Instance then
                return "nil", "nil"
            end
            return Instance.Name or "?", Instance.ClassName or "?"
        end

        local GetInstanceValue = function(Instance)
            local Success, Value = pcall(function()
                return Instance.Value
            end)

            if not Success or Value == nil then
                return nil
            end

            if typeof(Value) == "Instance" then
                return Value.Name and (Value.Name .. " [" .. (Value.ClassName or "?") .. "]") or "nil"
            elseif typeof(Value) == "EnumItem" then
                return tostring(Value)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "Color3" then
                return StringFormat("R:%d G:%d B:%d", Value.R * 255, Value.G * 255, Value.B * 255)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "Vector3" then
                return StringFormat("%.2f, %.2f, %.2f", Value.X, Value.Y, Value.Z)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "CFrame" then
                return tostring(Value)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "BrickColor" then
                return tostring(Value)
            end

            return tostring(Value)
        end

        local GetInstancePath = function(Instance)
            if not Instance then
                return ""
            end

            local Segments = { }
            local Current = Instance

            while Current and Current ~= game do
                local Name = Current.Name or "?"
                if Name == "" then
                    Name = "?"
                end
                TableInsert(Segments, 1, Name)
                Current = Current.Parent
            end

            if #Segments == 0 then
                return "game"
            end

            return "game." .. TableConcat(Segments, ".")
        end

        local ClearTree = function()
            for _, Entry in RowEntries do
                if Entry.Button and Entry.Button.Instance then
                    Entry.Button:Clean()
                end
            end
            RowEntries = { }
        end

        local ClearProps = function()
            for _, Entry in PropEntries do
                if Entry.Frame and Entry.Frame.Instance then
                    Entry.Frame:Clean()
                end
            end
            PropEntries = { }
        end

        local UpdateCanvasSize = function(ScrollKey)
            local Layout = Items[ScrollKey].Instance:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
            if Layout then
                Items[ScrollKey].Instance.CanvasSize = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, Layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y)
            end
        end

        local SafeProp = function(Instance, PropName)
            local Ok, Val = pcall(function()
                return Instance[PropName]
            end)
            if not Ok or Val == nil then
                return nil
            end
            return Val
        end

        local FormatPropValue = function(Value)
            if Value == nil then
                return "nil"
            end

            if typeof(Value) == "Instance" then
                return StringFormat("%s [%s]", Value.Name or "?", Value.ClassName or "?")
            elseif typeof(Value) == "EnumItem" then
                return tostring(Value)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "Color3" then
                return StringFormat("%d, %d, %d", Value.R * 255, Value.G * 255, Value.B * 255)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "Vector3" then
                return StringFormat("%.2f, %.2f, %.2f", Value.X, Value.Y, Value.Z)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "Vector2" then
                return StringFormat("%.2f, %.2f", Value.X, Value.Y)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "CFrame" then
                local X, Y, Z = Value.X, Value.Y, Value.Z
                return StringFormat("%.1f, %.1f, %.1f", X, Y, Z)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "UDim2" then
                return StringFormat("{%d, %d}, {%d, %d}", Value.X.Scale, Value.X.Offset, Value.Y.Scale, Value.Y.Offset)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "UDim" then
                return StringFormat("{%d, %d}", Value.Scale, Value.Offset)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "BrickColor" then
                return tostring(Value)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "number" then
                return tostring(Value)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "boolean" then
                return tostring(Value)
            elseif typeof(Value) == "string" then
                if #Value > 60 then
                    return Value:sub(1, 57) .. "..."
                end
                return Value
            elseif typeof(Value) == "PhysicalProperties" then
                return "PhysicalProperties"
            elseif typeof(Value) == "Font" then
                return Value.Family or "Font"
            end

            return tostring(Value)
        end

        local CollectProperties = function(Instance)
            local Properties = { }

            local Name, Class = FormatInstanceName(Instance)

            TableInsert(Properties, { "Name", Name })
            TableInsert(Properties, { "ClassName", Class })
            TableInsert(Properties, { "Archivable", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Archivable") or true) })
            TableInsert(Properties, { "Parent", Instance.Parent and StringFormat("%s [%s]", Instance.Parent.Name or "?", Instance.Parent.ClassName or "?") or "nil" })

            local ChildCount = 0
            local Ok, Kids = pcall(function() return Instance:GetChildren() end)
            if Ok and Kids then
                ChildCount = #Kids
            end
            TableInsert(Properties, { "Children", tostring(ChildCount) })

            local DescCount = 0
            local Ok2, Descs = pcall(function() return Instance:GetDescendants() end)
            if Ok2 and Descs then
                DescCount = #Descs
            end
            TableInsert(Properties, { "Descendants", tostring(DescCount) })

            local Value = GetInstanceValue(Instance)
            if Value ~= nil then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Value", Value })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("BasePart") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Position", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Position")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Size", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Size")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Material", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Material") or Enum.Material.Plastic) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Color", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Color")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Transparency", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Transparency") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Anchored", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Anchored") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CanCollide", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "CanCollide") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CanTouch", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "CanTouch") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CanQuery", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "CanQuery") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CastShadow", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "CastShadow") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CollisionGroupId", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "CollisionGroupId") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Locked", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Locked") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "BackSurface", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "BackSurface") or Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "FrontSurface", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "FrontSurface") or Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Reflectance", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Reflectance") or 0) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("Model") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "PrimaryPart", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "PrimaryPart")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "WorldPivot", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "WorldPivot")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Scale", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Scale") or 1) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("GuiObject") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Position", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Position")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Size", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Size")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "AnchorPoint", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "AnchorPoint")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Rotation", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Rotation") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Visible", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Visible") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ZIndex", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ZIndex") or 1) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Transparency", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "BackgroundTransparency") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ClipsDescendants", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ClipsDescendants") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Active", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Active") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "AutoButtonColor", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "AutoButtonColor") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "LayoutOrder", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "LayoutOrder") or 0) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("TextLabel") or Instance:IsA("TextButton") or Instance:IsA("TextBox") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Text", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Text")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "TextSize", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "TextSize") or 12) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "TextColor3", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "TextColor3")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "TextXAlignment", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "TextXAlignment") or Enum.TextXAlignment.Center) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "TextYAlignment", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "TextYAlignment") or Enum.TextYAlignment.Center) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "TextWrapped", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "TextWrapped") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "TextTruncate", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "TextTruncate") or Enum.TextTruncate.None) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "RichText", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "RichText") or false) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("ImageLabel") or Instance:IsA("ImageButton") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Image", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Image")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ImageColor3", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "ImageColor3")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ImageTransparency", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ImageTransparency") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ScaleType", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ScaleType") or Enum.ScaleType.Stretch) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CanvasSize", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "CanvasSize")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ScrollBarThickness", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ScrollBarThickness") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ScrollBarImageTransparency", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ScrollBarImageTransparency") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "AutomaticCanvasSize", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "AutomaticCanvasSize") or Enum.AutomaticSize.None) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ScrollingDirection", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ScrollingDirection") or Enum.ScrollingDirection.XY) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ScrollingEnabled", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ScrollingEnabled") or true) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("BaseScript") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Enabled", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Enabled") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "RunContext", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "RunContext") or Enum.RunContext.Legacy) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "LinkedSource", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "LinkedSource")) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("LocalScript") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "RunContext", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "RunContext") or Enum.RunContext.Client) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "LinkedSource", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "LinkedSource")) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("Camera") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CFrame", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "CFrame")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "FieldOfView", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "FieldOfView") or 70) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CameraType", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "CameraType") or Enum.CameraType.Fixed) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Focus", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Focus")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "HeadLocked", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "HeadLocked") or true) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("Humanoid") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Health", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Health") or 100) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "MaxHealth", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "MaxHealth") or 100) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "WalkSpeed", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "WalkSpeed") or 16) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "JumpPower", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "JumpPower") or 50) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "HipHeight", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "HipHeight") or 0) })

                TableInsert(Properties, { "AutoRotate", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "AutoRotate") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "PlatformStand", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "PlatformStand") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "RigType", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "RigType") or Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("Player") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "DisplayName", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "DisplayName")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "UserId", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "UserId") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "AccountAge", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "AccountAge") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Team", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Team")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Neutral", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Neutral") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Character", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Character")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CharacterAppearanceId", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "CharacterAppearanceId")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "GameplayPaused", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "GameplayPaused") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CameraMaxZoomDistance", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "CameraMaxZoomDistance") or 128) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CameraMinZoomDistance", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "CameraMinZoomDistance") or 0.5) })
            end

            for _, ValueClass in { "IntValue", "NumberValue", "StringValue", "BoolValue", "ObjectValue", "CFrameValue", "Color3Value", "Vector3Value", "BrickColorValue", "IntConstrainedValue", "DoubleConstrainedValue" } do
                if Instance:IsA(ValueClass) then
                    TableInsert(Properties, { "Value", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Value")) })
                    break
                end
            end

            if Instance:IsA("Folder") then
            end

            if Instance:IsA("Workspace") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "StreamingEnabled", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "StreamingEnabled") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "StreamingMinRadius", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "StreamingMinRadius") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "StreamingTargetRadius", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "StreamingTargetRadius") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Gravity", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Gravity") or 196.2) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "FallenPartsDestroyHeight", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "FallenPartsDestroyHeight") or -500) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "FilteringEnabled", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "FilteringEnabled") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "PivotShift", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "PivotShift")) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("Lighting") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Brightness", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Brightness") or 2) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ClockTime", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ClockTime") or 14) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "TimeOfDay", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "TimeOfDay")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "FogEnd", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "FogEnd") or 100000) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "FogStart", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "FogStart") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "FogColor", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "FogColor")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Ambient", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "Ambient")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "OutdoorAmbient", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "OutdoorAmbient")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ColorShift_Bottom", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "ColorShift_Bottom")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ColorShift_Top", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "ColorShift_Top")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "EnvironmentDiffuseScale", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "EnvironmentDiffuseScale") or 1) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "EnvironmentSpecularScale", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "EnvironmentSpecularScale") or 1) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "GlobalShadows", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "GlobalShadows") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ExposureCompensation", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ExposureCompensation") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Technology", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Technology") or Enum.Technology.Compatibility) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("Sound") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "SoundId", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "SoundId")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Volume", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Volume") or 0.5) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Pitch", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Pitch") or 1) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Playing", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Playing") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Looped", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Looped") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "TimeLength", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "TimeLength") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "TimePosition", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "TimePosition") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "PlaybackSpeed", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "PlaybackSpeed") or 1) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "RollOffMaxDistance", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "RollOffMaxDistance") or 10000) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "RollOffMinDistance", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "RollOffMinDistance") or 10) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "RollOffMode", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "RollOffMode") or Enum.RollOffMode.Inverse) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("SoundGroup") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Volume", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Volume") or 1) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("SoundService") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "RespectFilteringEnabled", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "RespectFilteringEnabled") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "VolumetricAudio", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "VolumetricAudio") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "ReverbLevel", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "ReverbLevel") or 0) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("RemoteEvent") or Instance:IsA("BindableEvent") then
            end
            if Instance:IsA("RemoteFunction") or Instance:IsA("BindableFunction") then
            end

            if Instance:IsA("Terrain") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Decoration", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Decoration") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "WaterColor", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "WaterColor")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "WaterTransparency", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "WaterTransparency") or 0.3) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "WaterWaveSize", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "WaterWaveSize") or 0.1) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "WaterWaveLength", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "WaterWaveLength") or 30) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "WaterReflectance", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "WaterReflectance") or 0.3) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("SpawnLocation") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Neutral", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Neutral") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Enabled", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Enabled") or true) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "AllowTeamChangeOnTouch", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "AllowTeamChangeOnTouch") or false) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "Duration", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "Duration") or 0) })
            end

            if Instance:IsA("Team") then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "TeamColor", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(Instance, "TeamColor")) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "AutoAssignable", tostring(SafeProp(Instance, "AutoAssignable") or true) })
            end

            if Instance == game then
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CreatorId", tostring(SafeProp(game, "CreatorId") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "CreatorType", tostring(SafeProp(game, "CreatorType") or Enum.CreatorType.User) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "GameId", tostring(SafeProp(game, "GameId") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "PlaceId", tostring(SafeProp(game, "PlaceId") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "PlaceVersion", tostring(SafeProp(game, "PlaceVersion") or 0) })
                TableInsert(Properties, { "JobId", FormatPropValue(SafeProp(game, "JobId")) })
            end

            return Properties
        end

        local BuildPropsFor = function(Instance)
            ClearProps()

            if not Instance then
                Items["StatusBar"].Instance.Text = "selected: none"
                return
            end

            local Name, Class = FormatInstanceName(Instance)
            Items["StatusBar"].Instance.Text = StringFormat("selected: %s [%s]", Name, Class)

            local Properties = CollectProperties(Instance)

            for _, Property in Properties do
                local Row = { Frame = nil, Name = nil, Value = nil }

                Row.Frame = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["PropsScroll"].Instance,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 13),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                    Name = "\0"
                })

                Row.Name = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                    Parent = Row.Frame.Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208),
                    Text = Property[1],
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2New(0.45, 0, 1, 0),
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    TextSize = 11,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                    Name = "\0"
                })
                Row.Name:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })

                Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = Row.Name.Instance,
                    LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                    Name = "\0"
                }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

                Row.Value = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                    Parent = Row.Frame.Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(167, 139, 250),
                    Text = tostring(Property[2]),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2New(0.45, 4, 0, 0),
                    Size = UDim2New(0.55, -4, 1, 0),
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    TextSize = 11,
                    TextWrapped = true,
                    TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                    Name = "\0"
                })
                Row.Value:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Accent" })

                Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = Row.Value.Instance,
                    LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                    Name = "\0"
                }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

                TableInsert(PropEntries, Row)
            end

            UpdateCanvasSize("PropsScroll")
        end

        local ApplySelectionVisual = function(Entry)
            if InstanceTree.Selected == Entry.Instance then
                Entry.Button.Instance.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                Entry.Button.Instance.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]
                Entry.Label.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                Entry.Arrow.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
            else
                Entry.Button.Instance.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                Entry.Label.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text
                Entry.Arrow.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text
            end
        end

        local MatchesSearch = function(Instance)
            local Query = InstanceTree.SearchQuery
            if not Query or Query == "" then
                return true
            end

            local Name, Class = FormatInstanceName(Instance)
            local LowerQuery = string.lower(Query)

            if string.find(string.lower(Name or ""), LowerQuery, 1, true) then
                return true
            end
            if string.find(string.lower(Class or ""), LowerQuery, 1, true) then
                return true
            end

            return false
        end

        local ShouldShowInSearch = function(Instance)
            if InstanceTree.SearchQuery == "" then
                return true
            end

            if MatchesSearch(Instance) then
                return true
            end

            local Success, Descendants = pcall(function()
                return Instance:GetDescendants()
            end)
            if Success and Descendants then
                for _, Descendant in Descendants do
                    if MatchesSearch(Descendant) then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end

            return false
        end

        BuildRow = function(Instance, Depth)
            if not Instance then
                return
            end

            if InstanceTree.SearchQuery ~= "" and not ShouldShowInSearch(Instance) then
                return
            end

            local Name, Class = FormatInstanceName(Instance)

            local Children = { }
            local Success, Kids = pcall(function()
                return Instance:GetChildren()
            end)
            if Success and Kids then
                for _, Child in Kids do
                    TableInsert(Children, Child)
                end
            end

            local HasChildren = #Children > 0
            local Expanded = InstanceTree.SearchQuery ~= "" or InstanceTree.Expanded[Instance] == true

            local Entry = {
                Instance = Instance,
                Depth = Depth,
                Button = nil,
                Arrow = nil,
                Label = nil
            }

            Entry.Button = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Items["TreeScroll"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 15),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 11,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                Name = "\0"
            })

            Entry.Arrow = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Entry.Button.Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208),
                Text = HasChildren and (Expanded and "-" or "+") or "",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Position = UDim2New(0, Depth * 12 + 2, 0, 3),
                Size = UDim2New(0, 12, 1, -3),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 11,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                Name = "\0"
            })
            Entry.Arrow:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Entry.Arrow.Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

            local DisplayClass = Class
            local Success, IsService = pcall(function() return Instance:IsA("Service") end)
            if Success and IsService then
                DisplayClass = "Service"
            end

            local DisplayText = StringFormat("%s [%s]", Name, DisplayClass)
            if #DisplayClass > 14 then
                DisplayText = StringFormat("%s [%s...]", Name, DisplayClass:sub(1, 11))
            end

            Entry.Label = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Entry.Button.Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208),
                Text = DisplayText,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2New(0, Depth * 12 + 16, 0, 3),
                Size = UDim2New(1, -(Depth * 12 + 18), 1, -3),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 11,
                TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                Name = "\0"
            })
            Entry.Label:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Entry.Label.Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

            Entry.Button:OnHover(function()
                if InstanceTree.Selected ~= Instance then
                    Entry.Button.Instance.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                    Entry.Button.Instance.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]
                end
            end)

            Entry.Button:OnHoverLeave(function()
                if InstanceTree.Selected ~= Instance then
                    Entry.Button.Instance.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                end
            end)

            Entry.Button:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                InstanceTree.Selected = Instance
                BuildPropsFor(Instance)
                RebuildTree()
            end)

            Entry.Button:Connect("MouseButton2Down", function()
                InstanceTree.Selected = Instance
                BuildPropsFor(Instance)
                RebuildTree()
                OpenContextMenu(Instance)
            end)

            Entry.Arrow:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                if HasChildren then
                    InstanceTree.Expanded[Instance] = not (InstanceTree.Expanded[Instance] == true)
                    RebuildTree()
                end
            end)

            ApplySelectionVisual(Entry)
            TableInsert(RowEntries, Entry)

            if Expanded and HasChildren then
                table.sort(Children, function(A, B)
                    local AN = A.Name or "?"
                    local BN = B.Name or "?"
                    return AN:lower() < BN:lower()
                end)

                for _, Child in Children do
                    BuildRow(Child, Depth + 1)
                end
            end
        end

        RebuildTree = function()
            ClearTree()

            for _, Instance in InstanceTree.Roots do
                BuildRow(Instance, 0)
            end

            UpdateCanvasSize("TreeScroll")
        end

        local CollectRoots = function()
            local Roots = { }
            local Candidates = {
                game:GetService("Workspace"),
                LocalPlayer,
                game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"),
                game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"),
                game:GetService("StarterGui"),
                game:GetService("StarterPlayer"),
                game:GetService("StarterPack"),
                game:GetService("Lighting"),
                game:GetService("SoundService"),
                game:GetService("CoreGui")
            }

            for _, Instance in Candidates do
                if Instance then
                    TableInsert(Roots, Instance)
                end
            end

            table.sort(Roots, function(A, B)
                local AN = A.Name or "?"
                local BN = B.Name or "?"
                return AN:lower() < BN:lower()
            end)

            return Roots
        end

        local CloseContextMenu = function()
            if ContextMenu.Frame and ContextMenu.Frame.Instance then
                ContextMenu.Frame.Instance.Visible = false
                ContextMenu.Visible = false
                JustOpenedMenu = false
            end
        end

        local AddContextMenuItem = function(Label, Callback)
            local Button = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Items["ContextMenu"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208),
                Text = Label,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 20),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                TextSize = 11,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                ZIndex = 201,
                Name = "\0"
            })
            Button:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Button.Instance,
                PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 8),
                PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 8)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Button.Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

            Button:OnHover(function()
                Button.Instance.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                Button.Instance.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]
            end)

            Button:OnHoverLeave(function()
                Button.Instance.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            end)

            Button:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                Callback()
                CloseContextMenu()
            end)

            return Button
        end

        local CurrentContextMenuItems = { }

        local RebuildContextMenu = function(Instance)
            for _, Item in CurrentContextMenuItems do
                if Item and Item.Instance then
                    Item:Clean()
                end
            end
            CurrentContextMenuItems = { }

            if not Instance then
                return
            end

            local Name, Class = FormatInstanceName(Instance)
            local Path = GetInstancePath(Instance)

            TableInsert(CurrentContextMenuItems, AddContextMenuItem("Copy Path", function()
                if setclipboard then
                    setclipboard(Path)
                    Library:Notification("Path copied: " .. Path, 2, Library.Theme.Accent)
                end
            end))

            TableInsert(CurrentContextMenuItems, AddContextMenuItem("Copy Name", function()
                if setclipboard then
                    setclipboard(Name)
                    Library:Notification("Name copied: " .. Name, 2, Library.Theme.Accent)
                end
            end))

            TableInsert(CurrentContextMenuItems, AddContextMenuItem("Copy ClassName", function()
                if setclipboard then
                    setclipboard(Class)
                    Library:Notification("ClassName copied: " .. Class, 2, Library.Theme.Accent)
                end
            end))

            local ItemHeight = 20
            local VerticalPadding = 4
            local TotalHeight = ItemHeight * #CurrentContextMenuItems + VerticalPadding
            Items["ContextMenu"].Instance.Size = UDim2New(0, 140, 0, TotalHeight)
        end


        OpenContextMenu = function(Instance)
            RebuildContextMenu(Instance)

            if not Instance then
                return
            end

            local MousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            local GuiInset = game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset().Y
            local ScreenPos = Vector2New(MousePos.X, MousePos.Y - GuiInset)

            local WindowPos = Items["Window"].Instance.AbsolutePosition
            local RelativeX = ScreenPos.X - WindowPos.X
            local RelativeY = ScreenPos.Y - WindowPos.Y

            local MenuWidth = 140
            local MenuHeight = Items["ContextMenu"].Instance.AbsoluteSize.Y
            if MenuHeight == 0 then
                MenuHeight = 64
            end
            local WindowWidth = Items["Window"].Instance.AbsoluteSize.X
            local WindowHeight = Items["Window"].Instance.AbsoluteSize.Y

            if RelativeX + MenuWidth > WindowWidth then
                RelativeX = WindowWidth - MenuWidth
            end
            if RelativeY + MenuHeight > WindowHeight then
                RelativeY = WindowHeight - MenuHeight
            end
            if RelativeX < 0 then RelativeX = 0 end
            if RelativeY < 0 then RelativeY = 0 end

            Items["ContextMenu"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0, RelativeX, 0, RelativeY)
            Items["ContextMenu"].Instance.Visible = true
            ContextMenu.Visible = true
            JustOpenedMenu = true
            task.delay(0.15, function()
                JustOpenedMenu = false
            end)
        end

        Library:Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(Input)
            if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and ContextMenu.Visible then
                if not JustOpenedMenu and not Library:IsMouseOverFrame(Items["ContextMenu"]) then
                    CloseContextMenu()
                end
                JustOpenedMenu = false
            end
            if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 and ContextMenu.Visible then
                if not JustOpenedMenu and not Library:IsMouseOverFrame(Items["ContextMenu"]) then
                    CloseContextMenu()
                end
                JustOpenedMenu = false
            end
        end)

        function InstanceTree:Refresh()
            self.Roots = CollectRoots()

            if self.Selected and (not self.Selected.Parent and self.Selected ~= game) then
                self.Selected = nil
                BuildPropsFor(nil)
            else
                BuildPropsFor(self.Selected)
            end

            RebuildTree()
        end

        function InstanceTree:SetVisibility(Bool)
            if Items["Window"] and Items["Window"].Instance then
                Items["Window"].Instance.Visible = Bool
                if not Bool then
                    CloseContextMenu()
                end
            end
        end

        function InstanceTree:Select(Instance)
            self.Selected = Instance
            BuildPropsFor(Instance)
            RebuildTree()
        end

        function InstanceTree:ToggleExpand(Instance)
            if not Instance then
                return
            end
            self.Expanded[Instance] = not (self.Expanded[Instance] == true)
            RebuildTree()
        end

        function InstanceTree:SetSearch(Query)
            self.SearchQuery = (Query or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
            RebuildTree()
        end

        Items["Refresh"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            InstanceTree:Refresh()
            Library:Notification("Instance tree refreshed", 2, Library.Theme.Accent)
        end)

        Items["Close"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            if InstanceTree.CloseToggle and InstanceTree.CloseToggle.Set then
                InstanceTree.CloseToggle:Set(false)
            else
                InstanceTree:SetVisibility(false)
            end
        end)

        Items["SearchBox"]:Connect("FocusLost", function()
            InstanceTree:SetSearch(Items["SearchBox"].Instance.Text)
        end)

        Items["SearchBox"].Instance:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            InstanceTree:SetSearch(Items["SearchBox"].Instance.Text)
        end)

        InstanceTree.Roots = CollectRoots()
        InstanceTree:Refresh()

        InstanceTree.Items = Items
        Library.InstanceTreeInstance = InstanceTree

        return InstanceTree
    end

    Library.Settings = function(self, Window)
        if not Window then
            return
        end

        if not Library.InstanceTreeInstance then
            pcall(function()
                Library:InstanceTree()
            end)
        end

        local Settings = {
            Window = Window,
            Elements = { }
        }

        local SettingsPage = Window:Page({
            Name = "Settings",
            Columns = 2
        })

        Settings.Page = SettingsPage

        local ConfigSection = SettingsPage:Section({
            Name = "Configs",
            Side = 1
        })

        local CurrentExplorerPath = Library.Folders.Configs
        local SelectedConfigPath  = nil
        local SelectedConfigName  = nil
        local SelectedEntryLabel  = nil

        local function Explorer_GetNameFromPath(Path)
            local Name = StringGSub(Path or "", ".*[/\\]", "")
            return Name
        end

        local function Explorer_GetParentPath(Path)
            if not Path or Path == "" then return "" end
            local Parent = StringGSub(Path, "[/\\][^/\\]+$", "")
            if Parent == Path then return "" end
            return Parent
        end

        local function Explorer_GetFolderLabel(Path)
            if Path == "" or Path == "." then return "Workspace" end
            local Name = Explorer_GetNameFromPath(Path)
            if Name == "" then return "Workspace" end
            return Name
        end

        local ExplorerContainer = Instances:Create("Frame", {
            Parent = ConfigSection.Elements["Content"].Instance,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 200),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Name = "\0",
        })

        local ExplorerHeader = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
            Parent = ExplorerContainer.Instance,
            Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 22),
            BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(20, 20, 25),
            BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
            BorderSizePixel = 1,
            Text = "",
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Name = "\0",
        })
        ExplorerHeader:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

        Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
            Parent = ExplorerHeader.Instance,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
            LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
            Name = "\0",
            Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
        }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

        local HeaderIcon = Instances:Create("ImageLabel", {
            Parent = ExplorerHeader.Instance,
            Size = UDim2New(0, 14, 0, 14),
            Position = UDim2New(0, 6, 0.5, -7),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image = Library:GetImage("Folder"),
            ImageColor3 = FromRGB(170, 170, 178),
            Name = "\0",
        })

        local HeaderLabel = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
            Parent = ExplorerHeader.Instance,
            FontFace = Library.Font,
            TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
            Text = Explorer_GetFolderLabel(CurrentExplorerPath),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2New(0, 24, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2New(1, -30, 1, 0),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            TextSize = 12,
            Name = "\0",
        })
        HeaderLabel:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

        local HeaderChevron = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
            Parent = ExplorerHeader.Instance,
            FontFace = Library.Font,
            TextColor3 = FromRGB(150, 150, 158),
            Text = "^",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2New(1, -18, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2New(0, 14, 1, 0),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            TextSize = 12,
            Name = "\0",
        })

        local ExplorerScroll = Instances:Create("ScrollingFrame", {
            Parent = ExplorerContainer.Instance,
            Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 24),
            Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, -24),
            BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20),
            BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
            BorderSizePixel = 2,
            ScrollBarThickness = 1,
            AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            CanvasSize = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0),
            ScrollBarImageColor3 = FromRGB(235, 157, 255),
            Name = "\0",
        })
        ExplorerScroll:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border", ScrollBarImageColor3 = "Accent"})

        Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
            Parent = ExplorerScroll.Instance,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
            LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
            Name = "\0",
            Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
        }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

        Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
            Parent = ExplorerScroll.Instance,
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        })

        Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
            Parent = ExplorerScroll.Instance,
            PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 2),
            PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 2),
        })

        local ExplorerEntryButtons = {}

        local function Explorer_ClearEntries()
            for _, Btn in ExplorerEntryButtons do
                pcall(function()
                    if Btn and Btn.Instance then
                        Btn.Instance:Destroy()
                    end
                end)
            end
            ExplorerEntryButtons = {}
            SelectedEntryLabel = nil
        end

        local Explorer_Refresh

        local function Explorer_BuildEntries()
            Explorer_ClearEntries()

            local Success, Files = pcall(function()
                return listfiles(CurrentExplorerPath)
            end)

            if not Success or not Files then
                local Empty = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                    Parent = ExplorerScroll.Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(120, 120, 128),
                    TextTransparency = 0.4,
                    Text = "  (empty or unreachable)",
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 18),
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    TextSize = 11,
                    Name = "\0",
                    ZIndex = ExplorerScroll.Instance.ZIndex + 5,
                })
                TableInsert(ExplorerEntryButtons, Empty)
                ExplorerScroll.Instance.CanvasSize = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 22)
                return
            end

            local Folders = {}
            local JsonFiles = {}

            for _, File in Files do
                local Name = Explorer_GetNameFromPath(File)
                if Name ~= "" and Name ~= "." and Name ~= ".." then
                    local IsFolder = false
                    pcall(function() IsFolder = isfolder(File) end)
                    if IsFolder then
                        TableInsert(Folders, {Name = Name, FullPath = File})
                    elseif StringFind(Name:lower(), "%.json$") then
                        TableInsert(JsonFiles, {Name = Name, FullPath = File})
                    end
                end
            end

            table.sort(Folders, function(a, b) return a.Name:lower() < b.Name:lower() end)
            table.sort(JsonFiles, function(a, b) return a.Name:lower() < b.Name:lower() end)

            local LayoutOrder = 1
            local TotalHeight = 4

            for _, Folder in Folders do
                local Btn = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                    Parent = ExplorerScroll.Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Text = "",
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 18),
                    LayoutOrder = LayoutOrder,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    TextSize = 12,
                    Name = "\0",
                    ZIndex = ExplorerScroll.Instance.ZIndex + 4,
                })
                LayoutOrder = LayoutOrder + 1
                TotalHeight = TotalHeight + 18

                local Icon = Instances:Create("ImageLabel", {
                    Parent = Btn.Instance,
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 12, 0, 12),
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 7, 0.5, -6),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Image = Library:GetImage("Folder"),
                    ImageColor3 = FromRGB(170, 170, 178),
                    Name = "\0",
                    ZIndex = Btn.Instance.ZIndex + 1,
                })

                local Label = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                    Parent = Btn.Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(180, 180, 188),
                    Text = Folder.Name,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 23, 0, 0),
                    Size = UDim2New(1, -28, 1, 0),
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    TextSize = 12,
                    Name = "\0",
                    ZIndex = Btn.Instance.ZIndex + 1,
                })

                Btn:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                    CurrentExplorerPath = Folder.FullPath
                    HeaderLabel.Instance.Text = Explorer_GetFolderLabel(CurrentExplorerPath)
                    SelectedConfigPath = nil
                    SelectedConfigName = nil
                    Explorer_BuildEntries()
                end)

                Btn:OnHover(function()
                    Label.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                end)
                Btn:OnHoverLeave(function()
                    Label.Instance.TextColor3 = FromRGB(180, 180, 188)
                end)

                TableInsert(ExplorerEntryButtons, Btn)
            end

            for _, File in JsonFiles do
                local Btn = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                    Parent = ExplorerScroll.Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Text = "",
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 18),
                    LayoutOrder = LayoutOrder,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    TextSize = 12,
                    Name = "\0",
                    ZIndex = ExplorerScroll.Instance.ZIndex + 4,
                })
                LayoutOrder = LayoutOrder + 1
                TotalHeight = TotalHeight + 18

                local Label = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                    Parent = Btn.Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                    TextTransparency = 0.48,
                    Text = File.Name,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 23, 0, 0),
                    Size = UDim2New(1, -28, 1, 0),
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    TextSize = 12,
                    Name = "\0",
                    ZIndex = Btn.Instance.ZIndex + 1,
                })
                Label:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

                local RowState = { Selected = false, Label = Label, File = File }

                Btn:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                    if SelectedEntryLabel and SelectedEntryLabel ~= RowState then
                        SelectedEntryLabel.Selected = false
                        if SelectedEntryLabel.Label and SelectedEntryLabel.Label.Instance then
                            SelectedEntryLabel.Label.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text
                            SelectedEntryLabel.Label.Instance.TextTransparency = 0.48
                        end
                    end

                    if RowState.Selected then
                        RowState.Selected = false
                        SelectedConfigPath = nil
                        SelectedConfigName = nil
                        SelectedEntryLabel = nil
                        Label.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text
                        Label.Instance.TextTransparency = 0.48
                    else
                        RowState.Selected = true
                        SelectedConfigPath = File.FullPath
                        SelectedConfigName = StringGSub(File.Name, "%.json$", "")
                        SelectedEntryLabel = RowState
                        Label.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                        Label.Instance.TextTransparency = 0
                    end
                end)

                Btn:Connect("MouseButton2Down", function()
                    if SelectedEntryLabel and SelectedEntryLabel ~= RowState then
                        SelectedEntryLabel.Selected = false
                        if SelectedEntryLabel.Label and SelectedEntryLabel.Label.Instance then
                            SelectedEntryLabel.Label.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text
                            SelectedEntryLabel.Label.Instance.TextTransparency = 0.48
                        end
                    end
                    RowState.Selected = true
                    SelectedConfigPath = File.FullPath
                    SelectedConfigName = StringGSub(File.Name, "%.json$", "")
                    SelectedEntryLabel = RowState
                    Label.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                    Label.Instance.TextTransparency = 0

                    Library:RenamePopup({
                        Window = ConfigSection.Window,
                        Parent = ConfigSection.Window.Elements["MainFrame"],
                        Text = "Rename Config",
                        DefaultName = SelectedConfigName,
                        OnConfirm = function(NewName)
                            local OldPath = File.FullPath
                            local NewPath = CurrentExplorerPath .. "/" .. NewName .. ".json"

                            if OldPath == NewPath then
                                Library:Notification("Name unchanged", 3, Library.Theme.Accent)
                                return
                            end

                            if isfile(NewPath) then
                                Library:Notification("A config with that name already exists", 4, FromRGB(255, 70, 70))
                                return
                            end

                            local Success, Err = pcall(function()
                                local Content = readfile(OldPath)
                                writefile(NewPath, Content)
                                delfile(OldPath)
                            end)

                            if Success then
                                Library:Notification("Renamed to " .. NewName .. ".json", 5, FromRGB(0, 255, 0))
                                Explorer_Refresh()
                            else
                                Library:Notification("Rename failed: " .. tostring(Err), 4, FromRGB(255, 70, 70))
                            end
                        end
                    })
                end)

                Btn:OnHover(function()
                    if not RowState.Selected then
                        Label.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                        Label.Instance.TextTransparency = 0.2
                    end
                end)
                Btn:OnHoverLeave(function()
                    if not RowState.Selected then
                        Label.Instance.TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text
                        Label.Instance.TextTransparency = 0.48
                    end
                end)

                TableInsert(ExplorerEntryButtons, Btn)
            end

            ExplorerScroll.Instance.CanvasSize = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, TotalHeight)
        end

        ExplorerHeader:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            local Parent = Explorer_GetParentPath(CurrentExplorerPath)
            if Parent ~= CurrentExplorerPath then
                CurrentExplorerPath = Parent
                HeaderLabel.Instance.Text = Explorer_GetFolderLabel(CurrentExplorerPath)
                SelectedConfigPath = nil
                SelectedConfigName = nil
                Explorer_BuildEntries()
            end
        end)

        Explorer_BuildEntries()

        ConfigSection:Textbox({
            Name = "Config Name",
            Default = "",
            Placeholder = "Enter config name (for Save As)...",
            Flag = "Settings_ConfigName",
            Callback = function(Value)
            end
        })

        Explorer_Refresh = function()
            SelectedConfigPath = nil
            SelectedConfigName = nil
            Explorer_BuildEntries()
        end

        ConfigSection:ButtonGroup({
            Buttons = {
                {
                    Name = "Save",
                    Callback = function()
                        local Name = Library.Flags["Settings_ConfigName"]
                        if not Name or Name == "" then
                            Library:Notification("Enter a config name first", 4, FromRGB(255, 70, 70))
                            return
                        end

                        local SavePath = CurrentExplorerPath .. "/" .. Name .. ".json"
                        local Success = pcall(function()
                            writefile(SavePath, Library:GetConfig())
                        end)

                        if Success then
                            Library:Notification("Saved config " .. Name .. ".json", 5, FromRGB(0, 255, 0))
                            Explorer_Refresh()
                        else
                            Library:Notification("Failed to save config", 4, FromRGB(255, 70, 70))
                        end
                    end
                },
                {
                    Name = "Load",
                    Callback = function()
                        if not SelectedConfigPath then
                            Library:Notification("Select a .json file first", 4, FromRGB(255, 70, 70))
                            return
                        end

                        if not isfile(SelectedConfigPath) then
                            Library:Notification("Config not found", 4, FromRGB(255, 70, 70))
                            return
                        end

                        local Content = readfile(SelectedConfigPath)
                        Library:LoadConfig(Content)
                    end
                }
            }
        })

        ConfigSection:ButtonGroup({
            Buttons = {
                {
                    Name = "Delete",
                    Risky = true,
                    RiskyText = "Are you sure you want to delete this config?",
                    Callback = function()
                        if not SelectedConfigPath then
                            Library:Notification("Select a .json file first", 4, FromRGB(255, 70, 70))
                            return
                        end

                        if not isfile(SelectedConfigPath) then
                            Library:Notification("Config not found", 4, FromRGB(255, 70, 70))
                            return
                        end

                        pcall(function() delfile(SelectedConfigPath) end)
                        Library:Notification("Deleted " .. (SelectedConfigName or "config"), 5, FromRGB(0, 255, 0))
                        Explorer_Refresh()
                    end
                },
                {
                    Name = "Refresh",
                    Callback = function()
                        Explorer_Refresh()
                        Library:Notification("Configs refreshed", 3, Library.Theme.Accent)
                    end
                }
            }
        })

        local UISettingsSection = SettingsPage:Section({
            Name = "UI Settings",
            Side = 2
        })

        local MenuKeybindLabel = UISettingsSection:Label({ Name = "Menu Keybind", Alignment = "Left" })
        local MenuKeybindExt
        MenuKeybindLabel, MenuKeybindExt = MenuKeybindLabel:Keybind({
            Name = "Menu Keybind",
            Mode = "Toggle",
            Default = Enum.KeyCode.RightShift,
            Flag = "Settings_MenuKeybind",
            Callback = function()
                if MenuKeybindExt and MenuKeybindExt.Key then
                    Library.MenuKeybind = MenuKeybindExt.Key
                end
            end
        })

        local WatermarkToggle = UISettingsSection:Toggle({
            Name = "Show Watermark",
            Default = true,
            Flag = "Settings_Watermark",
            Callback = function(Value)
                if Library.WatermarkInstance and Library.WatermarkInstance.SetVisibility then
                    Library.WatermarkInstance:SetVisibility(Value)
                end
                if Library.WatermarkFrame then
                    Library.WatermarkFrame.Visible = Value
                end
            end
        })

        local KeybindListToggle = UISettingsSection:Toggle({
            Name = "Show Keybind List",
            Default = true,
            Flag = "Settings_KeybindList",
            Callback = function(Value)
                if Library.KeyList and Library.KeyList.SetVisibility then
                    Library.KeyList:SetVisibility(Value)
                end
                if Library.KeyListFrame then
                    Library.KeyListFrame.Visible = Value
                end
            end
        })

        UISettingsSection:Dropdown({
            Name = "Notification Position",
            Items = { "Top Center", "Top Right", "Top Left", "Bottom Right", "Bottom Left", "Bottom Center" },
            Default = "Top Center",
            Flag = "Settings_NotifPosition",
            Callback = function(Value)
                if Library.NotifHolder and Library.NotifHolder.Instance then
                    local Inst = Library.NotifHolder.Instance
                    local Layout = Inst:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")

                    if Value == "Top Center" then
                        Inst.Position = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 0, 10)
                        Inst.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0.5, 0)
                        if Layout then
                            Layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
                            Layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
                        end
                    elseif Value == "Top Right" then
                        Inst.Position = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0)
                        Inst.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0)
                        if Layout then
                            Layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
                            Layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
                        end
                    elseif Value == "Top Left" then
                        Inst.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0)
                        Inst.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0)
                        if Layout then
                            Layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
                            Layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
                        end
                    elseif Value == "Bottom Right" then
                        Inst.Position = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0)
                        Inst.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 1)
                        if Layout then
                            Layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
                            Layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
                        end
                    elseif Value == "Bottom Left" then
                        Inst.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 0)
                        Inst.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 1)
                        if Layout then
                            Layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
                            Layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
                        end
                    elseif Value == "Bottom Center" then
                        Inst.Position = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 1, -40)
                        Inst.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0.5, 1)
                        if Layout then
                            Layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
                            Layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        })

        UISettingsSection:Slider({
            Name = "Tween Time",
            Min = 0.05,
            Max = 1,
            Default = Library.Tween.Time,
            Decimals = 0.01,
            Suffix = "s",
            Flag = "Settings_TweenTime",

        UISettingsSection:Slider({

            Max = 0.02,
            Default = 0.002,

        UISettingsSection:Textbox({
            Name = "Spotify Token",
            Default = "",
            Placeholder = "Paste token here...",
            Flag = "Settings_SpotifyToken",
            Callback = function(Value)
                Library.SpotifyToken = Value
            end
        })

        UISettingsSection:Button({
            Name = "Set Token",
            Callback = function()
                local Token = Library.Flags["Settings_SpotifyToken"]
                if Token and type(Token) == "string" and Token ~= "" then
                    Library.SpotifyToken = Token
                end

                if not Library.SpotifyToken or Library.SpotifyToken == "" then
                    Library:Notification("No token entered", 3, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                    return
                end

                Library:Notification("Token set, refreshing...", 2, Library.Theme.Accent)

                if Library.MusicPlayerInstance then
                    Library.MusicPlayerInstance:Refresh()
                end

                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(3)
                    if Library.SpotifyToken and Library.SpotifyToken ~= "" then
                        if not Library.MusicPlayerInstance or not Library.MusicPlayerInstance.Items then
                            Library.DeleteSpotifyToken()
                            Library:Notification("Token invalid, removed", 3, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                            return
                        end
                        local LastError = Library.MusicPlayerInstance.Items["SongName"] and Library.MusicPlayerInstance.Items["SongName"].Instance.Text
                        if LastError and (LastError:lower():find("error") or LastError:lower():find("unauthorized")) then
                            Library.DeleteSpotifyToken()
                            Library:Notification("Token invalid, removed", 3, FromRGB(220, 90, 90))
                        else
                            Library.SaveSpotifyToken(Library.SpotifyToken)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        })

        local SpotifyPanelToggle = UISettingsSection:Toggle({
            Name = "Show Spotify Panel",
            Default = true,
            Flag = "Settings_SpotifyPanel",
            Callback = function(Value)

    local Components = { } do
        Components.ColorpickerTab = function(self, Data)
            if not Data.Pages then
                return
            end

            local NewTab = {
                Name = Data.Name,
                Active = false
            }

            local Items = { } do
                Items["Inactive"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                    Parent = Data.PageHolder.Instance,
                    Name = "\0",
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Text = NewTab.Name,
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    TextSize = 12,
                    TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(26, 26, 32)
                })  Items["Inactive"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Inline"})

                Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                    LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                    Name = "\0"
                }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

                Items["AccentLine"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                    Name = "\0",
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 1),
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 0),
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 2),
                    Visible = false,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(167, 139, 250)
                })  Items["AccentLine"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Accent"})

                Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                    Parent = Items["AccentLine"].Instance,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Rotation = 0,
                    Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0),
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0),
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
                    })
                })

                Items["PageContent"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Data.ContentHolder.Instance,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Visible = false,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                })
            end

            function NewTab:Turn(Bool)
                NewTab.Active = Bool

                if NewTab.Active then
                    Items["PageContent"].Instance.Visible = true
                    Items["PageContent"].Instance.Parent = Data.ContentHolder.Instance

                    Items["AccentLine"].Instance.Visible = true

                    Items["Inactive"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background"})
                    Items["Inactive"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Background})
                else
                    Items["PageContent"].Instance.Visible = false

                    Items["AccentLine"].Instance.Visible = false

                    Items["Inactive"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Inline"})
                    Items["Inactive"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Inline})
                end
            end

            Items["Inactive"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                for Index, Value in Data.Stack do
                    Value:Turn(Value == NewTab)
                end
            end)

            if #Data.Stack == 0 then
                NewTab:Turn(true)
            end

            TableInsert(Data.Stack, NewTab)
            return NewTab, Items
        end

                })
