            Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent})
            Items["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Accent"})
            Items["Text"].Instance.Text = "..."

            local InputBegan
            InputBegan = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)
                if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                    if Input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or Input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                        Keybind:Set("None")
                    else
                        Keybind:Set(Input.KeyCode)
                    end
                elseif Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    Keybind:Set("MouseButton1")
                elseif Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
                    -- Right-click cancels key picking, does not bind right click
                    Keybind:Set(Keybind.Key or "None")
                elseif Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 then
                    Keybind:Set("MouseButton3")
                end

                if InputBegan then
                    InputBegan:Disconnect()
                    InputBegan = nil
                end
            end)
        end)

        local function toggleModeMenu()
            if Keybind.Picking then return end
            Keybind:SetOpen(not Keybind.IsOpen)
        end

        Items["KeyButton"]:Connect("MouseButton2Click", toggleModeMenu)

        Library:Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(Input, Processed)
            if Processed or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
            if not Keybind.Picking and Keybind.Key and Keybind.Key ~= "" and Keybind.Key ~= "None" and Keybind.Key ~= "nil" then
                if tostring(Input.KeyCode) == Keybind.Key or tostring(Input.UserInputType) == Keybind.Key then
                    if Keybind.Mode == "Toggle" then
                        Keybind:Press()
                    elseif Keybind.Mode == "Hold" then
                        Keybind:Press(true)
                    end
                end
            end

            if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
                if Keybind.IsOpen and not Debounce then
                    if not Library:IsMouseOverFrame(Items["Window"]) and not Library:IsMouseOverFrame(Items["KeyButton"]) then
                        Keybind:SetOpen(false)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        Library:Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(Input)
            if not Keybind.Picking and Keybind.Key and Keybind.Key ~= "" and Keybind.Key ~= "None" and Keybind.Key ~= "nil" then
                if tostring(Input.KeyCode) == Keybind.Key or tostring(Input.UserInputType) == Keybind.Key then
                    if Keybind.Mode == "Hold" then
                        Keybind:Press(false)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        Items["Toggle"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            Keybind.Mode = "Toggle"
            Keybind:SetMode("Toggle")
            Keybind:SetOpen(false)
        end)

        Items["Always"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            Keybind.Mode = "Always"
            Keybind:SetMode("Always")
            Keybind:SetOpen(false)
        end)

        Items["Hold"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            Keybind.Mode = "Hold"
            Keybind:SetMode("Hold")
            Keybind:SetOpen(false)
        end)

        if Data.Default then
            Keybind:Set({
                Key = Data.Default,
                Mode = Data.Mode or "Toggle"
            })
        else
            Keybind:Set({
                Key = nil,
                Mode = Data.Mode or "Toggle"
            })
        end

        Library.Defaults[Data.Flag] = {
            Key = Data.Default,
            Mode = Data.Mode or "Toggle"
        }
        Library.SetFlags[Data.Flag] = function(Value)
            Keybind:Set(Value)
        end

        Keybind.Items = Items

        return Keybind
    end

    Library.Window = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local Window = {
            Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Window",
            Subtitle = Data.Subtitle or Data.subtitle or "",
            TitlePosition = Data.TitlePosition or Data.titleposition or "Left",
            RichText = Data.RichText or Data.richtext or false,
            Size = Data.Size or Data.size or UDim2New(0, 500, 0, 600),
            TabLayout = Data.TabLayout or Data.tablayout or Data.Tablayout or "Vertical",

            FadeSpeed = Data.FadeSpeed or Data.fadespeed or 0.25,

            Pages = { },
            Sections = { },
            SubPages = { },
            Features = { },
            Elements = { },

            IsOpen = true
        }

        if Data.TitleColor or Data.titlecolor then
            Library:ChangeTheme("WindowTitle", Data.TitleColor or Data.titlecolor)
        end
        if Data.SubtitleColor or Data.subtitlecolor then
            Library:ChangeTheme("WindowSubtitle", Data.SubtitleColor or Data.subtitlecolor)
        end

        local Items = { } do
            Items["MainFrame"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Library.Holder.Instance,
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0),
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Size = Window.Size,
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20)
            })  Items["MainFrame"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Items["MainFrame"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0, Camera.ViewportSize.X / 4, 0, Camera.ViewportSize.Y / 4)

            Items["MainFrame"]:MakeDraggable()
            Items["MainFrame"]:MakeResizeable(Vector2New(Window.Size.X.Offset, Window.Size.Y.Offset), Vector2New(9999, 9999))

            Items["AccentBorder"] = Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["MainFrame"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(235, 157, 255),
                Thickness = 1.5
            })  Items["AccentBorder"]:AddToTheme({Color = "Accent"})

            Library:ApplyAnimatedGradient(Items["AccentBorder"].Instance)

            local TitleIsCenter = Window.TitlePosition == "Center"
            local TitleAlign = TitleIsCenter and Enum.TextXAlignment.Center or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            local TitleSize = TitleIsCenter and UDim2New(1, -12, 0, 15) or UDim2New(0.5, -6, 0, 15)
            local TitlePos = TitleIsCenter and UDim2New(0, 6, 0, 1) or UDim2New(0, 6, 0, 1)

            Items["Title"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["MainFrame"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = Window.Name,
                RichText = Window.RichText,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = TitleSize,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = TitleAlign,
                Position = TitlePos,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Title"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "WindowTitle"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Title"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["Subtitle"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["MainFrame"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = Library.Theme.WindowSubtitle or FromRGB(167, 139, 250),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = Window.Subtitle,
                RichText = Window.RichText,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(0.5, -6, 0, 15),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
                Position = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 0, 1),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Subtitle"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "WindowSubtitle"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Subtitle"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["Inline"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["MainFrame"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 20),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(27, 27, 32),
                Size = UDim2New(1, -14, 1, -27),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(20, 20, 25)
            })  Items["Inline"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Outline"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                Color = Library.Theme.Border,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Border"})

            local IsHorizontal = Window.TabLayout == "Horizontal"

            Items["SearchContainer"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                Position = IsHorizontal and UDim2New(1, -117, 0, 5) or UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 7),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Size = UDim2New(0, 110, 0, 22),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(30, 30, 35)
            })  Items["SearchContainer"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Page Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["SearchContainer"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                Color = Library.Theme.Outline,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["SearchContainer"].Instance,
                Rotation = 0,
                Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(108, 108, 108))}
            })

            Items["SearchIcon"] = Instances:Create("ImageLabel", {
                Parent = Items["SearchContainer"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 6, 0.5, 0),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0.5),
                Size = UDim2New(0, 11, 0, 11),
                Image = Library:GetImage("Search") or "https://github.com/jewhaxx/libraries/blob/main/assets/search.png?raw=true",
                ImageColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                ImageTransparency = 0.45,
                ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                BorderSizePixel = 0
            })  Items["SearchIcon"]:AddToTheme({ImageColor3 = "Text"})

            Items["SearchInput"] = Instances:Create("TextBox", {
                Parent = Items["SearchContainer"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 22, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, -26, 1, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ClearTextOnFocus = false,
                Text = "",
                PlaceholderText = "Search...",
                PlaceholderColor3 = FromRGB(130, 130, 140),
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                TextSize = 11,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            })  Items["SearchInput"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["SearchInput"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["Pages"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = IsHorizontal and UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 7) or UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 35),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = IsHorizontal and UDim2New(1, -131, 0, 19) or UDim2New(0, 110, 1, -42),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["Pages"].Instance,
                FillDirection = IsHorizontal and Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal or Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
                HorizontalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill,
                Padding = UDimNew(0, 6),
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            })

            Items["Content"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                Position = IsHorizontal and UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 32) or UDim2New(0, 123, 0, 7),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Size = IsHorizontal and UDim2New(1, -14, 1, -39) or UDim2New(1, -136, 1, -14),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20)
            })  Items["Content"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Content"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                Color = Library.Theme.Outline,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})
        end

        function Window:SetOpen(Bool)
            if Window.IsOpen == Bool then
                return
            end

            Window.IsOpen = Bool

            local Frame = Items["MainFrame"].Instance
            Frame.Visible = Bool
        end

        function Window:Search(Query)
            local IsEmpty = (not Query or Query == "" or StringGSub(Query, "%s+", "") == "")
            local CleanQuery = not IsEmpty and string.lower(StringGSub(Query, "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")) or ""
            local StrippedQuery = not IsEmpty and StringGSub(CleanQuery, "%s+", "") or ""

            local FirstMatchingPage = nil
            local CurrentActivePageHasMatches = false

            for _, Sec in Window.Sections do
                local SectionFrame = Sec.Elements and (Sec.Elements["Section"] or Sec.Elements["MultiSection"])
                if SectionFrame and SectionFrame.Instance then
                    if IsEmpty then
                        SectionFrame.Instance.Visible = true
                        if Sec.Collapsible and Sec.Collapsed then
                            if Sec.Elements["Content"] then
                                Sec.Elements["Content"].Instance.Visible = false
                            end
                            if Sec.Elements["Chevron"] then
                                Sec.Elements["Chevron"].Instance.Text = "+"
                            end
                        else
                            if Sec.Elements["Content"] then
                                Sec.Elements["Content"].Instance.Visible = true
                            end
                            if Sec.Elements["Chevron"] then
                                Sec.Elements["Chevron"].Instance.Text = "-"
                            end
                        end
                        if Sec.SectionContents then
                            for _, SubSec in Sec.SectionContents do
                                if SubSec.Elements and SubSec.Elements["Content"] then
                                    SubSec.Elements["Content"].Instance.Visible = SubSec.Active
                                end
                                for _, Feature in (SubSec.Features or { }) do
                                    if Feature.Instance then
                                        Feature.Instance.Visible = true
                                    end
                                    if Feature.ParentInstance then
                                        Feature.ParentInstance.Visible = true
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        for _, Feature in (Sec.Features or { }) do
                            if Feature.Instance then
                                Feature.Instance.Visible = true
                            end
                            if Feature.ParentInstance then
                                Feature.ParentInstance.Visible = true
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        local SectionName = string.lower(Sec.Name or "")
                        local TaglessSectionName = string.lower(StringGSub(Sec.Name or "", "<[^>]+>", ""))
                        local StrippedSectionName = StringGSub(TaglessSectionName, "%s+", "")
                        local SectionNameMatches = StringFind(SectionName, CleanQuery, 1, true) ~= nil
                            or StringFind(TaglessSectionName, CleanQuery, 1, true) ~= nil
                            or (StrippedQuery ~= "" and StringFind(StrippedSectionName, StrippedQuery, 1, true) ~= nil)

                        local SectionMatches = 0
                        local FirstMatchingSubSec = nil

                        if Sec.SectionContents then
                            for _, SubSec in Sec.SectionContents do
                                local SubName = string.lower(SubSec.Name or "")
                                local TaglessSubName = string.lower(StringGSub(SubSec.Name or "", "<[^>]+>", ""))
                                local StrippedSubName = StringGSub(TaglessSubName, "%s+", "")
                                local SubNameMatches = StringFind(SubName, CleanQuery, 1, true) ~= nil
                                    or StringFind(TaglessSubName, CleanQuery, 1, true) ~= nil
                                    or (StrippedQuery ~= "" and StringFind(StrippedSubName, StrippedQuery, 1, true) ~= nil)

                                local SubMatches = 0
                                for _, Feature in (SubSec.Features or { }) do
                                    local FeatureName = string.lower(Feature.Name or "")
                                    local TaglessFeatureName = string.lower(StringGSub(Feature.Name or "", "<[^>]+>", ""))
                                    local StrippedFeatureName = StringGSub(TaglessFeatureName, "%s+", "")
                                    local FlagName = Feature.Flag and string.lower(tostring(Feature.Flag)) or ""
                                    local StrippedFlagName = StringGSub(FlagName, "%s+", "")

                                    local IsMatch = SectionNameMatches or SubNameMatches
                                        or StringFind(FeatureName, CleanQuery, 1, true) ~= nil
                                        or StringFind(TaglessFeatureName, CleanQuery, 1, true) ~= nil
                                        or (StrippedQuery ~= "" and StringFind(StrippedFeatureName, StrippedQuery, 1, true) ~= nil)
                                        or (FlagName ~= "" and (StringFind(FlagName, CleanQuery, 1, true) ~= nil or (StrippedQuery ~= "" and StringFind(StrippedFlagName, StrippedQuery, 1, true) ~= nil)))

                                    if Feature.Instance then
                                        Feature.Instance.Visible = IsMatch
                                    end
                                    if IsMatch then
                                        if Feature.ParentInstance then
                                            Feature.ParentInstance.Visible = true
                                        end
                                        SubMatches = SubMatches + 1
                                        SectionMatches = SectionMatches + 1
                                    end
                                end

                                if SubMatches > 0 or SubNameMatches then
                                    if not FirstMatchingSubSec then
                                        FirstMatchingSubSec = SubSec
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            if SectionMatches > 0 or SectionNameMatches then
                                SectionFrame.Instance.Visible = true
                                if FirstMatchingSubSec then
                                    for _, SubSec in Sec.SectionContents do
                                        if SubSec ~= FirstMatchingSubSec then
                                            if SubSec.Elements and SubSec.Elements["Content"] then
                                                SubSec.Elements["Content"].Instance.Visible = false
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                    if FirstMatchingSubSec.Elements and FirstMatchingSubSec.Elements["Content"] then
                                        FirstMatchingSubSec.Elements["Content"].Instance.Visible = true
                                    end
                                end
                                if Sec.Page then
                                    if Sec.Page.Active then
                                        CurrentActivePageHasMatches = true
                                    end
                                    if not FirstMatchingPage then
                                        FirstMatchingPage = Sec.Page
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                SectionFrame.Instance.Visible = false
                            end
                        else
                            for _, Feature in (Sec.Features or { }) do
                                local FeatureName = string.lower(Feature.Name or "")
                                local TaglessFeatureName = string.lower(StringGSub(Feature.Name or "", "<[^>]+>", ""))
                                local StrippedFeatureName = StringGSub(TaglessFeatureName, "%s+", "")
                                local FlagName = Feature.Flag and string.lower(tostring(Feature.Flag)) or ""
                                local StrippedFlagName = StringGSub(FlagName, "%s+", "")

                                local IsMatch = SectionNameMatches
                                    or StringFind(FeatureName, CleanQuery, 1, true) ~= nil
                                    or StringFind(TaglessFeatureName, CleanQuery, 1, true) ~= nil
                                    or (StrippedQuery ~= "" and StringFind(StrippedFeatureName, StrippedQuery, 1, true) ~= nil)
                                    or (FlagName ~= "" and (StringFind(FlagName, CleanQuery, 1, true) ~= nil or (StrippedQuery ~= "" and StringFind(StrippedFlagName, StrippedQuery, 1, true) ~= nil)))

                                if Feature.Instance then
                                    Feature.Instance.Visible = IsMatch
                                end
                                if IsMatch then
                                    if Feature.ParentInstance then
                                        Feature.ParentInstance.Visible = true
                                    end
                                    SectionMatches = SectionMatches + 1
                                end
                            end

                            if SectionMatches > 0 or SectionNameMatches then
                                SectionFrame.Instance.Visible = true
                                if Sec.Elements["Content"] then
                                    Sec.Elements["Content"].Instance.Visible = true
                                end
                                if Sec.Elements["Chevron"] then
                                    Sec.Elements["Chevron"].Instance.Text = "-"
                                end
                                if Sec.Page then
                                    if Sec.Page.Active then
                                        CurrentActivePageHasMatches = true
                                    end
                                    if not FirstMatchingPage then
                                        FirstMatchingPage = Sec.Page
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                SectionFrame.Instance.Visible = false
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if not IsEmpty and not CurrentActivePageHasMatches and FirstMatchingPage then
                for _, P in Window.Pages do
                    local WantActive = (P == FirstMatchingPage)
                    P.Active = WantActive
                    if P.Elements and P.Elements["Page"] then
                        P.Elements["Page"].Instance.Visible = WantActive
                    end
                    if P.Elements then
                        if P.Elements["Text"] then
                            P.Elements["Text"].Instance.TextTransparency = WantActive and 0 or 0.5
                            P.Elements["Text"].Instance.TextColor3 = WantActive and Library.Theme.Accent or Library.Theme.Text
                        end
                        if P.Elements["AccentLine"] then
                            P.Elements["AccentLine"].Instance.Visible = WantActive
                        end
                        if P.Elements["Hide"] then
                            P.Elements["Hide"].Instance.Visible = WantActive
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local SearchTick = 0
        local LastSearchText = ""

        local SearchDebounce = function()
            SearchTick = SearchTick + 1
            local CurrentTick = SearchTick
            task.delay(0.5, function()
                if SearchTick == CurrentTick then
                    local CurrentText = Items["SearchInput"].Instance.Text
                    if CurrentText ~= LastSearchText then
                        LastSearchText = CurrentText
                        Window:Search(CurrentText)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        Library:Connect(Items["SearchInput"].Instance:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function()
            local CurrentText = Items["SearchInput"].Instance.Text
            if CurrentText == "" then
                SearchTick = SearchTick + 1
                LastSearchText = ""
                Window:Search("")
            else
                SearchDebounce()
            end
        end)

        Items["SearchContainer"]:OnHover(function()
            Items["SearchContainer"]:Tween(CachedHoverTweenInfo, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]})
            Items["SearchContainer"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
        end)

        Items["SearchContainer"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
            Items["SearchContainer"]:Tween(CachedHoverTweenInfo, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Page Background"]})
            Items["SearchContainer"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Page Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
        end)

        Items["SearchInput"]:Connect("Focused", function()
            Items["SearchContainer"]:Tween(CachedHoverTweenInfo, {BorderColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent})
            Items["SearchIcon"]:Tween(CachedHoverTweenInfo, {ImageColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent, ImageTransparency = 0})
        end)

        Items["SearchInput"]:Connect("FocusLost", function(EnterPressed)
            Items["SearchContainer"]:Tween(CachedHoverTweenInfo, {BorderColor3 = Library.Theme.Border})
            Items["SearchIcon"]:Tween(CachedHoverTweenInfo, {ImageColor3 = Library.Theme.Text, ImageTransparency = 0.45})
            if EnterPressed then
                SearchTick = SearchTick + 1
                local CurrentText = Items["SearchInput"].Instance.Text
                LastSearchText = CurrentText
                Window:Search(CurrentText)
            end
        end)

        Library:Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(Input, Processed)
            if Processed or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
            local InputKey = tostring(Input.KeyCode)
            local InputType = tostring(Input.UserInputType)

            local MenuKey = Library.MenuKeybind
            local FlagData = Library.Flags["Settings_MenuKeybind"]
            if type(FlagData) == "table" and FlagData.Key then
                MenuKey = FlagData.Key
            end

            if InputKey == MenuKey or InputType == MenuKey then
                Window:SetOpen(not Window.IsOpen)
            end
        end)

        Window.Elements = Items

        return setmetatable(Window, Library)
    end

    Library.Page = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local Page = {
            Window = self,

            Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Page",
            Columns = Data.Columns or Data.columns or 2,

            HasSubtabs = Data.Subtabs or Data.subtabs or false,

            Active = false,
            ColumnsData = { },
            Elements = { }
        }

        local IsHorizontal = Page.Window.TabLayout == "Horizontal"

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Inactive"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Page.Window.Elements["Pages"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = IsHorizontal and UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0) or UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 24),
                BorderSizePixel = IsHorizontal and 0 or 2,
                TextSize = 14,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(30, 30, 35)
            })  Items["Inactive"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Page Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                Color = Library.Theme.Outline,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            if IsHorizontal then
                Items["Hide"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                    Visible = false,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 1),
                    Name = "\0",
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 2),
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 2),
                    ZIndex = 5,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(20, 20, 25)
                })  Items["Hide"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background"})

                Items["MiscPixel1"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Hide"].Instance,
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1),
                    Name = "\0",
                    Position = UDim2New(0, -1, 0, 1),
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    ZIndex = 5,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
                })

                Items["MiscPixel2"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Hide"].Instance,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0),
                    Name = "\0",
                    Position = UDim2New(1, 1, 0, 1),
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1),
                    ZIndex = 5,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
                })
            else
                Items["AccentLine"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                    Visible = false,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0.5),
                    Name = "\0",
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 2, 1, -4),
                    ZIndex = 2,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(235, 157, 255)
                })  Items["AccentLine"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Accent"})

                Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                    Parent = Items["AccentLine"].Instance,
                    Rotation = 90,
                    Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0),
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0),
                        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
                    })
                })
            end

            Items["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                TextTransparency = 0.47999998927116394,
                Text = Page.Name,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = IsHorizontal and UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0) or UDim2New(1, -10, 1, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = IsHorizontal and UDim2New(0, 0, 0, -1) or UDim2New(0, 8, 0, -1),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                TextXAlignment = IsHorizontal and Enum.TextXAlignment.Center or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Text"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["UIGradient"] = Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                Rotation = IsHorizontal and 90 or 0,
                Color = IsHorizontal and RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(138, 138, 138))} or RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(108, 108, 108))}
            })

            Items["Page"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Page.Window.Elements["Content"].Instance,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                Visible = false
            })

            if not Page.HasSubtabs then
                Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                    Parent = Items["Page"].Instance,
                    FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
                    HorizontalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill,
                    SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
                    VerticalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill
                })

                for Index = 1, Page.Columns do
                    local NewColumn = Instances:Create("ScrollingFrame", {
                        Parent = Items["Page"].Instance,
                        ScrollBarImageColor3 = FromRGB(235, 157, 255),
                        Active = true,
                        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                        ScrollBarThickness = 1,
                        Name = "\0",
                        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                        Size = UDim2New(0, 100, 0, 100),
                        BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                        BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                        BorderSizePixel = 0,
                        BottomImage = Library:GetImage("Scrollbar"),
                        MidImage = Library:GetImage("Scrollbar"),
                        TopImage = Library:GetImage("Scrollbar"),
                        CanvasSize = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0)
                    })  NewColumn:AddToTheme({ScrollBarImageColor3 = "Accent"})

                    Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                        Parent = NewColumn.Instance,
                        PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 6),
                        PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 4),
                        PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 6),
                        PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 6)
                    })

                    Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                        Parent = NewColumn.Instance,
                        Padding = UDimNew(0, 12),
                        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                    })

                    Page.ColumnsData[Index] = NewColumn
                end
            else
                Items["Columns"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Page"].Instance,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 45),
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                    Size = UDim2New(1, -14, 1, -52),
                    BorderSizePixel = 2,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20)
                })  Items["Columns"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

                Items["SubTabs"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Page"].Instance,
                    Name = "\0",
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 7),
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Size = UDim2New(1, -14, 0, 35),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                })

                Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                    Parent = Items["SubTabs"].Instance,
                    FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
                    HorizontalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill,
                    Padding = UDimNew(0, 6),
                    SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                })
            end
        end

        local Debounce = false

        function Page:Turn(Bool)
            if Debounce then
                return
            end

            Page.Active = Bool

            Debounce = true

            if Bool then
                Items["Page"].Instance.Visible = true

                Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent, TextTransparency = 0})
                if IsHorizontal then
                    Items["Hide"].Instance.Visible = true
                else
                    Items["AccentLine"].Instance.Visible = true
                end

                Items["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Accent"})
            else
                Items["Page"].Instance.Visible = false
                Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text, TextTransparency = 0.5})
                if IsHorizontal then
                    Items["Hide"].Instance.Visible = false
                else
                    Items["AccentLine"].Instance.Visible = false
                end

                Items["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})
            end

            task.delay(0.1, function()
                Debounce = false
            end)
        end
        Items["Inactive"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            for Index, Value in Page.Window.Pages do
                Value:Turn(Value == Page)
            end
        end)

        if #Page.Window.Pages == 0 then
            Page:Turn(true)
        end

        Page.Elements = Items

        TableInsert(Page.Window.Pages, Page)
        return setmetatable(Page, Library.Pages)
    end

    Library.Pages.SubPage = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local RawIcon = Data.Icon or Data.icon

        -- Resolve CustomAsset icons: Icon = "CustomAsset:Search.png" or "CustomAsset:/path/to/file.png"
        local ResolvedIconImage
        if RawIcon and type(RawIcon) == "string" then
            local customPath = RawIcon:match("^CustomAsset:(.+)$")
            if customPath then
                -- If the path starts with / treat it as absolute, otherwise relative to Assets folder
                if customPath:sub(1, 1) == "/" then
                    pcall(function() ResolvedIconImage = getcustomasset(customPath) end)
                else
                    pcall(function() ResolvedIconImage = getcustomasset(Library.Folders.Assets .. "/" .. customPath) end)
                end
            end
        end

        local SubPage = {
            Window = self.Window,
            Page = self,

            Name = Data.Name or Data.name,
            Icon = RawIcon or "9080568477801",
            IconImage = ResolvedIconImage,  -- set if CustomAsset was used
            Columns = Data.Columns or Data.columns or 2,

            Active = false,
            ColumnsData = { },
            Elements = { }
        }

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Inactive"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = SubPage.Page.Elements["SubTabs"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, -2),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                TextSize = 14,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(30, 30, 35)
            })  Items["Inactive"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Page Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            Items["Hide"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                Visible = false,
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 1),
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 2),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 2),
                ZIndex = 5,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(20, 20, 25)
            })  Items["Hide"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background"})

            Items["MiscPixel1"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Hide"].Instance,
                Size = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1),
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, -1, 0, 1),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                ZIndex = 5,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            })

            Items["MiscPixel2"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Hide"].Instance,
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0),
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(1, 1, 0, 1),
                Size = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1),
                ZIndex = 5,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            })

            if SubPage.Name then
                Items["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                    Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                    TextTransparency = 0.47999998927116394,
                    Text = SubPage.Name,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, -1),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    TextSize = 12,
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                })  Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

                Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = Items["Text"].Instance,
                    LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                    Name = "\0"
                }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})
            else
                -- Use resolved CustomAsset image or fall back to rbxassetid
                local iconImage = SubPage.IconImage or ("rbxassetid://" .. SubPage.Icon)
                Items["Icon"] = Instances:Create("ImageLabel", {
                    Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                    ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                    ImageTransparency = 0.35,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Name = "\0",
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0.5, 0.5),
                    Image = iconImage,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 30, 0, 30),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                })  Items["Icon"]:AddToTheme({ImageColor3 = "Text"})
            end

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Inactive"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(138, 138, 138))}
            })

            Items["Subtab"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = SubPage.Page.Elements["Columns"].Instance,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["Subtab"].Instance,
                PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 6),
                PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 6),
                PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 6)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["Subtab"].Instance,
                FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
                HorizontalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill,
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
                VerticalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill
            })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Subtab"].Instance,
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32),
                Name = "\0",
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            for Index = 1, SubPage.Columns do
                local NewColumn = Instances:Create("ScrollingFrame", {
                    Parent = Items["Subtab"].Instance,
                    ScrollBarImageColor3 = FromRGB(235, 157, 255),
                    Active = true,
                    AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                    ScrollBarThickness = 1,
                    Name = "\0",
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 100, 0, 100),
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    CanvasSize = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0)
                })  NewColumn:AddToTheme({ScrollBarImageColor3 = "Accent"})

                Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                    Parent = NewColumn.Instance,
                    PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 6),
                    PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 4),
                    PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 6),
                    PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 6)
                })

                Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                    Parent = NewColumn.Instance,
                    Padding = UDimNew(0, 12),
                    SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                })

                SubPage.ColumnsData[Index] = NewColumn
            end
        end

        local Debounce = false

        function SubPage:Turn(Bool)
            if Debounce then
                return
            end

            SubPage.Active = Bool

            Debounce = true

            if Bool then
                Items["Subtab"].Instance.Visible = true

                if Items["Icon"] then
                    Items["Icon"]:Tween(nil, {ImageColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent, ImageTransparency = 0})
                    Items["Icon"]:ChangeItemTheme({ImageColor3 = "Accent"})
                end
                if Items["Text"] then
                    Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent, TextTransparency = 0})
                    Items["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Accent"})
                end
                Items["Hide"].Instance.Visible = true

                Items["Inactive"].Instance.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 1)
            else
                Items["Subtab"].Instance.Visible = false
                if Items["Icon"] then
                    Items["Icon"]:Tween(nil, {ImageColor3 = Library.Theme.Text, ImageTransparency = 0.35})
                    Items["Icon"]:ChangeItemTheme({ImageColor3 = "Text"})
                end
                if Items["Text"] then
                    Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text, TextTransparency = 0.5})
                    Items["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})
                end
                Items["Hide"].Instance.Visible = false

                Items["Inactive"].Instance.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, -2)
            end

            task.delay(0.1, function()
                Debounce = false
            end)
        end

        Items["Inactive"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            for Index, Value in SubPage.Window.SubPages do
                Value:Turn(Value == SubPage)
            end
        end)

        if #SubPage.Window.SubPages == 0 then
            SubPage:Turn(true)
        end

        SubPage.Elements = Items

        TableInsert(SubPage.Window.SubPages, SubPage)
        return setmetatable(SubPage, Library.Pages)
    end

    Library.Pages.Section = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local Section = {
            Window = self.Window,
            Page = self,

            Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Section",
            Side = Data.Side or Data.side or 1,

            Collapsible = Data.Collapsible or Data.collapsible or false,
            Collapsed = Data.Collapsed or Data.collapsed or false,

            Elements = { }
        }

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Section"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Section.Page.ColumnsData[Section.Side].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 25),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(27, 27, 32),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(20, 20, 25)
            })  Items["Section"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Inline", BorderColor3 = "Outline"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Section"].Instance,
                Color = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Name = "\0",
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["Section"].Instance,
                PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 6)
            })

            Items["AccentLine"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Section"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 2),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(235, 157, 255)
            })  Items["AccentLine"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Accent"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["AccentLine"].Instance,
                Rotation = 0,
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
                })
            })

            if Section.Collapsible then
                Items["HeaderButton"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                    Parent = Items["Section"].Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Text = "",
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 19),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                })

                Items["Chevron"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                    Parent = Items["HeaderButton"].Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                    Text = "-",
                    Name = "\0",
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 12, 0, 15),
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0),
                    Position = UDim2New(1, -4, 0, 2),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    TextSize = 12,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                })
                Items["Chevron"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

                Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = Items["Chevron"].Instance,
                    LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                    Name = "\0"
                }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})
            end

            Items["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Section.Collapsible and Items["HeaderButton"].Instance or Items["Section"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = Section.Name,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, -24, 0, 15),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 4, 0, 2),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                RichText = true,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Text"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["Content"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Section"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 21),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, -14, 0, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["Content"].Instance,
                PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 4),
                PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 0),
                PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 0),
                PaddingRight = UDimNew(0, 0)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["Content"].Instance,
                Padding = UDimNew(0, 6),
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            })
        end

        if Section.Collapsible then
            local CollapseSection = function(Bool)
                Section.Collapsed = Bool
                Items["Content"].Instance.Visible = not Bool
                Items["Chevron"].Instance.Text = Bool and "+" or "-"

                if Bool then
                    Items["Chevron"]:Tween(nil, { TextColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"] })
                else
                    Items["Chevron"]:Tween(nil, { TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text })
                end
            end

            function Section:SetCollapsed(Bool)
                CollapseSection(Bool)
            end

            function Section:ToggleCollapse()
                CollapseSection(not Section.Collapsed)
            end

            Items["HeaderButton"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                CollapseSection(not Section.Collapsed)
            end)

            Items["HeaderButton"]:OnHover(function()
                Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, { TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent })
                Items["Chevron"]:Tween(nil, { TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent })
            end)

            Items["HeaderButton"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
                Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, { TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text })
                Items["Chevron"]:Tween(nil, { TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text })
            end)

            if Section.Collapsed then
                CollapseSection(true)
            end
        end

        Section.Features = { }
        Section.Elements = Items

        Section.Window.Sections[#Section.Window.Sections+1] = Section
        return setmetatable(Section, Library.Sections)
    end

    Library.Pages.MultiSection = function(self, Data)
        local MultiSection = {
            Window = self.Window,
            Page = self,

            Sections = Data.Sections or Data.sections or { "Section 1", "Section 2", "Section 3" },
            Side = Data.Side or Data.side or 1,

            SectionContents = { },

            Elements = { }
        }

        local Items = { } do
            Items["MultiSection"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = MultiSection.Page.ColumnsData[MultiSection.Side].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 25),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(27, 27, 32),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(20, 20, 25)
            })  Items["MultiSection"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Inline", BorderColor3 = "Outline"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["MultiSection"].Instance,
                Color = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Name = "\0",
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["MultiSection"].Instance,
                PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 6)
            })

            Items["AccentLine"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["MultiSection"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 2),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(235, 157, 255)
            })  Items["AccentLine"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Accent"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["AccentLine"].Instance,
                Rotation = 0,
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
                })
            })

            Items["Sections"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["MultiSection"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 9),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, -14, 0, 19),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["Sections"].Instance,
                FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
                HorizontalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill,
                Padding = UDimNew(0, 5),
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            })

            Items["Content"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["MultiSection"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 33),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Size = UDim2New(1, -14, 0, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20)
            })
        end

        for Index, Value in MultiSection.Sections do
            local NewSection = {
                Window = MultiSection.Window,
                Page = MultiSection.Page,
                MultiSection = MultiSection,

                Name = Value,

                Elements = { },

                Active = false,
            }

            local SubItems = { } do
                SubItems["Inactive"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                    Parent = Items["Sections"].Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                    Text = "",
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                    BorderSizePixel = 2,
                    TextSize = 14,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(30, 30, 35)
                })  SubItems["Inactive"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Page Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

                SubItems["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                    Parent = SubItems["Inactive"].Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                    TextTransparency = 0.48,
                    Text = NewSection.Name,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, -1),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    TextSize = 12,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                })  SubItems["Text"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

                Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = SubItems["Text"].Instance,
                    LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                    Name = "\0"
                }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

                SubItems["Hide"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = SubItems["Inactive"].Instance,
                    Visible = false,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 1),
                    Name = "\0",
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 0),
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 3),
                    ZIndex = 2,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20)
                })  SubItems["Hide"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background"})

                SubItems["MiscPixel1"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = SubItems["Hide"].Instance,
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1),
                    Name = "\0",
                    Position = UDim2New(0, -1, 0, 1),
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    ZIndex = 2,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
                })  SubItems["MiscPixel1"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Outline"})

                SubItems["MiscPixel2"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = SubItems["Hide"].Instance,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0),
                    Name = "\0",
                    Position = UDim2New(1, 1, 0, 1),
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 1, 0, 1),
                    ZIndex = 2,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
                })  SubItems["MiscPixel2"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Outline"})

                Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = SubItems["Inactive"].Instance,
                    ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                    LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
                }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

                Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                    Parent = SubItems["Inactive"].Instance,
                    Rotation = 90,
                    Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(108, 108, 108))}
                })

                SubItems["Content"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Content"].Instance,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Name = "\0",
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                    Visible = false,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                })

                Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                    Parent = SubItems["Content"].Instance,
                    PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 4),
                    PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 0)
                })

                Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                    Parent = SubItems["Content"].Instance,
                    Padding = UDimNew(0, 5),
                    SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                })
            end

            local Debounce = false

            function NewSection:Turn(Bool)
                if Debounce then
                    return
                end

                NewSection.Active = Bool

                Debounce = true

                if Bool then
                    SubItems["Content"].Instance.Visible = true

                    SubItems["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent, TextTransparency = 0})

                    SubItems["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Accent"})
                else
                    SubItems["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text, TextTransparency = 0.5})

                    SubItems["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})
                end

                local Descendants = SubItems["Content"].Instance:GetDescendants()
                TableInsert(Descendants, SubItems["Content"].Instance)

                if not Bool then
                    SubItems["Content"].Instance.Visible = false
                end
                task.delay(0.1, function()
                    Debounce = false
                end)
            end

            SubItems["Inactive"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                for Index, Value in MultiSection.SectionContents do
                    Value:Turn(Value == NewSection)
                end
            end)

            if #MultiSection.SectionContents == 0 then
                NewSection:Turn(true)
            end

            NewSection.Features = { }
            NewSection.Elements = SubItems

            MultiSection.SectionContents[#MultiSection.SectionContents+1] = setmetatable(NewSection, Library.Sections)
        end

        MultiSection.Features = { }
        MultiSection.SectionContents[1]:Turn(true)
        MultiSection.Window.Sections[#MultiSection.Window.Sections+1] = MultiSection
        return TableUnpack(MultiSection.SectionContents)
    end

    Library.Pages.ScrollableSection = function(self, Data)

    end

    Library.Sections.RegisterFeature = function(self, Data)
        if not self.Window or not self.Window.Features then
            return
        end

        local Feature = {
            Name = Data.Name or "Feature",
            Type = Data.Type or "Element",
            Section = self,
            Page = self.Page,
            SubPage = self.SubPage,
            Instance = Data.Instance,
            ParentInstance = Data.ParentInstance,
            Object = Data.Object,
            Flag = Data.Flag
        }

        TableInsert(self.Window.Features, Feature)

        if self.MultiSection then
            if not self.MultiSection.Features then
                self.MultiSection.Features = { }
            end
            TableInsert(self.MultiSection.Features, Feature)
        end

        if not self.Features then
            self.Features = { }
        end
        TableInsert(self.Features, Feature)

        return Feature
    end
