    Library.Sections.Toggle = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local Toggle = {
            Window = self.Window,
            Page = self.Page,
            Section = self,

            Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Toggle",
            Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
            Default = Data.Default or Data.default or false,
            Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,

            Risky = Data.Risky or Data.risky or false,
            RiskyText = Data.RiskyText or Data.riskytext or "Enabling this feature may have unintended consequences. Are you sure you want to continue?",
            RiskConfirmed = false,

            Value = false,
            Class = "Toggle",

            Count = 0
        }

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Toggle"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Toggle.Section.Elements["Content"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 16),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 14,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Items["Indicator"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Toggle"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Size = UDim2New(0, 10, 0, 10),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(33, 33, 36)
            })  Items["Indicator"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Indicator"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Indicator"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100))}
            })

            Items["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Toggle"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = Toggle.Risky and FromRGB(220, 90, 90) or FromRGB(200, 200, 208),
                TextTransparency = 0.48,
                Text = Toggle.Risky and ("[!] " .. Toggle.Name) or Toggle.Name,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, -43, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2New(0, 18, 0, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            if Toggle.Risky then
                Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "RiskyText" })
            else
                Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })
            end

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Text"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["RightContainer"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Toggle"].Instance,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2New(1, -4, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 14),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["RightContainer"].Instance,
                FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
                HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
                Padding = UDimNew(0, 3.3),
                VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
            })

            Items["Toggle"]:OnHover(function()
                if Toggle.Value then
                    return
                end

                Items["Indicator"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]})
                Items["Indicator"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
            end)

            Items["Toggle"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
                if Toggle.Value then
                    return
                end

                Items["Indicator"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Element"]})
                Items["Indicator"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
            end)
        end

        function Toggle:Get()
            return Toggle.Value
        end

        function Toggle:Set(Bool)
            if type(Bool) == "table" and Bool.v ~= nil then
                Bool = Bool.v
            end
            if Bool == nil then
                Toggle.Value = not Toggle.Value
            else
                Toggle.Value = Bool and true or false
            end

            Library.Flags[Toggle.Flag] = Toggle.Value

            if Toggle.Value then
                Items["Indicator"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Accent"})

                Items["Indicator"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent})
                Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextTransparency = 0})
            else
                Items["Indicator"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element"})

                Items["Indicator"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element})
                Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextTransparency = 0.48})
            end

            if Toggle.Callback then
                Library:SafeCall(Toggle.Callback, Toggle.Value)
            end
        end

        function Toggle:SetVisiblity(Bool)
            Items["Toggle"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end
        Toggle.SetVisibility = Toggle.SetVisiblity

        function Toggle:Colorpicker(Data)
            Data = Data or { }

            local Colorpicker = {
                Window = self.Window,
                Tab = self.Tab,
                Section = self.Section,

                Parent = Items["Toggle"],
                Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Colorpicker",
                Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
                Default = Data.Default or Data.default or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,
                Alpha = Data.Alpha or Data.alpha or false,
                Count = Toggle.Count,

                FadeSpeed = self.Window.FadeSpeed
            }

            Toggle.Count += 1
            Colorpicker.Count = Toggle.Count

            local Extension = Library:CreateColorpicker(Colorpicker)
            Library.Flags[Colorpicker.Flag] = Extension

            if Extension.Items and Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"] then
                Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance.Parent = Items["RightContainer"].Instance
                Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0.5)
                Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
                Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance.LayoutOrder = Toggle.Count
            end

            Extension.Colorpicker = Toggle.Colorpicker
            Extension.Window = self.Window
            Extension.Tab = self.Tab
            Extension.Section = self.Section

            Toggle.Section:RegisterFeature({
                Name = Toggle.Name .. " " .. (Data.Name or "Color"),
                Type = "Colorpicker",
                Instance = Extension.Items and Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"] and Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance or nil,
                ParentInstance = Items["Toggle"].Instance,
                Object = Colorpicker,
                Flag = Colorpicker.Flag
            })

            return Extension
        end

        function Toggle:Keybind(Data)
            Data = Data or { }

            local Keybind = {
                Window = self.Window,
                Tab = self.Tab,
                Section = self.Section,

                Parent = Items["Toggle"],
                Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Keybind",
                Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
                Default = Data.Default ~= nil and Data.Default or Data.default or nil,
                Mode = Data.Mode or Data.mode or "Toggle",
                Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,
            }

            local Extension = Library:CreateKeybind(Keybind)
            Library.Flags[Keybind.Flag] = Extension

            if Extension.Items and Extension.Items["KeyButton"] then
                Extension.Items["KeyButton"].Instance.Parent = Items["RightContainer"].Instance
                Extension.Items["KeyButton"].Instance.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0.5)
                Extension.Items["KeyButton"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
                Extension.Items["KeyButton"].Instance.LayoutOrder = 999
            end

            setmetatable(Keybind, { __index = Extension })

            Toggle.Section:RegisterFeature({
                Name = Toggle.Name .. " " .. (Data.Name or "Keybind"),
                Type = "Keybind",
                Instance = Extension.Items and Extension.Items["KeyButton"] and Extension.Items["KeyButton"].Instance or nil,
                ParentInstance = Items["Toggle"].Instance,
                Object = Keybind,
                Flag = Keybind.Flag
            })

            return Extension, Keybind
        end

        Items["Toggle"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            if Toggle.Risky and not Toggle.Value and not Toggle.RiskConfirmed then
                Library:RiskyPopup({
                    Window = Toggle.Window,
                    Parent = Toggle.Window.Elements["MainFrame"],
                    Text = Toggle.RiskyText,
                    OnConfirm = function()
                        Toggle.RiskConfirmed = true
                        Toggle:Set(true)
                    end
                })
                return
            end

            Toggle:Set()
        end)

        if Toggle.Default then
            Toggle:Set(Toggle.Default)
        end

        Library.Defaults[Toggle.Flag] = Toggle.Default
        Library.SetFlags[Toggle.Flag] = function(Value)
            Toggle:Set(Value)
        end

        if Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip then
            Library:AddTooltip(Items["Toggle"].Instance, Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip)
        end

        self:RegisterFeature({
            Name = Toggle.Name,
            Type = "Toggle",
            Instance = Items["Toggle"].Instance,
            Object = Toggle,
            Flag = Toggle.Flag
        })

        return Toggle
    end

    Library.Sections.ButtonGroup = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or {}

        local ButtonGroup = {
            Window = self.Window,
            Page = self.Page,
            Section = self,
            Buttons = {},
        }

        local ButtonList = Data.Buttons or Data.buttons or {}
        local Count = math.min(#ButtonList, 3)

        if Count == 0 then
            return ButtonGroup
        end

        local Items = {} do
            Items["Container"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = ButtonGroup.Section.Elements["Content"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            })

            local Row1, Row2
            if Count == 1 then
                Row1 = Items["Container"]
                Row2 = nil
            else
                Row1 = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Container"].Instance,
                    Name = "\0",
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 17),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                })
                Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                    Parent = Row1.Instance,
                    FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
                    HorizontalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill,
                    Padding = UDimNew(0, 4),
                    SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
                })
            end

            if Count == 3 then
                Row2 = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Container"].Instance,
                    Name = "\0",
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 17),
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 21),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                })
            end

            local function BuildButton(BtnData, Parent, Width)
                local Btn = {
                    Window = ButtonGroup.Window,
                    Page = ButtonGroup.Page,
                    Section = ButtonGroup.Section,
                    Name = BtnData.Name or BtnData.name or "Button",
                    Callback = BtnData.Callback or BtnData.callback or function() end,
                    Risky = BtnData.Risky or BtnData.risky or false,
                    RiskyText = BtnData.RiskyText or BtnData.riskytext or "Performing this action may have unintended consequences. Are you sure you want to continue?",
                    RiskConfirmed = false,
                    Flag = BtnData.Flag or BtnData.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
                }

                local BtnItems = {}
                BtnItems["Button"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                    Parent = Parent,
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Size = Width or UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                    BorderSizePixel = 2,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(33, 33, 36),
                })
                BtnItems["Button"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

                Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                    Parent = BtnItems["Button"].Instance,
                    Rotation = 90,
                    Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100))}
                })

                Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = BtnItems["Button"].Instance,
                    ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                    LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
                }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

                BtnItems["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                    Parent = BtnItems["Button"].Instance,
                    FontFace = Library.Font,
                    TextColor3 = Btn.Risky and FromRGB(220, 90, 90) or FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                    BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    Text = Btn.Risky and ("[!] " .. Btn.Name) or Btn.Name,
                    Name = "\0",
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, -1),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    TextSize = 12,
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                })
                if Btn.Risky then
                    BtnItems["Text"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "RiskyText"})
                else
                    BtnItems["Text"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})
                end
                BtnItems["TextBorder"] = Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                    Parent = BtnItems["Text"].Instance,
                    LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                    Name = "\0"
                }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

                BtnItems["Button"]:OnHover(function()
                    BtnItems["Button"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]})
                    BtnItems["Button"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
                end)
                BtnItems["Button"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
                    BtnItems["Button"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Element"]})
                    BtnItems["Button"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
                end)

                function Btn:Press()
                    Library:SafeCall(Btn.Callback)
                    BtnItems["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Accent"})
                    BtnItems["Button"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Accent"})
                    BtnItems["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent})
                    BtnItems["Button"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent})
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    local RestoreTheme = Btn.Risky and "RiskyText" or "Text"
                    local RestoreColor = Btn.Risky and Library.Theme["RiskyText"] or Library.Theme.Text
                    BtnItems["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = RestoreTheme})
                    BtnItems["Button"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element"})
                    BtnItems["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = RestoreColor})
                    BtnItems["Button"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element})
                end

                function Btn:SetVisiblity(Bool)
                    BtnItems["Button"].Instance.Visible = Bool
                end
                Btn.SetVisibility = Btn.SetVisiblity

                BtnItems["Button"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                    if Btn.Risky and not Btn.RiskConfirmed then
                        Library:RiskyPopup({
                            Window = Btn.Window,
                            Parent = Btn.Window.Elements["MainFrame"],
                            Text = Btn.RiskyText,
                            OnConfirm = function()
                                Btn.RiskConfirmed = true
                                Library.Flags[Btn.Flag] = true
                                Btn:Press()
                            end
                        })
                        return
                    end
                    Btn:Press()
                end)

                if BtnData.Tooltip or BtnData.tooltip then
                    Library:AddTooltip(BtnItems["Button"].Instance, BtnData.Tooltip or BtnData.tooltip)
                end

                Btn.Items = BtnItems

                ButtonGroup.Section:RegisterFeature({
                    Name = Btn.Name,
                    Type = "Button",
                    Instance = BtnItems["Button"].Instance,
                    Object = Btn,
                    Flag = Btn.Flag
                })

                return Btn
            end

            if Count == 1 then
                local b = BuildButton(ButtonList[1], Items["Container"].Instance, UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 17))
                TableInsert(ButtonGroup.Buttons, b)
            elseif Count == 2 then
                local b1 = BuildButton(ButtonList[1], Row1.Instance)
                local b2 = BuildButton(ButtonList[2], Row1.Instance)
                TableInsert(ButtonGroup.Buttons, b1)
                TableInsert(ButtonGroup.Buttons, b2)
            elseif Count == 3 then
                local b1 = BuildButton(ButtonList[1], Row1.Instance)
                local b2 = BuildButton(ButtonList[2], Row1.Instance)
                local b3 = BuildButton(ButtonList[3], Row2.Instance, UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0))
                TableInsert(ButtonGroup.Buttons, b1)
                TableInsert(ButtonGroup.Buttons, b2)
                TableInsert(ButtonGroup.Buttons, b3)
            end
        end

        function ButtonGroup:SetVisibility(Bool)
            Items["Container"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end

        return ButtonGroup
    end

    Library.Sections.Button = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local Button = {
            Window = self.Window,
            Page = self.Page,
            Section = self,

            Name = Data.Name or Data.name,
            Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,

            Risky = Data.Risky or Data.risky or false,
            RiskyText = Data.RiskyText or Data.riskytext or "Performing this action may have unintended consequences. Are you sure you want to continue?",
            RiskConfirmed = false,
            Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
        }

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Button"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Button.Section.Elements["Content"].Instance,
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 17),
                Selectable = false,
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(33, 33, 36)
            })  Items["Button"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Button"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100))}
            })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Button"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            Items["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Button"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = Button.Risky and FromRGB(220, 90, 90) or FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = Button.Risky and ("[!] " .. Button.Name) or Button.Name,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, -1),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            if Button.Risky then
                Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "RiskyText" })
            else
                Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })
            end

            Items["TextBorder"] = Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Text"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["Button"]:OnHover(function()
                Items["Button"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]})
                Items["Button"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
            end)

            Items["Button"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
                Items["Button"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Element"]})
                Items["Button"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
            end)
        end

        function Button:Press()
            Library:SafeCall(Button.Callback)

            Library:ApplyClickGradient(Items["Button"].Instance)

            Items["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Accent"})
            Items["Button"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Accent"})

            Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent})
            Items["Button"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent})

            task.wait(0.1)

            local RestoreTheme = Button.Risky and "RiskyText" or "Text"
            local RestoreColor = Button.Risky and Library.Theme["RiskyText"] or Library.Theme.Text

            Items["Text"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = RestoreTheme})
            Items["Button"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element"})

            Items["Text"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = RestoreColor})
            Items["Button"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element})
        end

        function Button:SetVisiblity(Bool)
            Items["Button"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end
        Button.SetVisibility = Button.SetVisiblity

        Items["Button"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            if Button.Risky and not Button.RiskConfirmed then
                Library:RiskyPopup({
                    Window = Button.Window,
                    Parent = Button.Window.Elements["MainFrame"],
                    Text = Button.RiskyText,
                    OnConfirm = function()
                        Button.RiskConfirmed = true
                        Library.Flags[Button.Flag] = true
                        Button:Press()
                    end
                })
                return
            end

            Button:Press()
        end)

        if Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip then
            Library:AddTooltip(Items["Button"].Instance, Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip)
        end

        self:RegisterFeature({
            Name = Button.Name,
            Type = "Button",
            Instance = Items["Button"].Instance,
            Object = Button,
            Flag = Button.Flag
        })

        return Button
    end

    Library.Sections.Slider = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local Slider = {
            Window = self.Window,
            Page = self.Page,
            Section = self,

            Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Slider",
            Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
            Min = Data.Min or Data.min or 0,
            Default = Data.Default or Data.default or 0,
            Max = Data.Max or Data.max or 100,
            Suffix = Data.Suffix or Data.suffix or "",
            Decimals = Data.Decimals or Data.decimals or 1,
            Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,
            Compact = Data.Compact or Data.compact or false,

            Value = 0,
            Sliding = false,
            Class = "Slider",
        }

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Slider"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Slider.Section.Elements["Content"].Instance,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 27),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Items["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Slider"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = Slider.Name,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 13),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Text"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["RealSlider"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Items["Slider"].Instance,
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 1),
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 10),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(33, 33, 36)
            })  Items["RealSlider"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["RealSlider"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["RealSlider"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100))}
            })

            Items["Indicator"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["RealSlider"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(235, 157, 255)
            })  Items["Indicator"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Accent"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Indicator"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100))}
            })

            Items["Value"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["RealSlider"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = "50/100s",
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, -1),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Value"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Value"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            if Slider.Compact then
                Items["Value"]:Clean()
                Items["Value"] = nil

                Items["Slider"].Instance.Size = UDim2New(1,0,0,10)
                Items["Text"].Instance.Parent = Items["RealSlider"].Instance
                Items["Text"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0,0,0,-2)
                Items["Text"].Instance.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            end

            Items["RealSlider"]:OnHover(function()
                Items["RealSlider"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]})
                Items["RealSlider"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
            end)

            Items["RealSlider"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
                Items["RealSlider"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Background"]})
                Items["RealSlider"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
            end)
        end

        function Slider:Set(Value)
            if type(Value) == "table" then
                Value = Value.v ~= nil and Value.v or Value[1] or Value.Value or Value.val
            end
            Value = tonumber(Value) or Slider.Default or Slider.Min
            Slider.Value = MathClamp(Library:Round(Value, Slider.Decimals), Slider.Min, Slider.Max)

            Library.Flags[Slider.Flag] = Slider.Value

            if Slider.Compact then
                Items["Text"].Instance.Text = `{Slider.Name}: {Slider.Value}{Slider.Suffix}`
            else
                Items["Value"].Instance.Text = `{Slider.Value}{Slider.Suffix}`
            end

            Items["Indicator"]:Tween(CachedHoverTweenInfo, {Size = UDim2New((Slider.Value - Slider.Min) / (Slider.Max - Slider.Min), 0, 1, 0)})

            if Slider.Callback then
                Library:SafeCall(Slider.Callback, Slider.Value)
            end
        end

        function Slider:Get()
            return Slider.Value
        end

        function Slider:SetVisibility(Bool)
            Items["Slider"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end
        Slider.SetVisiblity = Slider.SetVisibility

        local SliderMoveConn
        local SliderEndConn

        Items["RealSlider"]:Connect("InputBegan", function(Input)
            if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                Slider.Sliding = true

                local MousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                local SizeX = (MousePos.X - Items["RealSlider"].Instance.AbsolutePosition.X) / Items["RealSlider"].Instance.AbsoluteSize.X
                local Value = ((Slider.Max - Slider.Min) * SizeX) + Slider.Min
                Slider:Set(Value)

                if SliderMoveConn then SliderMoveConn:Disconnect() SliderMoveConn = nil end
                if SliderEndConn then SliderEndConn:Disconnect() SliderEndConn = nil end

                SliderMoveConn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(moveInput)
                    if moveInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and Slider.Sliding then
                        local mPos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                        local szX = (mPos.X - Items["RealSlider"].Instance.AbsolutePosition.X) / Items["RealSlider"].Instance.AbsoluteSize.X
                        local val = ((Slider.Max - Slider.Min) * szX) + Slider.Min
                        Slider:Set(val)
                    end
                end)

                SliderEndConn = Input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        Slider.Sliding = false
                        if SliderMoveConn then SliderMoveConn:Disconnect() SliderMoveConn = nil end
                        if SliderEndConn then SliderEndConn:Disconnect() SliderEndConn = nil end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        Items["RealSlider"]:Connect("InputEnded", function(Input)
            if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                Slider.Sliding = false
                if SliderMoveConn then SliderMoveConn:Disconnect() SliderMoveConn = nil end
                if SliderEndConn then SliderEndConn:Disconnect() SliderEndConn = nil end
            end
        end)

        if Slider.Default then
            Slider:Set(Slider.Default)
        end

        Library.Defaults[Slider.Flag] = Slider.Default
        Library.SetFlags[Slider.Flag] = function(Value)
            Slider:Set(Value)
        end
        if Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip then
            Library:AddTooltip(Items["Slider"].Instance, Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip)
        end

        self:RegisterFeature({
            Name = Slider.Name,
            Type = "Slider",
            Instance = Items["Slider"].Instance,
            Object = Slider,
            Flag = Slider.Flag
        })

        return Slider
    end

    Library.Sections.Dropdown = function(self, Data)

            })  Items["RealDropdown"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["RealDropdown"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100))}
            })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {

            Items["Open"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Items["RealDropdown"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = "+",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
                Position = UDim2New(0, -4, 0, -1),

                Parent = Items["RealDropdown"].Instance,

                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = "--",
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, -25, 1, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 5, 0, -1),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Value"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Value"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["OptionHolder"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Dropdown"].Instance,
                Visible = false,
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 5),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(20, 20, 25)
            })  Items["OptionHolder"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Inline", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["OptionHolder"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",

            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["OptionHolder"].Instance,
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            })

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["OptionHolder"].Instance,

            })

            Items["RealDropdown"]:OnHover(function()

            end)

            Items["RealDropdown"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
                Items["RealDropdown"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Background"]})
                Items["RealDropdown"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
            end)
        end

        function Dropdown:Set(Option)
            if type(Option) == "table" and Option.v ~= nil then
                Option = Option.v
            end
            if Dropdown.Multi then
                if type(Option) ~= "table" then
                    if type(Option) == "string" then
                        Option = { Option }
                    else
                        Option = {}
                    end
                end

                local validValues = {}
                for _, Value in ipairs(Option) do
                    local strVal = tostring(Value)
                    local matchedKey = nil
                    for k, _ in pairs(Dropdown.Options) do
                        if tostring(k):lower() == strVal:lower() then
                            matchedKey = tostring(k)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    if matchedKey and not TableFind(validValues, matchedKey) then
                        TableInsert(validValues, matchedKey)
                    end
                end

                Dropdown.Value = validValues

                for key, OptionData in pairs(Dropdown.Options) do
                    if TableFind(validValues, tostring(key)) then
                        OptionData.Selected = true
                        OptionData:Toggle("Active")
                    else
                        OptionData.Selected = false
                        OptionData:Toggle("Inactive")
                    end
                end

                Library.Flags[Dropdown.Flag] = Dropdown.Value

                local displayStrings = {}
                for _, v in ipairs(validValues) do
                    TableInsert(displayStrings, tostring(v))
                end
                Items["Value"].Instance.Text = #displayStrings > 0 and TableConcat(displayStrings, ", ") or "--"
            else
                if type(Option) == "table" then
                    Option = Option[1] or Option.Value or Option.Name or ""
                end
                Option = tostring(Option or "")

                local OptionData = Dropdown.Options[Option]
                if not OptionData then
                    for k, v in pairs(Dropdown.Options) do
                        if tostring(k):lower() == Option:lower() then
                            OptionData = v
                            Option = tostring(k)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end

                if not OptionData then
                    Items["Value"].Instance.Text = Option ~= "" and Option or "--"
                    return
                end

                Dropdown.Value = OptionData.Name

                OptionData.Selected = true
                OptionData:Toggle("Active")

                for Index, Value in Dropdown.Options do
                    if Value ~= OptionData then
                        Value.Selected = false
                        Value:Toggle("Inactive")
                    end
                end

                Library.Flags[Dropdown.Flag] = Dropdown.Value

                Items["Value"].Instance.Text = tostring(Option)
            end

            if Dropdown.Callback then
                Library:SafeCall(Dropdown.Callback, Dropdown.Value)
            end
        end

        function Dropdown:Get()
            return Dropdown.Value
        end

        function Dropdown:SetVisibility(Bool)
            Items["Dropdown"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end

        function Dropdown:Add(Option)
            local ParentZ = Items["OptionHolder"].Instance.ZIndex
            local OptionButton = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Items["OptionHolder"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 15),
                ZIndex = ParentZ + 4,
                TextSize = 14,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            local OptionText = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = OptionButton.Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                TextTransparency = 0.48,
                Text = Option,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, -5, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2New(0, 5, 0, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = ParentZ + 5,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            OptionText:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = OptionText.Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            local OptionData = {
                Selected = false,
                Name = Option,
                Text = OptionText,
                Button = OptionButton
            }

            function OptionData:Toggle(State)
                if State == "Active" then
                    OptionData.Text:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Accent"})
                    OptionData.Text:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent, TextTransparency = 0})
                else
                    OptionData.Text:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})
                    OptionData.Text:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text, TextTransparency = 0.48})
                end
            end

            function OptionData:Set()
                OptionData.Selected = not OptionData.Selected

                if Dropdown.Multi then
                    local Index = TableFind(Dropdown.Value, OptionData.Name)

                    if Index then
                        TableRemove(Dropdown.Value, Index)
                    else
                        TableInsert(Dropdown.Value, OptionData.Name)
                    end

                    Library.Flags[Dropdown.Flag] = Dropdown.Value

                    OptionData:Toggle(Index and "Inactive" or "Active")

                    local TextFormat = #Dropdown.Value > 0 and TableConcat(Dropdown.Value, ", ") or "--"

                    Items["Value"].Instance.Text = TextFormat
                else
                    if OptionData.Selected then
                        Dropdown.Value = OptionData.Name

                        Library.Flags[Dropdown.Flag] = Dropdown.Value

                        OptionData:Toggle("Active")
                        Items["Value"].Instance.Text = OptionData.Name

                        for Index, Value in Dropdown.Options do
                            if Value ~= OptionData then
                                Value.Selected = false
                                Value:Toggle("Inactive")
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        Dropdown.Value = nil
                        Library.Flags[Dropdown.Flag] = nil

                        OptionData:Toggle("Inactive")
                        Items["Value"].Instance.Text = "--"
                    end
                end

                if Dropdown.Callback then
                    Library:SafeCall(Dropdown.Callback, Dropdown.Value)
                end
            end

            OptionButton:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                OptionData:Set()
            end)

            Dropdown.Options[Option] = OptionData
            return OptionData
        end

        function Dropdown:Remove(Option)
            if Dropdown.Options[Option] then
                Dropdown.Options[Option].Button:Clean()
            end
        end

        function Dropdown:Refresh(List)
            for Index, Value in Dropdown.Options do
                Dropdown:Remove(Value.Name)
            end

            for Index, Value in List do
                Dropdown:Add(Value)
            end
        end

        local Debounce = false

        function Dropdown:SetOpen(Bool)
            if Debounce then
                return
            end

            Dropdown.IsOpen = Bool

            Debounce = true

            if Bool then
                Items["OptionHolder"].Instance.Visible = true
                local TopZ = Items["Dropdown"].Instance.ZIndex + 10
                Items["OptionHolder"].Instance.ZIndex = TopZ
                Items["Open"].Instance.Text = "-"
                Items["Open"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0, -5, 0, -1)
            else
                Items["Open"].Instance.Text = "+"
                Items["Open"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0, -4, 0, -1)
            end

            local Descendants = Items["OptionHolder"].Instance:GetDescendants()
            TableInsert(Descendants, Items["OptionHolder"].Instance)

            local TopZ = Items["Dropdown"].Instance.ZIndex + 10
            for Index, Value in Descendants do
                if not StringFind(Value.ClassName, "UI") then
                    Value.ZIndex = Bool and TopZ or 1
                end
            end

            if not Bool then
                Items["OptionHolder"].Instance.Visible = false
                Items["OptionHolder"].Instance.ZIndex = 1
            end
            task.delay(0.1, function()
                Debounce = false
            end)
        end

        for Index, Value in Dropdown.Items do
            Dropdown:Add(Value)
        end

        Items["Open"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            Dropdown:SetOpen(not Dropdown.IsOpen)
        end)

        if Dropdown.Default then
            Dropdown:Set(Dropdown.Default)
        end

        Library.Defaults[Dropdown.Flag] = Dropdown.Default
        Library.SetFlags[Dropdown.Flag] = function(Value)
            Dropdown:Set(Value)
        end

        if Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip then
            Library:AddTooltip(Items["RealDropdown"].Instance, Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip)
        end

        self:RegisterFeature({
            Name = Dropdown.Name,
            Type = "Dropdown",
            Instance = Items["Dropdown"].Instance,
            Object = Dropdown,
            Flag = Dropdown.Flag
        })

        return Dropdown
    end

    Library.Sections.Colorpicker = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local Colorpicker = {
            Window = self.Window,
            Page = self.Page,
            Section = self,

            Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Colorpicker",
            Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
            Default = Data.Default or Data.default or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,
            Alpha = Data.Alpha or Data.alpha or false,
            Count = 0,
            FadeSpeed = self.Window.FadeSpeed
        }

        local Items = { }
        Items["Holder"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
            Parent = self.Elements["Content"].Instance,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 16),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        })

        Items["Label"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
            Parent = Items["Holder"].Instance,
            FontFace = Library.Font,
            TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208),
            TextTransparency = 0.48,
            Text = Colorpicker.Name,
            Name = "\0",
            Size = UDim2New(1, -43, 1, 0),
            Position = UDim2New(0, 18, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            TextSize = 12,
            BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        })
        Items["Label"]:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })

        Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
            Parent = Items["Label"].Instance,
            LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
            Name = "\0"
        }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

        Colorpicker.Parent = Items["Holder"]

        local Extension = Library:CreateColorpicker(Colorpicker)

        if Extension.Items and Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"] then
            Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0.5)
            Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(1, -2, 0.5, 0)
        end

        function Colorpicker:Set(Color, Alpha)
            Extension:Set(Color, Alpha)
        end

        function Colorpicker:Get()
            return Extension:Get()
        end

        self:RegisterFeature({
            Name = Colorpicker.Name,
            Type = "Colorpicker",
            Instance = Items["Holder"].Instance,
            Object = Colorpicker,
            Flag = Colorpicker.Flag
        })

        return Colorpicker
    end

            })

            Items["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {

                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = Label.Name,

                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment[Label.Alignment],
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),

                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Label.Instance = Items["Text"].Instance

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Contextual,
                Parent = Items["Text"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["RightContainer"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Label"].Instance,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(1, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2New(1, -4, 0.5, 0),

            })
        end

        function Label:Colorpicker(Data)
            Data = Data or { }

            local Colorpicker = {
                Window = self.Window,
                Tab = self.Tab,
                Section = self.Section,

                Parent = Items["Label"],
                Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Colorpicker",
                Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
                Default = Data.Default or Data.default or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,
                Alpha = Data.Alpha or Data.alpha or false,
                Count = Label.Count,
                FadeSpeed = self.Window.FadeSpeed
            }

            Label.Count += 1
            Colorpicker.Count = Label.Count

            local Extension = Library:CreateColorpicker(Colorpicker)

            if Extension.Items and Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"] then
                Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance.Parent = Items["RightContainer"].Instance
                Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0.5)
                Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
                Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance.LayoutOrder = Label.Count
            end

            Label.Section:RegisterFeature({
                Name = Label.Name .. " " .. (Data.Name or "Color"),
                Type = "Colorpicker",
                Instance = Extension.Items and Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"] and Extension.Items["ColorpickerButton"].Instance or nil,
                ParentInstance = Items["Label"].Instance,
                Object = Colorpicker,
                Flag = Colorpicker.Flag
            })

            return Colorpicker, Extension
        end

        function Label:Keybind(Data)
            Data = Data or { }

            local Keybind = {
                Window = self.Window,
                Tab = self.Tab,
                Section = self.Section,

                Parent = Items["Label"],
                Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Keybind",
                Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
                Default = Data.Default ~= nil and Data.Default or Data.default or nil,
                Mode = Data.Mode or Data.mode or "Toggle",
                Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,
            }

            local Extension = Library:CreateKeybind(Keybind)

            if Extension.Items and Extension.Items["KeyButton"] then
                Extension.Items["KeyButton"].Instance.Parent = Items["RightContainer"].Instance
                Extension.Items["KeyButton"].Instance.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 0.5)
                Extension.Items["KeyButton"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
                Extension.Items["KeyButton"].Instance.LayoutOrder = 999
            end

            setmetatable(Keybind, { __index = Extension })

            Label.Section:RegisterFeature({
                Name = Label.Name .. " " .. (Data.Name or "Keybind"),
                Type = "Keybind",
                Instance = Extension.Items and Extension.Items["KeyButton"] and Extension.Items["KeyButton"].Instance or nil,
                ParentInstance = Items["Label"].Instance,
                Object = Keybind,
                Flag = Keybind.Flag
            })

            return Extension, Keybind
        end

        function Label:Set(Text)
            Label.Name = tostring(Text or "")
            Items["Text"].Instance.Text = Label.Name
        end
        Label.SetText = Label.Set

        function Label:SetVisibility(Bool)
            Items["Label"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end
        Label.SetVisiblity = Label.SetVisibility

        if Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip then
            Library:AddTooltip(Items["Label"].Instance, Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip)
        end

        self:RegisterFeature({
            Name = Label.Name,
            Type = "Label",
            Instance = Items["Label"].Instance,
            Object = Label
        })

        return Label
    end

    Library.Sections.Textbox = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local Textbox = {
            Window = self.Window,
            Tab = self.Tab,
            Section = self,

            Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Textbox",
            Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
            Placeholder = Data.Placeholder or Data.placeholder or "...",
            Default = Data.Default or Data.default or "",
            Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,

            Value = "",
            Class = "Textbox"
        }

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Textbox"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Textbox.Section.Elements["Content"].Instance,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 34),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Items["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Textbox"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = Textbox.Name,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 13),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Text"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Items["Background"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Textbox"].Instance,
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 1),
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 17),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(33, 33, 36)
            })  Items["Background"]:AddToTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Background"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100))}
            })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Background"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            Items["Inline"] = Instances:Create("TextBox", {
                Parent = Items["Background"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = "",
                Name = "\0",
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ClearTextOnFocus = false,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                PlaceholderColor3 = FromRGB(178, 178, 178),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                PlaceholderText = Textbox.Placeholder,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })  Items["Inline"]:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["Inline"].Instance,
                PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 3),
                PaddingLeft = UDimNew(0, 5)
            })

            Items["Background"]:OnHover(function()
                Items["Background"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"]})
                Items["Background"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
            end)

            Items["Background"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
                Items["Background"]:Tween(nil, {BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Element"]})
                Items["Background"]:ChangeItemTheme({BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border"})
            end)
        end

        function Textbox:Get()
            return Textbox.Value
        end

        function Textbox:SetVisibility(Bool)
            Items["Textbox"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end

        function Textbox:Set(Value)
            if type(Value) == "table" then
                Value = Value.v ~= nil and Value.v or Value[1] or Value.Value or ""
            end
            Value = tostring(Value or "")
            Textbox.Value = Value

            Items["Inline"].Instance.Text = Textbox.Value
            Items["Inline"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text})
            Items["Inline"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Library.Flags[Textbox.Flag] = Textbox.Value

            if Textbox.Callback then
                Library:SafeCall(Textbox.Callback, Textbox.Value)
            end
        end

        Items["Inline"]:Connect("Focused", function()
            Items["Inline"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Accent"})
            Items["Inline"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent})
        end)

        Items["Inline"]:Connect("FocusLost", function()
            Items["Inline"]:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})
            Items["Inline"]:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text})

            Textbox:Set(Items["Inline"].Instance.Text)
        end)

        if Textbox.Default then
            Textbox:Set(Textbox.Default)
        end

        Library.Defaults[Textbox.Flag] = Textbox.Default
        Library.SetFlags[Textbox.Flag] = function(Value)
            Textbox:Set(Value)
        end

        self:RegisterFeature({
            Name = Textbox.Name,
            Type = "Textbox",
            Instance = Items["Textbox"].Instance,
            Object = Textbox,
            Flag = Textbox.Flag
        })

        return Textbox
    end

    Library.Sections.Listbox = function(self, Data)

        local Listbox = {

            Items = Data.Items or Data.items or { },
