            Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
            Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,
            Size = Data.Size or Data.size or 175,

            Value = { },
            Options = { },
            Class = "Listbox",
        }

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Listbox"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Listbox.Section.Elements["Content"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, Listbox.Size),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Items["RealListbox"] = Instances:Create("ScrollingFrame", {
                Parent = Items["Listbox"].Instance,
                ScrollBarImageColor3 = FromRGB(235, 157, 255),
                Active = true,
                AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                ScrollBarThickness = 1,
                AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0, 1),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                CanvasSize = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 0)
            })  Items["RealListbox"]:AddToTheme({ScrollBarImageColor3 = "Accent", BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["RealListbox"].Instance,
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32),
                Name = "\0",
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Outline"})

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["RealListbox"].Instance,
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            })

            Instances:Create("UIPadding", {
                Parent = Items["RealListbox"].Instance,
                PaddingBottom = UDimNew(0, 5),
                PaddingTop = UDimNew(0, 2)
            })
        end

        function Listbox:Set(Option)
            if type(Option) == "table" and Option.v ~= nil then
                Option = Option.v
            end
            if Listbox.Multi then
                if type(Option) ~= "table" then
                    if type(Option) == "string" then
                        Option = { Option }
                    else
                        Option = {}
                    end
                end

                Listbox.Value = Option

                Library.Flags[Listbox.Flag] = Listbox.Value

                for Index, Value in Option do
                    local OptionData = Listbox.Options[Value]

                    if not OptionData then
                        continue
                    end

                    OptionData.Selected = true
                    OptionData:Toggle("Active")
                end
            else
                if type(Option) == "table" then
                    Option = Option[1] or Option.Value or Option.Name or ""
                end
                Option = tostring(Option or "")

                local OptionData = Listbox.Options[Option]
                if not OptionData then
                    for k, v in pairs(Listbox.Options) do
                        if tostring(k):lower() == Option:lower() then
                            OptionData = v
                            Option = tostring(k)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end

                if not OptionData then
                    return
                end

                Listbox.Value = OptionData.Name

                Library.Flags[Listbox.Flag] = Listbox.Value

                OptionData.Selected = true
                OptionData:Toggle("Active")

                for Index, Value in Listbox.Options do
                    if Value ~= OptionData then
                        Value.Selected = false
                        Value:Toggle("Inactive")
                    end
                end
            end

            if Listbox.Callback then
                Library:SafeCall(Listbox.Callback, Option)
            end
        end

        function Listbox:Get()
            return Listbox.Value
        end

        function Listbox:Select(Option)
            return Listbox:Set(Option)
        end

        function Listbox:SetVisibility(Bool)
            Items["Listbox"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end
        Listbox.SetVisiblity = Listbox.SetVisibility

        function Listbox:Remove(Option)
            if Listbox.Options[Option] then
                Listbox.Options[Option].Button:Clean()
            end
        end

        function Listbox:Refresh(List)
            for Index, Value in Listbox.Options do
                Listbox:Remove(Value.Name)
            end

            for Index, Value in List do
                Listbox:Add(Value)
            end
        end

        function Listbox:Add(Option)
            local StrOption = tostring(type(Option) == "table" and (Option[1] or Option.Name or "") or (Option or ""))
            local ParentZ = Items["RealListbox"].Instance.ZIndex
            local OptionButton = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Items["RealListbox"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 15),
                ZIndex = ParentZ + 4,
                TextSize = 14,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            local OptionText = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = OptionButton.Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                TextTransparency = 0.48,
                Text = StrOption,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, -5, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2New(0, 5, 0, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = ParentZ + 5,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            OptionText:AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = OptionText.Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({Color = "Text Border"})

            local OptionData = {
                Selected = false,
                Name = StrOption,
                Text = OptionText,
                Button = OptionButton
            }

            function OptionData:Toggle(State)
                if State == "Active" then
                    OptionData.Text:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Accent"})
                    OptionData.Text:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent, TextTransparency = 0})
                else
                    OptionData.Text:ChangeItemTheme({TextColor3 = "Text"})
                    OptionData.Text:Tween(nil, {TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text, TextTransparency = 0.48})
                end
            end

            function OptionData:Set()
                OptionData.Selected = not OptionData.Selected

                if Listbox.Multi then
                    local Index = TableFind(Listbox.Value, OptionData.Name)

                    if Index then
                        TableRemove(Listbox.Value, Index)
                    else
                        TableInsert(Listbox.Value, OptionData.Name)
                    end

                    OptionData:Toggle(Index and "Inactive" or "Active")

                    local TextFormat = #Listbox.Value > 0 and TableConcat(Listbox.Value, ", ") or "--"
                else
                    if OptionData.Selected then
                        Listbox.Value = OptionData.Name

                        OptionData:Toggle("Active")

                        for Index, Value in Listbox.Options do
                            if Value ~= OptionData then
                                Value.Selected = false
                                Value:Toggle("Inactive")
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        Listbox.Value = nil

                        OptionData:Toggle("Inactive")
                    end
                end

                Library.Flags[Listbox.Flag] = Listbox.Value

                if Listbox.Callback then
                    Library:SafeCall(Listbox.Callback, Listbox.Value)
                end
            end

            OptionButton:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                OptionData:Set()
            end)

            Listbox.Options[Option] = OptionData
            return OptionData
        end

        for Index, Value in Listbox.Items do
            Listbox:Add(Value)
        end

        if Listbox.Default then
            Listbox:Set(Listbox.Default)
        end

        Library.Defaults[Listbox.Flag] = Listbox.Default
        Library.SetFlags[Listbox.Flag] = function(Value)
            Listbox:Set(Value)
        end

        if Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip then
            Library:AddTooltip(Items["Listbox"].Instance, Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip)
        end

        self:RegisterFeature({
            Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Listbox",
            Type = "Listbox",
            Instance = Items["Listbox"].Instance,
            Object = Listbox,
            Flag = Listbox.Flag
        })

        return Listbox
    end

    local CatmullRom = function(t, P0, P1, P2, P3)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
        local t2 = t * t
        local t3 = t2 * t
        local X = 0.5 * ((2 * P1[1]) + (-P0[1] + P2[1]) * t + (2 * P0[1] - 5 * P1[1] + 4 * P2[1] - P3[1]) * t2 + (-P0[1] + 3 * P1[1] - 3 * P2[1] + P3[1]) * t3)
        local Y = 0.5 * ((2 * P1[2]) + (-P0[2] + P2[2]) * t + (2 * P0[2] - 5 * P1[2] + 4 * P2[2] - P3[2]) * t2 + (-P0[2] + 3 * P1[2] - 3 * P2[2] + P3[2]) * t3)
        return X, Y
    end

    Library.Sections.BezierMapper = function(self, Data)
        Data = Data or { }

        local Bezier = {
            Window = self.Window,
            Page = self.Page,
            Section = self,

            Name = Data.Name or Data.name or "Bezier Mapper",
            Flag = Data.Flag or Data.flag or Library:NextFlag(),
            Callback = Data.Callback or Data.callback or function() end,

            Points = { },
            SlidingPoint = nil,
            DraggingWindow = false,
            Class = "BezierMapper"
        }

        local DefaultPoints = Data.Default or Data.default or { { 0, 0 }, { 1, 1 } }
        for _, Pt in DefaultPoints do
            TableInsert(Bezier.Points, {
                MathClamp(Pt[1] or 0, 0, 1),
                MathClamp(Pt[2] or 0, 0, 1)
            })
        end

        if #Bezier.Points < 2 then
            Bezier.Points = { { 0, 0 }, { 1, 1 } }
        end

        local Items = { } do
            Items["Bezier"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Bezier.Section.Elements["Content"].Instance,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Name = "\0",
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Items["Text"] = Instances:Create("TextLabel", {
                Parent = Items["Bezier"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(215, 215, 215),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                Text = Bezier.Name,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 13),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                TextSize = 12,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })
            Items["Text"]:AddToTheme({ TextColor3 = "Text" })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Text"].Instance,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0"
            }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Text Border" })

            Items["Canvas"] = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = Items["Bezier"].Instance,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                Text = "",
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Name = "\0",
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 16),
                BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 70),
                BorderSizePixel = 2,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(15, 15, 20)
            })
            Items["Canvas"]:AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border" })

            Instances:Create("UIStroke", {
                Parent = Items["Canvas"].Instance,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
                Name = "\0",
                Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            }):AddToTheme({ Color = "Outline" })

            Instances:Create("UIGradient", {
                Parent = Items["Canvas"].Instance,
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = RGBSequence{ RGBSequenceKeypoint(0, FromRGB(255, 255, 255)), RGBSequenceKeypoint(1, FromRGB(100, 100, 100)) }
            })

            Items["Grid"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Canvas"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                ZIndex = 2
            })

            for i = 1, 3 do
                Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Grid"].Instance,
                    Name = "\0",
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(40, 40, 48),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Size = UDim2New(0, 1, 1, 0),
                    Position = UDim2New(i / 4, 0, 0, 0),
                    ZIndex = 2
                })

                Instances:Create("Frame", {
                    Parent = Items["Grid"].Instance,
                    Name = "\0",
                    BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(40, 40, 48),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 1),
                    Position = UDim2New(0, 0, i / 4, 0),
                    ZIndex = 2
                })
            end

            Items["CurveLayer"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Canvas"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                ZIndex = 3
            })

            Items["HandleLayer"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Canvas"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                ZIndex = 4
            })

            Items["DebugHolder"] = Instances:Create("Frame", {
                Parent = Items["Bezier"].Instance,
                Name = "\0",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2New(0, 0, 0, 90),
                Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            })

            Instances:Create("UIListLayout", {
                Parent = Items["DebugHolder"].Instance,
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
                Padding = UDimNew(0, 1)
            })
        end

        local CanvasInstance = Items["Canvas"].Instance
        local CurveLayer = Items["CurveLayer"].Instance
        local HandleLayer = Items["HandleLayer"].Instance
        local DebugHolder = Items["DebugHolder"].Instance

        local PointHandles = { }
        local CurveDots = { }
        local DebugLabels = { }
        local DotsPerSegment = 20

        local GetMousePos = function()
            local MousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            local GuiInset = game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset().Y
            return Vector2New(MousePos.X, MousePos.Y - GuiInset)
        end

        local GetRelativePos = function()
            local MPos = GetMousePos()
            local CPos = CanvasInstance.AbsolutePosition
            local CSize = CanvasInstance.AbsoluteSize
            local RelX = MathClamp((MPos.X - CPos.X) / CSize.X, 0, 1)
            local RelY = MathClamp((MPos.Y - CPos.Y) / CSize.Y, 0, 1)
            return RelX, RelY, MPos
        end

        local FindNearestPoint = function(RelX, RelY)
            local NearestIndex = nil
            local NearestDist = 0.06

            for i, Pt in Bezier.Points do
                local DX = Pt[1] - RelX
                local DY = Pt[2] - (1 - RelY)
                local Dist = math.sqrt(DX * DX + DY * DY)
                if Dist < NearestDist then
                    NearestDist = Dist
                    NearestIndex = i
                end
            end

            return NearestIndex
        end

        local DrawCurve = function()
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            local CWidth = CanvasInstance.AbsoluteSize.X
            local CHeight = CanvasInstance.AbsoluteSize.Y
            if CWidth <= 0 or CHeight <= 0 then
                return
            end

            local TotalDots = (#Bezier.Points - 1) * DotsPerSegment
            if TotalDots < 1 then
                TotalDots = 1
            end

            local DotIndex = 1
            local AllPoints = { }

            for i = 1, #Bezier.Points do
                AllPoints[i] = Bezier.Points[i]
            end

            if #AllPoints >= 2 then
                if #AllPoints == 2 then
                    AllPoints = { AllPoints[1], AllPoints[1], AllPoints[2], AllPoints[2] }
                elseif #AllPoints == 3 then
                    TableInsert(AllPoints, 1, AllPoints[1])
                    TableInsert(AllPoints, AllPoints[#AllPoints])
                else
                    TableInsert(AllPoints, 1, AllPoints[1])
                    TableInsert(AllPoints, AllPoints[#AllPoints])
                end

                for seg = 2, #AllPoints - 2 do
                    local P0 = AllPoints[seg - 1]
                    local P1 = AllPoints[seg]
                    local P2 = AllPoints[seg + 1]
                    local P3 = AllPoints[seg + 2]

                    for d = 0, DotsPerSegment - 1 do
                        local t = d / DotsPerSegment
                        local X, Y = CatmullRom(t, P0, P1, P2, P3)

                        local Dot = CurveDots[DotIndex]
                        if not Dot then
                            Dot = InstanceNew("Frame")
                            Dot.Name = "\0"
                            Dot.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                            Dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
                            Dot.Size = UDim2New(0, 2, 0, 2)
                            Dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0.5, 0.5)
                            Dot.ZIndex = 3
                            Dot.Parent = CurveLayer

                            local Stroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
                            Stroke.Color = FromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                            Stroke.Transparency = 0.5
                            Stroke.Parent = Dot

                            CurveDots[DotIndex] = Dot
                        end

                        Dot.Position = UDim2New(X, 0, 1 - Y, 0)
                        Dot.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                        Dot.Visible = true
                        DotIndex = DotIndex + 1
                    end
                end

                local LastPt = Bezier.Points[#Bezier.Points]
                local Dot = CurveDots[DotIndex]
                if not Dot then
                    Dot = InstanceNew("Frame")
                    Dot.Name = "\0"
                    Dot.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                    Dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    Dot.Size = UDim2New(0, 2, 0, 2)
                    Dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0.5, 0.5)
                    Dot.ZIndex = 3
                    Dot.Parent = CurveLayer
                    CurveDots[DotIndex] = Dot
                end
                Dot.Position = UDim2New(LastPt[1], 0, 1 - LastPt[2], 0)
                Dot.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                Dot.Visible = true
                DotIndex = DotIndex + 1
            end

            for i = DotIndex, #CurveDots do
                CurveDots[i].Visible = false
            end

            for i, Pt in Bezier.Points do
                local Handle = PointHandles[i]
                if not Handle then
                    Handle = InstanceNew("Frame")
                    Handle.Name = "\0"
                    Handle.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                    Handle.BorderColor3 = FromRGB(10, 10, 10)
                    Handle.BorderSizePixel = 2
                    Handle.Size = UDim2New(0, 7, 0, 7)
                    Handle.AnchorPoint = Vector2New(0.5, 0.5)
                    Handle.ZIndex = 4
                    Handle.Parent = HandleLayer

                    local Stroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
                    Stroke.Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
                    Stroke.Parent = Handle

                    PointHandles[i] = Handle
                end

                Handle.Position = UDim2New(Pt[1], 0, 1 - Pt[2], 0)
                Handle.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent
                Handle.Visible = true
            end

            for i = #Bezier.Points + 1, #PointHandles do
                PointHandles[i].Visible = false
            end

            for i = #DebugLabels, 1, -1 do
                if i > #Bezier.Points then
                    DebugLabels[i]:Destroy()
                    DebugLabels[i] = nil
                end
            end

            for i, Pt in Bezier.Points do
                local Label = DebugLabels[i]
                if not Label then
                    Label = InstanceNew("TextLabel")
                    Label.Name = "\0"
                    Label.FontFace = Library.Font
                    Label.TextColor3 = FromRGB(150, 150, 158)
                    Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    Label.Size = UDim2New(1, 0, 0, 11)
                    Label.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    Label.TextSize = 10
                    Label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    Label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
                    Label.BackgroundColor3 = FromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    Label.Parent = DebugHolder

                    local Stroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
                    Stroke.Color = FromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    Stroke.Parent = Label

                    DebugLabels[i] = Label
                end

                Label.Text = StringFormat("Point%d: %.3f, %.3f", i, Pt[1], Pt[2])
            end
        end

        local FireCallback = function()
            local ReturnPoints = { }
            for _, Pt in Bezier.Points do
                TableInsert(ReturnPoints, { Pt[1], Pt[2] })
            end
            Library.Flags[Bezier.Flag] = ReturnPoints
            Library:SafeCall(Bezier.Callback, ReturnPoints)
        end

        local BezierMoveConn

        local function StopBezierMove()
            Bezier.SlidingPoint = nil
            Bezier.DraggingWindow = false
            if BezierMoveConn then
                BezierMoveConn:Disconnect()
                BezierMoveConn = nil
            end
        end

        local function StartBezierMove()
            if BezierMoveConn then return end
            BezierMoveConn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(Input)
                if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    if Bezier.SlidingPoint then
                        local RelX, RelY = GetRelativePos()
                        Bezier.Points[Bezier.SlidingPoint] = { RelX, 1 - RelY }
                        table.sort(Bezier.Points, function(A, B)
                            return A[1] < B[1]
                        end)

                        for i, Pt in Bezier.Points do
                            if Pt[1] == RelX and Pt[2] == (1 - RelY) then
                                Bezier.SlidingPoint = i
                                break
                            end
                        end

                        DrawCurve()
                        FireCallback()
                    elseif Bezier.DraggingWindow then
                        local MainFrame = Bezier.Window.Elements["MainFrame"].Instance
                        local DragDelta = Input.Position - Bezier._DragStart
                        local NewX = Bezier._DragStartPos.X.Offset + DragDelta.X
                        local NewY = Bezier._DragStartPos.Y.Offset + DragDelta.Y
                        local ViewportX = Camera.ViewportSize.X
                        local ViewportY = Camera.ViewportSize.Y
                        local FrameW = MainFrame.AbsoluteSize.X
                        local FrameH = MainFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y
                        NewX = MathClamp(NewX, 0, ViewportX - FrameW)
                        NewY = MathClamp(NewY, 0, ViewportY - FrameH)
                        MainFrame.Position = UDim2New(0, NewX, 0, NewY)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        CanvasInstance.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)
            if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                local RelX, RelY = GetRelativePos()
                local NearestIdx = FindNearestPoint(RelX, RelY)

                if NearestIdx and #Bezier.Points > 2 then
                    TableRemove(Bezier.Points, NearestIdx)
                    DrawCurve()
                    FireCallback()
                else
                    TableInsert(Bezier.Points, { RelX, 1 - RelY })
                    table.sort(Bezier.Points, function(A, B)
                        return A[1] < B[1]
                    end)
                    DrawCurve()
                    FireCallback()
                end
            elseif Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                local RelX, RelY = GetRelativePos()
                local NearestIdx = FindNearestPoint(RelX, RelY)

                if NearestIdx then
                    Bezier.SlidingPoint = NearestIdx
                    StartBezierMove()
                else
                    Bezier.DraggingWindow = true
                    local MainFrame = Bezier.Window.Elements["MainFrame"].Instance
                    Bezier._DragStart = Input.Position
                    Bezier._DragStartPos = MainFrame.Position
                    StartBezierMove()
                end
            end
        end)

        CanvasInstance.InputEnded:Connect(function(Input)
            if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                StopBezierMove()
            end
        end)

        Library:Connect(CanvasInstance:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"), function()
            task.wait()
            DrawCurve()
        end)

        Library:Connect(CanvasInstance:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsolutePosition"), function()
            task.wait()
            DrawCurve()
        end)

        function Bezier:Set(Points)
            if type(Points) ~= "table" then
                return
            end

            Bezier.Points = { }
            for _, Pt in Points do
                TableInsert(Bezier.Points, {
                    MathClamp(Pt[1] or 0, 0, 1),
                    MathClamp(Pt[2] or 0, 0, 1)
                })
            end

            if #Bezier.Points < 2 then
                Bezier.Points = { { 0, 0 }, { 1, 1 } }
            end

            table.sort(Bezier.Points, function(A, B)
                return A[1] < B[1]
            end)

            DrawCurve()
            FireCallback()
        end

        function Bezier:Get()
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            local ReturnPoints = { }
            for _, Pt in Bezier.Points do
                TableInsert(ReturnPoints, { Pt[1], Pt[2] })
            end
            return ReturnPoints
        end

        function Bezier:Evaluate(t)
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(PRESET(FAST))
            t = MathClamp(t, 0, 1)
            if #Bezier.Points < 2 then
                return 0
            end

            if #Bezier.Points == 2 then
                local P1 = Bezier.Points[1]
                local P2 = Bezier.Points[2]
                return P1[2] + (P2[2] - P1[2]) * t
            end

            local AllPoints = { }
            for _, Pt in Bezier.Points do
                TableInsert(AllPoints, Pt)
            end
            TableInsert(AllPoints, 1, AllPoints[1])
            TableInsert(AllPoints, AllPoints[#AllPoints])

            local TotalSegs = #AllPoints - 3
            local SegT = t * TotalSegs
            local SegIndex = MathFloor(SegT) + 1
            if SegIndex >= TotalSegs then
                SegIndex = TotalSegs
                SegT = 1
            else
                SegT = SegT - (SegIndex - 1)
            end

            local P0 = AllPoints[SegIndex]
            local P1 = AllPoints[SegIndex + 1]
            local P2 = AllPoints[SegIndex + 2]
            local P3 = AllPoints[SegIndex + 3]

            local _, Y = CatmullRom(SegT, P0, P1, P2, P3)
            return Y
        end

        function Bezier:SetVisibility(Bool)
            Items["Bezier"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end

        function Bezier:AddPoint(X, Y)
            TableInsert(Bezier.Points, { MathClamp(X, 0, 1), MathClamp(Y, 0, 1) })
            table.sort(Bezier.Points, function(A, B)
                return A[1] < B[1]
            end)
            DrawCurve()
            FireCallback()
        end

        function Bezier:RemovePoint(Index)
            if #Bezier.Points <= 2 then
                return
            end
            TableRemove(Bezier.Points, Index)
            DrawCurve()
            FireCallback()
        end

        Library.Flags[Bezier.Flag] = { }
        for _, Pt in Bezier.Points do
            TableInsert(Library.Flags[Bezier.Flag], { Pt[1], Pt[2] })
        end

        Library.SetFlags[Bezier.Flag] = function(Value)
            Bezier:Set(Value)
        end

        task.defer(function()
            task.wait(0.1)
            DrawCurve()
        end)

        if Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip then
            Library:AddTooltip(Items["Bezier"].Instance, Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip)
        end

        self:RegisterFeature({
            Name = Bezier.Name,
            Type = "Bezier",
            Instance = Items["Bezier"].Instance,
            Object = Bezier,
            Flag = Bezier.Flag
        })

        return Bezier
    end

        local function OpenSettings(Bool)
            if SettingsDebounce then return end
            SettingsIsOpen = Bool
            SettingsDebounce = true

            if SettingsIsOpen then
                Items["SettingsWindow"].Instance.Visible = true
                Items["SettingsWindow"].Instance.Parent = Library.Holder.Instance
                Library:BringToFront(Items["SettingsWindow"].Instance)

                local BtnPos = Items["SettingsButton"].Instance.AbsolutePosition
                local BtnSize = Items["SettingsButton"].Instance.AbsoluteSize
                local WinSize = Items["SettingsWindow"].Instance.AbsoluteSize
                local Viewport = Camera.ViewportSize

                local PosX = BtnPos.X - WinSize.X + BtnSize.X
                local PosY = BtnPos.Y + BtnSize.Y + 5

                if PosX < 5 then PosX = 5 end
                if PosY + WinSize.Y > Viewport.Y then
                    PosY = BtnPos.Y - WinSize.Y - 5
                end

                Items["SettingsWindow"].Instance.Position = UDim2New(0, PosX, 0, PosY)
            else
                Items["SettingsWindow"].Instance.Visible = false
                Items["SettingsWindow"].Instance.Parent = Library.UnusedHolder.Instance
            end

            task.delay(0.1, function()
                SettingsDebounce = false
            end)
        end

                SettingsState.Random = Bool

                    SliderFrame.Instance.Visible = Bool

                UpdateWindowSize()
                UpdateAllPartsVisual()

                    ToggleIndicator:Tween(nil, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"] })
                end
            end)

            ToggleFrame:OnHoverLeave(function()
                if not RandomValue then
                    ToggleIndicator:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })
                    ToggleIndicator:Tween(nil, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element })

            SettingsItems.Sliders = { }

                    SliderValue = Library:Round(MathClamp(V, 0, 100), 1)
                    Library.Flags[BodyPartBox.Flag .. "_random_" .. PartName] = SliderValue
                    Indicator:Tween(CachedQuartTweenInfo, { Size = UDim2New(SliderValue / 100, 0, 1, 0) })
                    ValueLabel.Instance.Text = StringFormat("%s%%", tostring(SliderValue))
                    SettingsState.RandomPercents[PartName] = SliderValue
                    if UpdatePartVisual then
                        UpdatePartVisual(PartName, BodyPartBox.Selected[PartName])
                    end

                local MoveConn
                local EndConn

                RealSlider:Connect("InputBegan", function(Input)
                    if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        Sliding = true
                        local SizeX = (Mouse.X - RealSlider.Instance.AbsolutePosition.X) / RealSlider.Instance.AbsoluteSize.X
                        SetValue(SizeX * 100)

                        if MoveConn then MoveConn:Disconnect() MoveConn = nil end
                        if EndConn then EndConn:Disconnect() EndConn = nil end

                        MoveConn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(moveInput)
                            if (moveInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or moveInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) and Sliding then
                                local szX = (Mouse.X - RealSlider.Instance.AbsolutePosition.X) / RealSlider.Instance.AbsoluteSize.X
                                SetValue(szX * 100)
                            end
                        end)

                        EndConn = Input.Changed:Connect(function()
                            if Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                                Sliding = false
                                if MoveConn then MoveConn:Disconnect() MoveConn = nil end
                                if EndConn then EndConn:Disconnect() EndConn = nil end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end)

                RealSlider:Connect("InputEnded", function(Input)
                    if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        Sliding = false
                        if MoveConn then MoveConn:Disconnect() MoveConn = nil end
                        if EndConn then EndConn:Disconnect() EndConn = nil end
                    end
                end)

                SliderFrame:OnHover(function()
                    RealSlider:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Hovered Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })
                    RealSlider:Tween(nil, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"] })
                end)

                SliderFrame:OnHoverLeave(function()
                    RealSlider:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Background", BorderColor3 = "Border" })
                    RealSlider:Tween(nil, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Background })
                end)

                SetValue(50)
                SettingsItems.Sliders[PartName] = SliderFrame
            end
        end

        local JustOpened = false

        Items["SettingsButton"]:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
            JustOpened = true
            OpenSettings(not SettingsIsOpen)
            task.delay(0.15, function()
                JustOpened = false
            end)
        end)

        Items["SettingsButton"]:OnHover(function()
            Items["SettingsIcon"]:Tween(nil, { ImageColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent })
        end)

        Items["SettingsButton"]:OnHoverLeave(function()
            Items["SettingsIcon"]:Tween(nil, { ImageColor3 = Library.Theme.Text })
        end)

        Library:Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(Input)
            if SettingsIsOpen and not JustOpened and Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                local MousePos = Vector2New(Mouse.X, Mouse.Y)
                local WinPos = Items["SettingsWindow"].Instance.AbsolutePosition
                local WinSize = Items["SettingsWindow"].Instance.AbsoluteSize
                local BtnPos = Items["SettingsButton"].Instance.AbsolutePosition
                local BtnSize = Items["SettingsButton"].Instance.AbsoluteSize

                local InWindow = MousePos.X >= WinPos.X and MousePos.X <= WinPos.X + WinSize.X
                    and MousePos.Y >= WinPos.Y and MousePos.Y <= WinPos.Y + WinSize.Y
                local InButton = MousePos.X >= BtnPos.X and MousePos.X <= BtnPos.X + BtnSize.X
                    and MousePos.Y >= BtnPos.Y and MousePos.Y <= BtnPos.Y + BtnSize.Y

                if not InWindow and not InButton then
                    OpenSettings(false)
                end
            end
        end)

        BodyPartBox.Settings = SettingsState

        local RigContainer = Items["RigContainer"].Instance

        local PartFrames = { }

        local CreatePartFrame = function(Name, Props)
            local Frame = Instances:Create("TextButton", {
                Parent = RigContainer,
                FontFace = Library.Font,
                TextColor3 = FromRGB(200, 200, 208),
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element,
                BorderColor3 = Library.Theme.Border,
                BorderSizePixel = 1,
                Name = Name,
                ZIndex = 4,
                TextSize = 9
            })

            for Prop, Value in Props do
                Frame.Instance[Prop] = Value
            end

            local Stroke = InstanceNew("UIStroke")
            Stroke.Color = FromRGB(27, 27, 32)
            Stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
            Stroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
            Stroke.Parent = Frame.Instance

            Frame:AddToTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Element", BorderColor3 = "Border" })

            return Frame
        end

        local R6Layout = {
            Head = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -11, 0, 3), Size = UDim2New(0, 22, 0, 28) },
            Torso = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -22, 0, 34), Size = UDim2New(0, 45, 0, 45) },
            ["Left Arm"] = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -43, 0, 34), Size = UDim2New(0, 17, 0, 45) },
            ["Right Arm"] = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, 27, 0, 34), Size = UDim2New(0, 17, 0, 45) },
            ["Left Leg"] = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -22, 0, 81), Size = UDim2New(0, 20, 0, 50) },
            ["Right Leg"] = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, 3, 0, 81), Size = UDim2New(0, 20, 0, 50) },
            HumanoidRootPart = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -11, 0, 45), Size = UDim2New(0, 22, 0, 22) }
        }

        local R15Layout = {
            Head = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -11, 0, 3), Size = UDim2New(0, 22, 0, 28) },
            UpperTorso = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -22, 0, 34), Size = UDim2New(0, 45, 0, 25) },
            LowerTorso = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -22, 0, 62), Size = UDim2New(0, 45, 0, 17) },
            LeftUpperArm = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -43, 0, 34), Size = UDim2New(0, 17, 0, 20) },
            LeftLowerArm = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -43, 0, 56), Size = UDim2New(0, 17, 0, 14) },
            LeftHand = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -43, 0, 71), Size = UDim2New(0, 17, 0, 7) },
            RightUpperArm = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, 27, 0, 34), Size = UDim2New(0, 17, 0, 20) },
            RightLowerArm = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, 27, 0, 56), Size = UDim2New(0, 17, 0, 14) },
            RightHand = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, 27, 0, 71), Size = UDim2New(0, 17, 0, 7) },
            LeftUpperLeg = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -22, 0, 81), Size = UDim2New(0, 20, 0, 20) },
            LeftLowerLeg = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -22, 0, 104), Size = UDim2New(0, 20, 0, 18) },
            LeftFoot = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -22, 0, 125), Size = UDim2New(0, 20, 0, 7) },
            RightUpperLeg = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, 3, 0, 81), Size = UDim2New(0, 20, 0, 20) },
            RightLowerLeg = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, 3, 0, 104), Size = UDim2New(0, 20, 0, 18) },
            RightFoot = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, 3, 0, 125), Size = UDim2New(0, 20, 0, 7) },
            HumanoidRootPart = { Position = UDim2New(0.5, -11, 0, 45), Size = UDim2New(0, 22, 0, 22) }
        }

        local Layout = BodyPartBox.RigType == "R15" and R15Layout or R6Layout

        for _, PartName in Parts do
            local Props = Layout[PartName]
            if Props then
                local Frame = CreatePartFrame(PartName, Props)
                PartFrames[PartName] = Frame

                Frame:Connect("MouseButton1Down", function()
                    BodyPartBox:TogglePart(PartName)
                end)

                Frame:OnHover(function()
                    if not BodyPartBox.Selected[PartName] and not SettingsState.Random then
                        Frame:Tween(CachedHoverTweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme["Hovered Element"] })
                    end
                end)

                Frame:OnHoverLeave(function()
                    if not BodyPartBox.Selected[PartName] and not SettingsState.Random then
                        Frame:Tween(CachedHoverTweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element })
                    end
                end)
                local PartTooltip = Data.Tooltips and (Data.Tooltips[PartName] or Data.Tooltips[PartName:lower()])
                    or Data.tooltips and (Data.tooltips[PartName] or Data.tooltips[PartName:lower()])
                    or Data.Tooltip or Data.tooltip
                if PartTooltip then
                    Library:AddTooltip(Frame.Instance, PartTooltip)
                end
            end
        end

        UpdatePartVisual = function(PartName, Selected)
            local Frame = PartFrames[PartName]
            if not Frame then return end

            if SettingsState.Random then
                local Chance = SettingsState.RandomPercents[PartName] or 0
                local Alpha = Chance / 100
                Frame:Tween(CachedQuartTweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1 - Alpha })
                Frame:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Accent" })
            elseif Selected then
                Frame:Tween(CachedQuartTweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
                Frame:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Accent" })
            else
                Frame:Tween(CachedQuartTweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = Library.Theme.Element, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
                Frame:ChangeItemTheme({ BackgroundColor3 = "Element" })
            end
        end

        UpdateAllPartsVisual = function()
            for PartName, Frame in PartFrames do
                UpdatePartVisual(PartName, BodyPartBox.Selected[PartName])
            end
        end

        function BodyPartBox:TogglePart(PartName)
            if SettingsState.Random then return end

            if self.Selected[PartName] then
                self.Selected[PartName] = nil
                UpdatePartVisual(PartName, false)
            else
                if not self.Multiple then
                    for OtherPart, _ in self.Selected do
                        self.Selected[OtherPart] = nil
                        UpdatePartVisual(OtherPart, false)
                    end
                end
                self.Selected[PartName] = true
                UpdatePartVisual(PartName, true)
            end

            local SelectedList = { }
            for Part, _ in self.Selected do
                TableInsert(SelectedList, Part)
            end

            Library.Flags[self.Flag] = SelectedList
            Library:SafeCall(self.Callback, SelectedList)
        end

        function BodyPartBox:Set(Parts)
            for Part, _ in self.Selected do
                self.Selected[Part] = nil
                UpdatePartVisual(Part, false)
            end

            self.Selected = { }

            if type(Parts) == "string" then
                Parts = { Parts }
            end

            if type(Parts) == "table" then
                for _, Part in Parts do
                    if PartFrames[Part] then
                        self.Selected[Part] = true
                        UpdatePartVisual(Part, true)
                    end
                end
            end

            local SelectedList = { }
            for Part, _ in self.Selected do
                TableInsert(SelectedList, Part)
            end

            Library.Flags[self.Flag] = SelectedList
            Library:SafeCall(self.Callback, SelectedList)
        end

        function BodyPartBox:Get()
            local SelectedList = { }
            for Part, _ in self.Selected do
                TableInsert(SelectedList, Part)
            end
            return SelectedList
        end

        function BodyPartBox:SetVisibility(Bool)
            Items["Holder"].Instance.Visible = Bool
        end

        function BodyPartBox:Clear()
            for Part, _ in self.Selected do
                self.Selected[Part] = nil
                UpdatePartVisual(Part, false)
            end
            self.Selected = { }
            Library.Flags[self.Flag] = { }
            Library:SafeCall(self.Callback, { })
        end

        for _, Part in BodyPartBox.Default do
            if PartFrames[Part] then
                BodyPartBox.Selected[Part] = true
                UpdatePartVisual(Part, true)
            end
        end

        local SelectedList = { }
        for Part, _ in BodyPartBox.Selected do
            TableInsert(SelectedList, Part)
        end
        Library.Flags[BodyPartBox.Flag] = SelectedList

        Library.SetFlags[BodyPartBox.Flag] = function(Value)
            BodyPartBox:Set(Value)
        end

        BodyPartBox.Items = Items
        BodyPartBox.PartFrames = PartFrames

        self:RegisterFeature({
            Name = BodyPartBox.Name,
            Type = "BodyParts",
            Instance = Items["Holder"].Instance,
            Object = BodyPartBox,
            Flag = BodyPartBox.Flag
        })

        return BodyPartBox
    end

end

getgenv().Library = Library
return Library
